# Annex A — Full decomposition into sets, primitives, derivatives, pipelines and throughlines

> A full independent reading of all 33 corpus documents, sorted into **fourteen overlapping sets**
> with stated membership criteria — a document belongs to a set if any section of it specifies a
> rule, value or state belonging to that set's subject, so most belong to several (membership matrix
> in §A2.1). Each set is flattened — duplicates collapsed, contradictions preserved and marked, gaps
> named — then decomposed into primitives with owner and mutation surface, derivatives with their
> composition rule, pipelines as state machines, and the throughlines bearing on it.
>
> **318 primitives · 293 derivatives · 129 pipelines · 18 throughlines · 79 indexed contradictions ·
> 37 specification holes where a human referee is required · 196 set-local gaps.**
>
> Formulas, thresholds and band edges are verbatim, including original glyphs. `[INFERRED]` marks a
> claim the corpus does not state, with its basis. **Contradictions are preserved here rather than
> reconciled** — Part III of the main document is where they are decided, and this annex is what
> those decisions were taken against.

---

# Stage A — Sets and Decomposition of the Valoria Governance Corpus

**Corpus:** 33 `.md` files, 810,408 bytes, 114,917 words (wc), read in full.
**Lens:** every document is read as a specification for an executable videogame with no GM. Where a document delegates a decision to a human referee, a table, or "authorial review", that is recorded as a **specification hole** (index `H-nn`, §H). Where two documents disagree, both claims survive as a **contradiction** (index `CT-nn`, §CT). Nothing is reconciled silently.
**Citation form:** `filename › section heading` (headings quoted as they appear; where a file has no headings above the cited table, the nearest bold label or table caption is used).
**Marking:** `[INFERRED]` = not stated by the corpus, basis given. `[ASPIRATION]` = design intent, not a mechanic. `[PROPOSAL]` = the source document declares itself a proposal, not canon. Numbers, thresholds and formulas are reproduced verbatim (including their original glyphs `≥`, `÷`, `×`, `⌊⌋`).
**IDs:** sets `SET-nn`; primitives `P-nn.mm`; derivatives `D-nn.mm`; pipelines `PL-nn.mm`; throughlines `TL-nn` (global register in §TL); contradictions `CT-nn`; gaps `GP-nn.mm` (set-local); holes `H-nn`.

A reader cannot resolve currency from the header alone; this is recorded once here and not repeated per set.

## A2. Sets

Membership criterion is stated so that a third party can re-apply it. A document belongs to a set if **any section of it specifies a rule, value or state belonging to the set's subject** — so most documents belong to several sets. "Centre of gravity" = the member whose removal would destroy the most of the set's specification.

| Set | Name | Members (● = centre of gravity) | Membership criterion |
|---|---|---|---|
| SET-01 | Resolution Engine & Action Economy | ● `stats_1_7_scale`, ● `core`, `strategic_layer`, `ci_political`, `parliament`, `phases`, `faction_behavior` (Ob modifier only) | States how any roll/check is resolved (dice, degree, resolver), stat ranges/caps, or how actions are gated (cards, cooldowns, Ob reference). |
| SET-02 | Season Structure & Accounting Cascade | ● `phases`, `clocks` (cap), `strategic_layer` (P-18, hybrid Cascade), `ci_political` (§5.3), `ministry` (Step 11), `treaty_expiration` (arc boundary), `insurgency_pipeline` (sustained-N), `player_agency` (§7.2), `conflict_architecture` (resolution order), `governance_play_redesign` (season loop) | States an ordered step of the season, Accounting or Year-End, or a rule about ordering/simultaneity/queuing of effects. |
| SET-03 | Peninsula Clocks & Tracks | ● `clocks`, ● `core` (Starting Values), `tracks`, `parliament` (PI), `stats_1_7_scale` (PI/Public Instability/CI passive), `ci_seizure`, `ci_political` (§2, §4.4), `campaign_architecture` (Parts 3, 5), `southernmost` (TT), `settlement_layer` (§7.1), `geography` (Proximity), `march_layer` (§7.3 IP), `generational_transition` (clock list), `phases` (Steps 4–6) | States the range, starting value, advancement, decay or threshold effect of a peninsula-wide or faction-private clock/track. |
| SET-04 | Church Influence, Piety & Seizure | ● `ci_seizure`, ● `ci_political`, `campaign_architecture` (Part 1), `tracks` (PT, RDT/TD, Excommunication cap, Attention Pool), `institutions` (Cardinals, Parish/Cathedral, Excommunication), `settlement_layer` (§1.5–1.7), `conflict_architecture` (Bishop Appointment), `geography` (SW), `stats_1_7_scale` (Unique Actions), `faction_politics` (Part 5), `strategic_layer` (P-23, Cascade Test 4), `clocks` (CI table), `parliament` (Excommunication Ob, PP-560) | States how CI is generated/consumed, how PT/SW/Church infrastructure behave, or how the Church acquires territory or influence. |
| SET-05 | Territory Geography & Strategic Movement | ● `geography`, ● `march_layer`, `settlement_adjacency`, `valoria_political_hierarchy`, `settlement_layer` (Part 2, §5), `fractional_province_ownership` (§2.5 movement), `territory_temperaments` (T-table), `southernmost` (zones), `clocks` (Proximity, Vanguard route), `core` (PV table), `ci_political` (§1 PV/SW) | States a map entity (territory, province, duchy, settlement, edge), a per-map-entity fixed attribute, or a rule for moving armies across the map. |
| SET-06 | Settlement Layer & Governance | ● `settlement_layer`, `governance_play_redesign` [PROPOSAL], `valoria_political_hierarchy`, `conflict_architecture` (settlement friction, Niflhel phenomena), `campaign_architecture` (Part 1), `faction_behavior` (LPS relocation), `stats_1_7_scale` (Mandate header), `player_agency` (§5.4 governance responsibility, §9 Resources sources), `faction_politics` (Hall tiers), `fractional_province_ownership` (§2.5) | States a per-settlement stat, a governor rule, a settlement-level phenomenon, or the settlement→faction aggregation. |
| SET-07 | Province Control, Accord, Fracturing & Transfer | ● `fractional_province_ownership`, ● `parliamentary_transfer`, `valoria_political_hierarchy` (§2.3–2.4), `tracks` (Accord), `core` (Accord, elimination vacuum), `phases` (4c), `settlement_adjacency` (§2.3–2.4), `settlement_layer` (§1.3, §5.1), `ci_political` (§4.4), `strategic_layer` (P-24, PP-647), `treaty_expiration` (territorial protection), `geography` (Starting Accord/Control) | States who controls a province/territory, how control changes, how Accord is computed/changed, or what Uncontrolled/Vacuum means. |
| SET-08 | Faction Stats, Behaviour & NPC Faction AI | ● `faction_behavior`, `territory_temperaments`, `stats_1_7_scale`, `core` (starting stats, Conviction texts), `ci_political` (§4, §6), `ministry` (priority tree), `institutions` (NPC rules), `parliament` (Institutional Mandate), `strategic_layer` (P-20, P-24, fog bands), `settlement_layer` (§1.8), `insurgency_pipeline` (§3.4 AI) | States a faction-level stat, its update rule, a rule for how an NPC faction chooses actions, or an Ob modifier derived from faction identity. |
| SET-09 | Succession, Splits, Emergence & Collapse | ● `faction_succession_split`, `baralta_crown_claim`, `insurgency_pipeline`, `settlement_layer` (§6.2–6.3, §7.2), `core` (Löwenritter Autonomy, Elimination), `conflict_architecture` (Graduated Autonomy), `institutions` (Deposition, Reconstitution), `worldbuilding` (§6.2 No Confidence), `campaign_architecture` (Parts 2, 6), `faction_politics` (Parts 6, 8, §1.3a), `stats_1_7_scale` (Löwenritter Coup Trigger), `parliament` (PP-569), `strategic_layer` (§9.9–9.10) | States how leadership changes hands, how a faction divides, forms, contracts or dies, or how an NPC entity becomes/ceases to be a faction. |
| SET-10 | Kingdom Institutions (Church, Löwenritter, Ministry, Guilds, Parliament) | ● `institutions`, `ministry`, `worldbuilding`, `parliament`, `faction_politics` (Part 7, §2.1–2.6), `core` (roster), `stats_1_7_scale` (Riskbreakers, PP-241), `early_game_ignition` (Cardinal unilateral action) | States the internal structure or standing mechanics of an institution other than the four player factions' stat sheets. |
| SET-11 | Player Character Agency, Standing, Renown & Rank | ● `player_agency`, ● `faction_politics`, `generational_transition`, `campaign_architecture` (Part 7), `settlement_layer` (§3.2, §6.1, §7.2), `governance_play_redesign` (Part 1, Part 3) [PROPOSAL], `strategic_layer` (P-14/P-15, §9.9–9.14), `baralta_crown_claim` (§5 rank transitions) | States a rule whose subject is the player character (or a named NPC's rank) rather than a faction or a map entity. |
| SET-12 | Conflict Ignition & Campaign Arc | ● `conflict_architecture`, `early_game_ignition` (superseded), `phases` (Game Setup), `campaign_architecture` (Parts 4–6), `worldbuilding` (event cards, §6.3), `strategic_layer` (Part Six scenarios), `clocks` (Vanguard) | States a rule that exists to start or shape the campaign's arc (setup draws, fuses, event cards, revelation, invasion phases) rather than to resolve a standing mechanic. |
| SET-13 | Southernmost, Thread & Warden Interface | ● `southernmost`, `geography` (Southernmost Access), `campaign_architecture` (Parts 3, 4, 6), `clocks` (MS effects), `core` (WC/WR, Community Weaving Ob), `settlement_layer` (§4.4, §4.9), `march_layer` (§3.4, §7), `strategic_layer` (Thread cascade tests, G-04/G-06, §9.5/9.15), `stats_1_7_scale` (Community Weaving supersession) | States a rule about the Southernmost (T15), Thread operations' strategic-layer effects, Forgetting, or Warden tracks. |
| SET-14 | Diplomacy, Treaties, Pledges & Casus Belli | ● `treaty_expiration`, ● `parliamentary_transfer` (§3), `parliament` (Pledges, Crown-break, Alignment, Missed Coalition), `march_layer` (§5.4), `core` (Formal Crown Treaty Ob), `ci_seizure` (CB on seizure), `fractional_province_ownership` (§5 CB scope), `faction_succession_split` (re-merge), `ci_political` (§3.2 treaty rows, §6.2), `stats_1_7_scale` (treaty positioning/ratification), `strategic_layer` (I-03) | States a rule about inter-faction agreements, their formation/expiry/breach, or the generation and consumption of Casus Belli. |

**Documents that resist classification, and why.**
- `early_game_ignition_analysis.md` — self-declared `[SUPERSEDED]` analysis; ~60% historical precedent prose. It is a member of SET-12 only because its 8-card deck and two provocation mechanics survive nowhere else and contradict the successor (CT-22).
- `worldbuilding_v30 (1).md` — a lore-reconciliation register whose territory numbering (`§9.1`) is a retired scheme (T12 = Valorsplatz); its mechanics are event cards and reversal paths. Placed in SET-10 and SET-12; its `§9.1` table is excluded from SET-05 as non-current (CT-32).
- `strategic_layer_v30.md` — a patch log (`v0.4 → v0.5 → v0.6 Transition Document`) rather than a spec: it states rules only as corrections to an absent base text ("Remove current degree table. Replace with:"). Its patches are treated as rule statements wherever they are the only statement; where a later document restates the rule, the later document is centre of gravity.
- `governance_play_redesign_v1.md` — `PROPOSAL` throughout; included in SET-06/SET-11 with every entry tagged `[PROPOSAL]` so that stages B/C can exclude it wholesale.
- `campaign_architecture_v30.md` — spans seven unrelated subjects (Church infra, RM identity, MS/Coherence, revelation, IP phases, Warden paths, Portrait); it is a member of six sets and centre of none.

## SET-01 — Resolution Engine & Action Economy

### SET-01 Flattened statement

**F-01.01 Dice.** All rolls use d10 pools. Face results: `1 → −1 success; 2–6 → 0; 7–9 → +1 success; 10 → +2 successes`. `Net successes = sum of contributions. May be negative (treated as 0 for degree purposes = Failure).` (`core (1).md › Dice System (v05 correction)`; `strategic_layer_v30.md › CORRECTION 1 — Dice System`). Standard target number is `TN 7` (`core › Dice System`). One document uses `TN 8` for one check (`southernmost.md › Forgetting Check`) — **CT-48**.

**F-01.02 Ob floor.** `Ob minimum: 1. No modifier may push Ob below 1.` (`core › Dice System`); `Modifiers that would push Ob below 1 are wasted — they do not carry over, grant additional dice, or create other effects.` (`strategic_layer › CORRECTION 2`). Restated on every Ob table (`core › Standard Action Ob Reference`: "All Obs: floor 1"). → TL-02.

**F-01.03 Degree table — CONTRADICTION CT-01.** Two live tables:
- (a) `core › Degree Table (PP-179 + PP-249)`: `≥ 2× Ob AND ≥ 3 → Overwhelming; ≥ Ob → Success; 0 < net < Ob → Partial; ≤ 0 → Failure`. `Overwhelming floor: net must be ≥ 3 regardless of Ob`. `Ob 10 exception: Overwhelming unavailable. Partial requires net ≥ 5.` Explicitly: `ED-031 (Ob+1 surplus) is SUPERSEDED by PP-179 (2×Ob). PP-179 is canonical.` (flagged `ED-142-R` for confirmation).
- (b) `strategic_layer › CORRECTION 3 — Degree Table Update`: `Ob + 1 or more → Overwhelming; = Ob → Success; Ob − 1 → Partial; 0 → Failure`. This is the Ob+1 rule that (a) declares superseded; the strategic_layer doc never records the supersession. Its own cascade tests use "surplus" language (`Ob 1, surplus 2 = Overwhelming`), i.e. rule (b).
A reader must decide which table binds any legacy dice roll that survives the resolver migration (F-01.05).

**F-01.04 Catastrophic Failure — CONTRADICTION CT-02.** `Majority-1s (Catastrophic Failure) override: STRUCK (v05 DESIGN DECISION 2026-04-02). All rolls resolve through standard degree table only.` (`core › Dice System`; repeated `strategic_layer › DESIGN DECISION (2026-04-02)`). But the same strategic_layer document retains `P-12: CF rule: If more dice show 1 than show 7–10 in the same roll, result is Critical Failure: apply normal Failure consequences plus one additional immediate consequence (Game Master discretion: −1 Standing, −1 Stability, or one stat degradation).` (`strategic_layer › I-01`). P-12 is both a contradiction and a spec hole (**H-01**).

**F-01.05 Deterministic+stochastic resolver (ED-874, ratified 2026-05-31).** `This is the canonical resolution method for faction Domain Actions and bare-stat faction checks. It supersedes the bare-stat-pool-vs-Ob dice approach`. Verbatim (`stats_1_7_scale (1).md › Domain Action Resolution (deterministic+stochastic) — CANONICAL`):
```
margin  M = acting_stat − difficulty
   difficulty = the contested target's relevant stat (contested actions), OR a fixed action-difficulty rating (non-contested actions).
   Legacy Ob mapping: an action previously "vs Ob O" has difficulty D = max(1, (O−1)·2).
P_success(M)        = clamp(0.50 + 0.10·M, 0.05, 0.90)
P_overwhelming(M)   = clamp(0.50 + 0.10·M − 0.35, 0, 0.55)
P_atleast_partial   = clamp(0.50 + 0.10·M + 0.20, P_success, 0.97)
draw r ~ U[0,1)  (lower = better):
   r < P_overwhelming → Overwhelming; P_overwhelming ≤ r < P_success → Success; P_success ≤ r < P_atleast_partial → Partial; r ≥ P_atleast_partial → Failure
```
Parameters: `BASE 0.50, SLOPE 0.10, FLOOR 0.05, CAP 0.90`; `Live leverage zone M ∈ [−4, +4]`. Scope: `bare-stat faction checks only. Healthy dice systems — personal combat, social contest (pools 5–18D), aggregated mass battle — remain dice`. Governed checks named: all Domain Actions (`Assert: M = Influence − 2`; `Reconstitute: M = Influence − 6`), `Suppress — M = Mandate − (Church-L difficulty); Failure → Stability −1 retained`, Parliamentary Rebuttal, Treaty positioning `M = own Influence − target Influence`, ratification `M = Mandate − 2`, `§1.4 Accounting Stability Check — M = Stability − loss_magnitude`, and the four migrated Unique Actions (Royal Decree `M = Mandate − 2`; Excommunication `M = Mandate − target Mandate` / `Mandate − 2`; Private Collection `M = Intel − 2`; Economic Leverage `M = Wealth − target Wealth`). `A degree band an action's table omits takes the next-lower listed result`. Hafenmark Sovereign Authority Doctrine `pending decision` (**GP-01.01**). → TL-08.

**F-01.06 Resolver reach — CONTRADICTION CT-03.** Every other document in the corpus that states a faction check states it as `pool vs Ob` with dice (e.g. `parliament.md` L-suppression `Ob = floor(target L / 2) + 1`; `ministry.md` `roll Ministry L (3D) vs Ob 1`; `fractional_province_ownership › §3` `Roll 5d10 vs Ob 3`; `baralta_crown_claim › §2` `rolls their pool vs Ob 3 (TN 7)`; `parliamentary_transfer › §4` `Pool-vs-Ob roll resolved per params/core.md Continuous Engine + degree table`; `ci_seizure` `Battle Ob = floor(defender Military / 2) + 1`). The legacy mapping `D = max(1,(O−1)·2)` converts an Ob; it does not say how a composite pool (`Mandate + Influence`, `Influence + floor(CI/15)`, `Military + Fort dice`) maps to `acting_stat`. Two rule systems coexist with an incomplete bridge (**GP-01.02**).

**F-01.07 Probability reference (dice).** `Pool 2: E 0.80, P(≥1) ~58%, P(≥2) ~22%, P(≥3) ~5%; 3: 1.20/74/40/15; 4: 1.60/85/56/27; 5: 2.00/91/69/42; 6: 2.40/95/80/57; 7: 2.80/97/88/69` with `P(success-or-bonus)=0.40, P(subtraction)=0.10, P(neutral)=0.50` (`strategic_layer › Probability summary (d10)`).

**F-01.08 Stat ranges & caps.** Faction stats `Mandate / Influence / Wealth / Military / Intel / Stability` on 1–7 (`stats_1_7_scale › Stats (1–7 scale)`). Floors/ceilings: `Legitimacy 0–7; Popular_Support 0–7; Influence 1–7; Wealth 0–7; Military 0–7; Stability 0–7` (`core › Stat Ceilings and Floors`); `Mandate cannot exceed 7 … floor is 0` (`strategic_layer › P-20`). Seasonal cap: `±2 per stat per season (TTRPG); ±varies (BG — see accounting)` (`stats_1_7_scale › Stats`); `±2 cap applied at accounting. Multiple actions within a season tally; net clipped at accounting.` (`stats_1_7_scale › PP-242`). Military additionally `±2/season from all Domain Actions combined. Hard cap = faction Military rating (cannot exceed starting value +1 via Domain Actions alone) [PROVISIONAL]` (`stats_1_7_scale › Military Seasonal Cap (ED-039)`); unit destruction `Military −1 (immediate). Cap: −2 per season [PROVISIONAL]` (`› Military Stat Change on Unit Destruction`) but `BG mode: Military −1 from unit destruction queues to the season's Accounting phase` (`strategic_layer › Military loss timing by mode (PP-041)`). Player caps: `Standing: Cannot exceed 5. Cannot fall below 0.` (`strategic_layer › P-15`) — contradicted by the 0–7 ladder, **CT-19**; `Reputation: Cannot exceed 5` (`› P-14`). → TL-07.

**F-01.09 Failed DA cost.** `A Domain Action that results in Failure (net successes ≤ 0) costs the acting faction −1 Stability. Partial: no cost.` Exclusions: self-improvement DAs, TTRPG scene rolls, Thread ops, Restoration Movement. `Stability 1 edge case: … reduces it to 0, triggering an immediate Stability Check` (`stats_1_7_scale › Failed Domain Action Stability Cost (PP-403)`).

**F-01.10 Standard Action Ob reference** (`core › Standard Action Ob Reference (P-21, v04 B3)`), verbatim: Muster `2` (−1 T12 garrison); March `No roll` (contested entry = Battle); Govern `floor(Prosperity / 2) + 1` (−1 own capital); Trade `floor(Prosperity / 2) + 1` (+1 IP≥30; +1 T2); Diplomacy vs NPC `floor(NPC Stability / 2) + 1`; Diplomacy between players `Negotiated. Not a roll`; Formal Crown Treaty `floor(target L / 2) + 1` (Crown only); Thread Operation `Ob 2 base`; Investigate/Intel `2` (+2 in Church territory with Inquisitor); Spy `floor(target Intel / 2) + 1`; Survey `(5 − Proximity Rating) + 1, min 1`; Parliamentary Manoeuvre `floor(opponent Influence / 2) + 1`; Community Organising `2` (`Pool: 1D base + 1D per adjacent territory with RM Presence marker`); Community Weaving `(100−MS)÷20 round up min 1` (−1 per Presence marker); Dynastic Proclamation `floor(target Stability / 2) + 1`; Martial Governance `floor(Prosperity / 2) + 2`; Fortify `Fort level + 1`. **CT-72**: `strategic_layer › P-21` gives different territory modifiers (`Muster … −1 in T2 (Garrison), −1 in T11 (Breadbasket)`; `March 2, +1 in T8`; `Govern 2, −1 in own capital`) and `geography.md › Territory Table` gives `T8 Gransol … Garrison: Muster +1D`, `T11 … Muster Ob −1`. **CT-73**: Govern Ob `floor(Prosperity/2)+1` (core) vs flat `2` (strategic P-21). **CT-52**: Suppress Ob `floor(target L / 2) + 1` (`parliament › L Suppression`, `ci_seizure › §3.7`) vs `floor(Church L / 2) + 1 ÷ 2 (round up, min 1)` (`stats_1_7_scale › CI Passive Advance (PP-402)`) — the latter is not a well-formed expression.

**F-01.11 Card hands & cooldown.** Hands (`core › Batch Card Hand`; `ci_political › §5.1`): Crown `2× Legionary, 1× Consul, 1× Senator, 1× Prefect, 1× Recess`; Church `2× Senator, 1× Pontifex, 1× Consul, 1× Legionary, 1× Recess`; Hafenmark `2× Consul, 1× Senator, 1× Legionary, 1× Diplomat, 1× Recess`; Varfell `2× Tribune, 1× Legionary, 1× Consul, 1× Colonist, 1× Recess`. Restoration — **CT-24**: `core` lists `1× Pontifex, 2× Praetor, 1× Senator, 1× Tribune, 1× Recess` and, in the same paragraph, `RM hand: 2× Praetor …, 1× Pontifex …, 1× Recess. No Legionary, no Senator …, no Tribune. (PP-460)`. Domain Expertise `+1D`: Crown = Legionary; Church = Senator; Hafenmark = Consul/Prefect; Varfell = Tribune; Restoration = Pontifex only. Card → action mapping (`ci_political › §5.2`): Legionary = Muster, March; Consul = Govern, Trade, Fortify, Survey; Senator = Diplomacy, Crown Treaty, Suppress, Parliamentary Motion, Assert, Parliamentary Manoeuvre; Pontifex = Thread ops, Assert, Sacred Assembly; Tribune = Intel, Spy, Investigate; Prefect = Crown Decree, tax enforcement; Diplomat = Dynastic Proclamation, trade negotiation; Colonist = Varfell Transformation/Settlement; Praetor = Community Projects; Recess = Pass. Cooldown Duration (seasons): `Legionary 1; Consul 1; Senator 1; Pontifex 2; Tribune 1; Prefect 2; Diplomat 1; Colonist 2; Recess 0` (`ci_political › §5.3`). Return rule: `At each Accounting (Phase 5 Step 3): all items on the Cooldown Track −1 slot. At 0 slots: card returns to hand`.

**F-01.12 Policy Instrument.** `When Crown's Mandate ≥ 4, Crown may take one additional Standard Action per season designated as the Policy Instrument … not limited by card-hand constraints … cannot be interrupted by Parliamentary Manoeuvre (see P-19). Activation condition: Mandate ≥ 4 at the start of the season.` (`strategic_layer › Policy Instrument definition (PP-036)`) vs `Once per season, Crown may issue one Policy if L ≥ 4. Same Policy cannot repeat 2 seasons. Resolution order: Policy → Hafenmark Opposition → Censor.` (`parliament.md › Crown Policy Instrument (PP-180)`). P-19: the Hafenmark free interrupt `is triggered only by Crown's Policy Instrument … NOT … Crown Senator card plays`. Also `Crown Policy Instruments require Ministry countersignature` (`ministry.md › Ministry and Crown Policy`). `[Full Policy table in faction card section]` — the Policy table is not in the corpus (**GP-01.03**).

**F-01.13 Coalition suppression.** `When 2+ factions play Domain Actions targeting the same faction's L in the same Phase 4 resolution, each additional faction beyond the first adds +2D` — `1: +0D; 2: +2D; 3: +4D; 4+: +6D (cap)`. `Pool floor: 1D.` Valid suppression action = `Any Domain Action whose stated primary effect includes 'L −N' on the target faction` (`parliament.md › L Suppression — General Cap and Coalition Bonus (PP-296)`, `(PP-518)`). In dice units; no resolver translation (**GP-01.02**).

**F-01.14 Ob modifier from faction identity.** Legacy per-faction Ethical Framework modifiers are struck (`core › ~~Ethical Framework Modifiers~~ — SUPERSEDED`; `stats_1_7_scale › ~~Ethical Framework Ob Modifiers~~ — SUPERSEDED`). Replacement: `Ob_modifier = clamp(mission_alignment_modifier + cascade_alignment_modifier + expectation_alignment_modifier, −2, +2)` (`faction_behavior_v30 › §3.7`; decomposed in SET-08). **CT-40**: `faction_politics_v30 › §1.1–§1.4` still state the struck modifiers as live (`Visible, public, virtuous actions are −1 Ob; covert or morally ambiguous are +1 Ob`, etc.), and `strategic_layer › Cascade Test 1/3` apply `doctrine-aligned −1 Ob` and `Scholastic … −1 Ob`.

**F-01.15 Zoom-In / hybrid resolution.** Personal-scale outcome modifies mass action: `PC Overwhelming: mass action at −1 Ob; Success: none; Partial: +1 Ob; Failure: +2 Ob` (`stats_1_7_scale › Scene→Mass Transition Modifiers — Hybrid (PP-244)`). Resource spending threshold `2× rolled net successes. Below threshold = stressed — faction takes Wealth −1 at Cascade.` (`strategic_layer › §9.7`).

**Gaps.** GP-01.01 SAD resolution pending. GP-01.02 no mapping from composite dice pools / bonus dice (+1D, +2D coalition, Fort dice, CI bonus dice) into resolver margin terms — the resolver text only converts `Ob`. GP-01.03 Crown Policy table absent. GP-01.04 "Standard Action" count per season is never stated as a number (cards imply it; Policy Instrument is "one additional"). GP-01.05 Degree table for opposed rolls when both sides roll (battle) is only stated in `strategic_layer › P-16` as "use the DIFFERENCE as the effective surplus … vs Ob" — which of CT-01's tables applies to the difference is unstated.

| D-01.08 | Ob after modifiers | base Ob, modifiers | `Ob = max(1, base + Σ modifiers)` | arithmetic [INFERRED composition; floor stated] | `core › Dice System` |
| D-01.09 | Card availability | Cooldown Track | `At 0 slots: card returns to hand` | arithmetic | `ci_political › §5.3` |
| D-01.10 | Seasonal stat clip | tally | `net clipped at accounting` to ±2 | arithmetic | `stats › PP-242` |
| D-01.11 | Failed-DA Stability cost | Degree | `Failure → −1 Stability` with exclusions | rule | `stats › PP-403` |
| D-01.12 | Scene→Mass Ob modifier | PC degree | `OW −1 / S 0 / P +1 / F +2` | arithmetic | `stats › PP-244` |
| D-01.13 | Stressed expenditure | Resources spent, net successes | `Below 2× rolled net = stressed → Wealth −1` | arithmetic | `strategic_layer › §9.7` |

### SET-01 Pipelines

**PL-01.01 Dice roll resolution.** Entry: an action with pool P and Ob O declared. Steps: (1) apply Ob modifiers, floor 1; (2) roll P d10; (3) sum faces → net; (4) map net → Degree via D-01.02 (or D-01.03 — CT-01); (5) apply action's degree table; (6) if Failure and a faction DA, Stability −1 (D-01.11). Branches: Ob = 10 special case. Terminal: Degree + effects. Advanced by: the action's declaration. Reversible: no (`strategic_layer › §9.6` "What has been rolled stands").

**PL-01.02 Resolver resolution.** Entry: a governed faction check with `acting_stat` and `difficulty`. Steps: (1) M = acting − difficulty; (2) compute P_ow, P_s, P_p; (3) draw r; (4) band → Degree; (5) apply named degree hooks (e.g. Suppress Failure → Stability −1; Rebuttal OW → +1 effect); (6) omitted band → next-lower result. Terminal: Degree. Advanced by: action declaration. Reversible: no.

**PL-01.03 Card lifecycle.** Entry: card in hand. Steps: play in Phase 1 → resolve in Phase 4 → place on Cooldown Track with `Cooldown Duration` slots → each Accounting Step 3 `−1 slot` → at 0 return to hand. Recess: `0 (returns immediately)`. Terminal: back in hand. Advanced by: Accounting.

**PL-01.04 Policy → Opposition → Censor.** Entry: Crown declares Policy (Mandate/L ≥ 4). Steps: `Policy → Hafenmark Opposition → Censor` (`parliament › Crown Policy Instrument`); Hafenmark may `immediately respond with Senator Inward (interrupt, no card play cost)` only against the Policy Instrument (`strategic_layer › P-19`); Ministry countersignature check (`ministry › Ministry and Crown Policy`: L < 2 → +1 Ob; L = 0 → unavailable). Terminal: Policy in effect / blocked. **GP-01.03**: what "Censor" is, and the Policy list, are absent.

### SET-01 Throughlines applied
TL-02 (Ob floor 1) — asserted by `core`, `strategic_layer`, every Ob table; no violation found. TL-07 (seasonal caps) — asserted; see SET-03/SET-04 for cap breaches. TL-08 (resolver governs bare-stat checks) — asserted by `stats_1_7_scale`, `ci_political §3.2/§4.2/§4.3`, `faction_succession_split §2.2`, `insurgency §6.2`; violated by every remaining `pool vs Ob` faction rule (CT-03). TL-10 (triadic Ob modifier replaces ethical frameworks) — violated by `faction_politics` and `strategic_layer` cascade tests (CT-40). TL-12 (no GM) — violated by P-12 (H-01).

## SET-02 — Season Structure & Accounting Cascade

### SET-02 Flattened statement

**F-02.01 Game Setup** (`phases.md › Game Setup (Session C — conflict_architecture_proposal CANON)`), before Season 1, in order: (1) `Place starting settlements. 5 provinces begin with non-aligned settlements (T1, T8, T14, T4, T13).` (2) `Draw Tensions Deck card. Draw 1 card from 6.` (3) `Determine assassination target (if Royal Crisis drawn). Sub-roll for target (Lenneth/Torben/Almud).` (4) `Set Löwenritter Autonomy to Loyal.` The referenced `params/bg/tensions_deck.md` and `params/bg/royal_assassination.md` are not in the corpus (**GP-02.01**).

**F-02.02 Three-scale resolution order.** `Each season resolves in three layers, bottom-up: 1. Settlement layer (resolves first) … 2. Province layer (resolves second) … 3. Peninsula layer (resolves third)` (`phases › Three-Scale Resolution Model (PP-TBD)`; `conflict_architecture_proposal › The Three Scales`: "Resolution order each season: Settlement → Province → Peninsula"). Settlement layer contents: infrastructure actions, `Settlement Order and Prosperity update. Black markets emerge (Order ≤ 1, no governor) or resolve (Order ≥ 3). Governor authority checks fire.` Province: `Fragmentation checks … Accord recalculates from settlement Order floor-average. Province PV recalculates … Military movement along settlement adjacency graph. Mass battles resolve at settlement nodes. Territorial transfer.` Peninsula: `RS/CI/IP/PI/Strain clocks update … Altonian pressure evaluated. Victory condition check … Warden emergence. Season advances.` → TL-03. **GP-02.02**: how this three-layer order composes with the Phase 1–5 structure (F-02.03) is unstated — both are "the season", neither references the other.

**F-02.03 Phase structure.** Board game: Phases 1–5 with Phase 4 = resolution, Phase 5 = Accounting (`phases › Phase 4 Resolution Priority Order`; `› Phase 5 Seasonal Accounting`). Player-agency revision: `Phase 1a — Duty Assignment; 1b — Scene Slate Generation; 1c — Personal Phase; Phase 2 — Strategic Phase; Phase 3 — Accounting` (`player_agency › §7.2`). Hybrid: `Personal → Strategic → Cascade` (`strategic_layer › §9.1`). Governance proposal: `world→player stroke resolved at season open … player→world stroke` (`governance_play_redesign › §4.2–4.3` [PROPOSAL]). Four phase schemes with no mapping between them (**GP-02.03**). Phase 1 also hosts declarations: Open Pledge (`parliament › PP-515`), Crown-break dissolution (`parliament › PP-525`), Altonian diplomacy (`institutions › Temperance`).

**F-02.04 Phase 4 priority order** (`phases › Phase 4 Resolution Priority Order (v04 B4 — CORRECTED)`): `1 Intel/Covert (Tribune); 2 Military (Legionary) — Battle (simultaneous per territory); 3 Domain (Consul, Prefect, Architectus, Colonist); 4 Social (Senator); 5 Thread operations (Pontifex, Weaver); 6 Special/Unique Powers; 7 Project advancement (Praetor)`. `Within tier: descending Stability order. Ties: simultaneous.` Tiebreak refinement: `Tied Stability (exactly equal): resolve simultaneously only if there are exactly two factions tied. Three or more factions tied on Stability: resolve in alphabetical faction order … Ties between two factions in adversarial military actions always resolve simultaneously` (`strategic_layer › I-07 / P-18`). Ministry NPC acts at `Phase 4, Priority 4 — Domain Actions tier` (`ministry.md › Ministry NPC AI Priority Tree`) — but Domain is tier 3 in phases.md (**GP-02.04**, numbering mismatch). Ministry conflict rule: if Ministry would act in a territory already acted on this Phase 4, `redirects to next-priority viable territory … If all … already acted-on: Priority 5 default in any uncontested AP-token territory` (`parliament.md › Ministry Domain Action Conflict (PP-480)`).

**F-02.05 Phase 5 Accounting — 13 steps** (`phases › Phase 5 Seasonal Accounting (v04 B4 — CORRECTED)`), verbatim order:
1. Apply pending attribute changes. 2. `Faction Stability checks: any faction with ≥2 attribute loss this season: Stability pool vs Ob = loss magnitude.` 3. Cooldown Track −1. 4. `Clock advances: RS baseline drift (−1 at Year-End/Winter only). CI per formula. IP per Altonian pressure table. PI changes.` 4b. Church Prominence update. 4c. Accord checks (i–iii). 4d. Turmoil update (i–iii). 4e. `Battle consequence accounting: IP +2 if inter-faction battle occurred this season.` 5. Church Attention Pool threshold responses; pool resets to 0. 6. Thread Debt (`tokens >1 season old: RS −1/token … permanent residual RS −0.5`). 7. Clear Thread Resonance markers. 8. Threshold events (one Event Card per threshold crossed). 8b. Milestone Bonus check. 9. Warden Emergence check. 9b. Vaynard-Edeyja same-season rule (references `VTM ≥ 4`, a struck track — **GP-02.05**). 10. Warden Cooperation check. 10b. Torben/Elske Loyalty events. 11. `[DISSOLVED — Hollow Victory totals no longer tracked.]` 12. `Victory condition check. Any faction meeting all its victory conditions for 2 consecutive Accounting steps declares victory … Co-victory pairings checked simultaneously (§4). Shared loss conditions checked first (§5).` 13. Season marker advances; if Winter (every 4th season) → Year-End.
**CT-12** (step 4e retains battle → IP +2, struck by ED-743 per `ci_political › §4.4`). **CT-28** (step 12 faction-specific/co-victory/shared-loss vs GD-1 sole victory). **CT-70** (`ministry.md` fires Ministry Stabilisation "at Accounting Step 11, before Hollow Victory totals" — step 11 is dissolved). Stability check at step 2 is also stated in resolver form `M = Stability − loss_magnitude` (`stats › Domain Action Resolution`) and paired with `§1.3 Institutional Consolidation recovery (CC-4) … deterministic clean-season +1 recovery` — the §1.3 rule itself is not in the corpus (**GP-02.06**).

**F-02.06 Year-End Accounting** (`phases › Year-End Accounting (every 4th season = Winter, v04 B11)`), 10 steps verbatim: `1. Apply Year-End CI and RS fractions. 2. RS baseline drift −1. 3. Löwenritter (if active): raise one free unit; Prosperity −1. 4. Torben Loyalty Year-End modifiers: Crown PI ≥ 5 →+1; Crown upheld L 2+ consecutive seasons →+1; Löwenritter PI ≥ 3 without Emergency Powers →+1. 5. Elske Loyalty Year-End modifiers. 6. Hollow Victory totals announced publicly. 7. Once-Per-Year effects trigger. 8. Age track (optional Long Campaign only). 9. Check CI Year-End fractions for threshold crossings. 10. Season resets to Spring. Campaign year advances.` **CT-71**: step 6 announces totals that step 11 of Phase 5 says are "no longer tracked". Other Year-End hooks stated elsewhere: Prosperity ≥ 3 → +1 Wealth (`strategic_layer › P-22`); Ministry Legislative Record (`ministry › Ministry Legislative Record`); Parliament Ministry Nomination (`institutions › Parliament Nomination Mechanic`); Cardinal replacement (`institutions › Cardinal Death — Succession`); Church Levies raise (`institutions › Church Levies`); tithe rounding (`institutions › Prudence`); Church Ascendant PT drift at CI 80 (`ci_political › §2.1`); Mass Seizure bid failure check "in a single Year-End" (`ci_political › §2.2`); Elske step 5 content absent (**GP-02.07**).

**F-02.07 Arc boundary.** `At each arc boundary (4 seasons per arc)` … `Trigger: Arc boundary (end of season 4, 8, 12, …)`; `At arc boundary, before any other arc-boundary processing, iterate active Crown Treaties.` (`treaty_expiration › §1.1`). Arc-boundary processes stated elsewhere: RM PT decay (`insurgency › §2`), Adjacent-instability CB grant (`parliamentary_transfer › §3`), Parliamentary Transfer frequency `1 per arc per faction`, Crown constitutional-restoration CB refresh. `arc` ≡ 4 seasons ≡ year (`faction_politics` uses "year-arc"). Relationship of "arc boundary" to Year-End step order is unstated (**GP-02.08**). → TL-17.

**F-02.08 Sustained-N discipline.** `at Accounting of season N, all 3 conditions are true; at Accounting of season N+1, all 3 remain true … Non-consecutive seasons meeting the conditions do NOT aggregate.` (`insurgency › §5.1`); same discipline for victory (`phases` step 12), Mass Seizure bid, Fragmentation stabilization window (4 seasons), Hafenmark unconsecrated rule (3 consecutive seasons, `baralta › §3`).

**F-02.09 Cascade Depth Cap.** `Maximum 3 immediate mechanical effects per card play resolution step. Additional effects queue to next Accounting. State-based modifiers (MS/CI/IP/PI environmental effects) do NOT count against this cap.` (`clocks.md › Cascade Depth Cap (v04 B4)`). Whether clock changes count is left as a to-do: `Confirm that clock changes count against the 3-effect cap. Add this to PATCH P-29.` (`strategic_layer › Cascade Test 2`) — P-29 as printed is about MS gain, not the cap (**GP-02.09**). The treaty-betrayal cascade is deliberately staggered across windows `keeping each step within the Cascade Depth Cap of 3` (`parliament › PP-523`). → TL-06.

**F-02.10 Hybrid Cascade** (`strategic_layer › §9.8`): fixed order `1. Domain Echoes … 2. Thread operation clock changes (MS, TT) 3. Clock threshold events 4. Board order consequences 5. Accounting`. `Game Master-run; not skippable.` Batching rules `§9.3` (all consequences batch to Cascade "via the Game Master ledger"; inline exception "a Game Master call"). Zoom In restricted to Personal phase; `No player may initiate a Personal phase flashback scene to alter a Strategic phase outcome already resolved.` (`§9.6`). CI threshold check `fires at turn-end regardless of Zoom In suspension` (`ci_seizure › CI Threshold Check — Zoom In Interaction`). Holes **H-02..H-06** (GM ledger, GM inline judgement, GM sequencing "by dramatic logic", GM decides "against temporal flow", GM approval of replacement PC).

**F-02.11 Governance-proposal season loop** [PROPOSAL] (`governance_play_redesign › §1.1, §4.2–4.3`): `Π recomputes → the deck draws → NPCs advance ambitions → the Provincial Authority issues the Directive → rivals/subnationals maneuver`, then governor spends AP, then Personal Phase.

**Gaps.** GP-02.01 setup sub-tables absent. GP-02.02 three-scale order vs Phase 1–5 not composed. GP-02.03 four phase schemes unmapped. GP-02.04 Ministry tier numbering. GP-02.05 step 9b references struck VTM. GP-02.06 §1.3 Institutional Consolidation absent. GP-02.07 Elske Year-End modifiers unspecified. GP-02.08 arc-boundary vs Year-End ordering. GP-02.09 cap counting of clock changes unresolved. GP-02.10 "Event Card per threshold crossed" (step 8) — deck absent. GP-02.11 "Milestone Bonus check" (8b) undefined here. GP-02.12 Cascade cap's "queue to next Accounting" — queue ordering among queued effects unstated.

### SET-02 Pipelines

**PL-02.01 Season (board game).** Entry: start of season N. Steps: Phase 1 declarations (card plays, Pledges, dissolutions) → Phases 2–3 (unspecified in corpus; **GP-02.14**) → Phase 4 resolution by D-02.03 → Phase 5 Accounting (13 steps, F-02.05) → if Winter, Year-End (F-02.06) → season N+1. Terminal: victory declared (step 12) or MS = 0 Rupture (SET-03) or continue. Advanced by: sequencer. Reversible: no.

**PL-02.02 Accounting cascade (13 steps).** Entry: end of Phase 4. Steps: exactly F-02.05 1→13, sub-steps in lettered order. Branch predicates: step 2 (tally ≥ 2); 4c(i) (Accord 1 and no garrison); 4c(ii) (Accord 0); 4c(iii) (garrison AND no hostile action 2 consecutive seasons); 4d(i) (ALL territories Accord ≥ 2); 4d(ii) (Treaty formed / Pledge honoured); 4e (inter-faction battle occurred); 5 (Attention Pool thresholds); 6 (token age > 1); 8 (threshold crossed); 9/10 (Warden checks — conditions in SET-13); 9b (`Warden Emergence AND VTM ≥ 4 AND Varfell played Tribune Inward in T6`); 12 (all conditions 2 consecutive Accountings); 13 (Winter). Terminal: season advances. Reversible: no.

**PL-02.03 Year-End.** Entry: Accounting step 13 with Winter. Steps F-02.06 1→10 plus the externally-stated Year-End hooks listed there (ordering among them unstated — **GP-02.15**). Terminal: Spring of year+1.

**PL-02.04 Three-scale resolution.** Entry: season start. Steps: Settlement layer → Province layer → Peninsula layer (F-02.02), each with the listed sub-processes; "Control flows upward … Pressure flows downward". Terminal: season advances. Composition with PL-02.01 unstated (GP-02.02).

**PL-02.05 Cascade-cap queue.** Entry: a card-play resolution step producing > 3 immediate effects. Steps: apply first 3 (ordering among effects unstated — GP-02.12); enqueue the rest; at next Accounting apply queued. State-based modifiers bypass. Terminal: queue empty.

**PL-02.06 Hybrid Cascade (5 steps).** Entry: end of Strategic phase. Steps: F-02.10 order 1→5. Every step GM-run (H-02). Terminal: Accounting complete.

Assert optional only once CI caps. (PP-511)` (`ci_seizure`). Milestones (`ci_political › §2.1`): `28 Starting; 40 Church Assertive — +1D on all Assert and Seizure rolls; 55 Institutional Reach — All factions: Ob +1 to actions directly opposing Church Domain Actions; 65 Church Dominant — Secular factions must spend an extra action slot … to pass Parliamentary motions against Church; 80 Church Ascendant — Seizure Ob reduced by 1 globally. All territory PT drifts +1 toward piety pole at Year-End (unless Warden Cooperation ≥ 2); 100 Theocracy Unification Attempt`. Seasonal cap `±5 CI per season from all sources combined (PP-504). ±3 from player Domain Actions specifically.` Generation is SET-04. Analytical claim `CI grows at +5/season` (`early_game_ignition › §1`) is not a rule.

**F-03.04 Invasion/Institutional Pressure (IP).** Effects (`clocks › Invasion Pressure (IP) Effects`): `Below 30 | Trade with Schoenland: +1D; 30–59 | Trade with Schoenland: +1 Ob. All factions: +1D to Intel orders; 60–74 | Trade disrupted: +2 Ob. Proxy at T4: +1D military`. Vanguard `[PROVISIONAL ED-340] … (PP-568 — flag for authorial review)`: `appears as NPC military presence at T10 … Military 5 equivalent; Size 3; cannot be negotiated, traded, or Diplomatically managed. Advance: if no faction contests Vanguard territory for 2 consecutive seasons, Vanguard advances one territory along the invasion route (T10→T3→T2→T1). Contest: any faction in Vanguard's territory triggers automatic Battle (defender Military vs Ob 3). Win: Vanguard held. Loss: Vanguard advances. While Vanguard occupies T1 (Valorsplatz): all factions −1 Stability/season.` **CT-32**: T10 is not adjacent to T3 (`geography › Adjacency`). **H-08** (authorial review required). Three-phase invasion (`campaign_architecture › §5.1`): `Phase 1 (IP reaches 100): First mountain pass. One border territory under Active Invasion. Domain Actions +1 Ob in contested territory. IP decay begins. Phase 2 (IP sustained 85+ for 3 seasons after Phase 1): Second corridor through Schoenland. Two additional territories. Altonian Governorate formally established (Mandate 2, Military 4, Stability 3). Underground Network activates. Phase 3 (IP sustained 80+ for 3 more seasons): Third corridor northwest. Governorate contiguous territory chain. Mandate 3, Military 5, Stability 4. All faction Domain Actions +2 Ob in occupied territories. Phase retreats: IP < 85 → Phase 1 retreats. IP < 75 → Phase 2 retreats. IP < 60 → full retreat.` Repulsion (`§5.2`): `Military: Two consecutive Overwhelming Mass Battle results vs Altonian forces → full withdrawal. IP resets to 60. Cannot rise above 80 for 10 seasons. Diplomatic: Elske Loyalty ≥ 6 + Social Contest vs Vanguard Commander (Ob 4) + IP < 80 → IP drops to 40. Overwhelming: IP to 20 + Non-Aggression Pact 20 seasons. Resistance (during Occupation): Underground Network Mandate 3 + Governorate Accord 0 in all occupied territories + Mass Battle Success vs Governorate → full withdrawal. IP resets to 30. After repulsion by any path: IP freezes, cannot rise.` NW pass `Event-only. Not a playable march. Fires at IP ≥ 90.` (`geography › Altonian Mountain Passes`). Advancement — **CT-26** (five statements): `Base: each season 0 (Institutional Pressure does not advance automatically)`; `Altonian Trade Mission refused (IP 30 event) +1`; `Torben complied (sent to Altonia) −3/season while` [truncated in source]; `From scattered references: Church Influence > 60 → Institutional Pressure +1/season. No base … advancement is stated.` (`strategic_layer › G-11`); `IP +1/season baseline → +1/2 seasons baseline (halved)` (`settlement_layer › §7.1`); `IP now advances from Accord-based territory-count thresholds at Accounting (peninsular_strain_v30 §3.2), not directly from battle-occurrence` (`ci_political › §4.4`, the referenced §3.2 absent); `IP +2 if inter-faction battle occurred this season` (`phases` 4e; `clocks`; `strategic_layer PP-647`); `Holding a gate confers +1 IP per season` and `Unauthorised crossings produce … (b) IP −2 to the trespassing faction` (`march_layer › §7.3, §5.4`) — the last treats IP as a per-faction quantity. **CT-66**: `Torben Loyalty … No IP trigger` (`core`) vs `existing IP +1 from Torben Loyalty ≤ 3` (`ci_political › §7.5`, `strategic_layer › G-11`). Simultaneous catastrophe: `If MS=0 AND IP≥80 both trigger in the same Accounting phase: 1. MS=0 resolves first … 2. IP≥80 … second.` (`stats_1_7_scale › Simultaneous Catastrophe Rule (PP-199 — ED-077)`). AER (Altonian Ecclesiastical Relationship) `removed` 2026-05-04; strategic_layer Scenario C's AER-raised Vanguard threshold (`75 → 80`) is obsolete (`strategic_layer` header note).

**F-03.05 Parliament Integrity (PI).** `0–20 (cumulative pressure meter). Starts at 7.` Table: `8–10 | Full Parliament. Crown Policy requires L ≥ 4. 5–7 | Standard. Parliamentary Manoeuvre available. 3–4 | Degraded. Parliamentary Manoeuvre +1 Ob. Crown Decree Ob reduced to 1. ≤ 2 | Non-functional. Hafenmark loses Parliamentary Manoeuvre. Crown governs by decree. CI +2 (does not apply when CI is frozen at 75 — PP-560).` `PI degrades: Crown Emergency Powers (−1), Church territorial seizure (−1), Löwenritter coup (−3). PI recovers: Hafenmark Parliamentary Manoeuvre success (+1), Crown Parliamentary Session policy (+1).` (`parliament.md › Parliament Integrity (PI) Scale`). Also: Autonomous stage `PI −1`, Split `PI −3` (`conflict_architecture › Graduated Löwenritter Autonomy`); Ministry Stabilisation and Legislative Record (`ministry.md`); Reconstitution at `PI = 0` (`institutions › Löwenritter — Reconstitution`); `Regency primary victory (PI ≥ 4 required)` (same); `HF victory needs PI ≥ 5` (`ci_political › §6.2`). **CT-27**: the table is monotone (higher PI = more functional Parliament) but `core` says `Auto-resolves at PI ≥ 20 (Crown elimination)` and calls it a "pressure meter"; no rule states what happens between 11 and 20. **CT-27** also: the abbreviation PI is used for **Public Instability** in `stats_1_7_scale › PP-255 / ED-174 / PP-281` (`PI ≥ 8: revolt check … PI = 10: GM narrative uprising event`; `PI increase cap +2/season`) and `strategic_layer` (`Public Instability = 4`).

**F-03.06 Public Instability.** Hybrid (`stats_1_7_scale › Public Instability — Hybrid Mode (PP-237)`): `Range: 0–10. Starting value: 5. In TTRPG mode: not a separate clock. Effects folded into Institutional Pressure. Hybrid increases: +1 per season Revolution Agitation resolves (any degree); +1 per season IP increases while CI > 40. Hybrid decreases: −1 per season Crown or Guilds completes successful social Domain Action in contested territory. Threshold 8: Revolution gains one free Agitation action at no Domain Action cost. Threshold 10: Shared loss condition check (Institutional collapse — distinct from MS=0 Rupture).` BG (`› PP-255`): `Range 0–10. Start 5. Accrual: +1/season any faction PS < 3 at accounting. Recovery: −1/season zero hostile Stability-targeting DAs. PI ≥ 8: revolt check (Stability ≤ 3 factions → Stability Ob 2; fail → PS −1). PI = 10: GM narrative uprising event.` (**H-07**). `PP-281 — PI cascade brake: At PI ≥ 8: revolt-pass → Stability +1. PI increase rate cap: +2/season max from all combined sources.` `Public Instability −1 (Church Territorial Seizure)` (`strategic_layer › Cascade Test 4`). "Revolution" is an alias for Restoration Movement [INFERRED from context].

**F-03.07 Turmoil (= Strain).** `Global, 0–10 … Starts at 0.` Advance — **CT-12**: `+1 per season with inter-faction battle. +2 per faction elimination. +1 per Revolt.` (`tracks.md › Turmoil (Global, 0–10) — PP-646`; `core › Starting Values`; `clocks › Battle Consequences`; `settlement_adjacency › §2.3` `Strain +1: applied peninsula-wide`) vs `Turmoil (range 0–10) rises from territory-instability (Accord ≤ 1 territory thresholds at Accounting — see peninsular_strain_v30 §4.1), faction eliminations (+2), and revolts (+1). ~~Direct battle-occurrence trigger STRUCK by ED-743~~ (2026-04-29).` (`ci_political › §4.4`). Decay — **CT-13**: `−1 per peaceful season (no battles, no revolts). −1 per diplomatic resolution (max 1/season).` (`tracks`; `core`) vs `Strain −1 (min 0) if ALL territories at Accord ≥ 2 this season (no territory-instability — per peninsular_strain §4.2, ED-797 correction; supersedes prior "no battles AND no Revolts" condition). (ii) If diplomatic resolution occurred (Treaty formed, Pledge honoured): Strain −1 (max one from this source/season).` (`phases › Phase 5` 4d). Threshold table — **CT-15** (three versions):

| Strain | `tracks.md` | `core (1).md` | `ci_political_v30.md › §4.4` |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0–2 Peace | None. | No effect. | No effect |
| 3–4 Tension | `All factions: L check at Accounting (pool vs Ob 1). Failure: L −1.` | `L check at Accounting (L pool vs Ob 1). Failure: L −1.` | `Mandate check at Accounting (Mandate pool vs Ob 1). Failure → Mandate −1` |
| 5–6 Fracture | `Accord −1 in one territory (lowest first).` | `Accord −1 in one territory (lowest-Accord first, controller choice).` | `Accord −1 in one non-capital territory (controller's choice)` |
| 7–8 Crisis | `Accord −1 in ALL non-capital territories. L check Ob 2.` | same | `Mandate check Ob 2` |
| 9–10 Collapse | `Non-capital Accord cap 2. L check Ob 3. RS −1/season additional.` | `MS −1/season additional` | `Mandate check Ob 3. MS −1/season additional` |

**F-03.08 Torben and Elske Loyalty.** Torben `0–7 … start 7`; `On Crown elimination: Torben Loyalty track transfers to Löwenritter (they inherit the succession claim). Löwenritter wins or loses Torben via Influence actions the same way Crown did. Church and Hafenmark may contest via Senator Outward Diplomacy (Ob = current Torben Loyalty ÷ 2).` (`core`). Year-End modifiers (SET-02 D-02.07). Löwenritter autonomy increment `Torben's loyalty reaches 3–2 or lower` (`stats_1_7_scale › Löwenritter — Martial Law / Coup Trigger`). Tensions card T1 sets `Torben Loyalty starts at 3 (not 7)` (`early_game › §4.1`, superseded). Elske `0–7 … 4`; diplomatic repulsion needs `Elske Loyalty ≥ 6` (`campaign_architecture › §5.2`); `Elske CANNOT return this season … The return condition requires Institutional Pressure < 60` (`strategic_layer › Scenario C`) — the return rule itself is not stated anywhere (**GP-03.02**). Residency (`campaign_architecture › §5.3`): Elske unaffected by Löwenritter stages Loyal/Restless/Autonomous; at Split `If Crown enters open civil war and is at risk of elimination (Crown Stability ≤ 1), Altonia may recall Elske on its own diplomatic calculation` — an NPC choice with no rule (**H-09**).

**F-03.09 Warden tracks.** WC effects: `0 | No effect. ≥ 1 | +1D to all Thread operations peninsula-wide. ≥ 2 | MS decay rate halved … 3 | MS +2/season at Accounting.` (`core`). `WR ≥ 2 required to advance` WC (`core`, PP-605) vs Expedition Forgetting `Success: … Cooperation +1` with no WR precondition (`geography › Expedition Procedure`). WR ladder (`geography › Varfell Path B`): `WR 0: unaware; 1: observed Vaynard (≥ 1 successful Expedition); 2: recognise as steward (prior WA ≥ +1 AND WC ≥ 1 conditions collapsed here); 3: actively cooperate (+1D Thread ops peninsula-wide); 4: Edeyja has made substantive contact (Overwhelming Forgetting Check). WR advances: successful Expedition seasons (+1 per Overwhelming or cumulative Successes). WR decreases: RM emergence (WR −2); T15 neglect 3+ seasons (no Expedition attempted): WR −1. Blocked if: WR has ever returned to 0 after first advancing past 1.` `+1 per Overwhelming or cumulative Successes` — "cumulative" unquantified (**GP-03.03**). Accounting steps 9–10 Warden Emergence / Cooperation checks — the check conditions are not in the corpus (**GP-03.04**). Warden's Accord (WA) is referenced (`insurgency › §3.1` `WA ≤ −2`; `faction_politics › §1.3b` `WA ≥ +1`) but never defined (**GP-03.05**).

**F-03.10 Other tracks.** Church Attention Pool: `Per-territory ceiling: 10. First Inquisitor at AP ≥ 3. Second Inquisitor at AP ≥ 6. Max 2 Inquisitors/territory.` (`tracks › Church Attention Pool — Per-Territory Rules (PP-185)`); resets to 0 at Accounting step 5 (`phases`); `+1 Church Attention Pool` on Survey failure at Depth ≥ 3 (`core › Survey`); `+1 Exposure … +1 Church Attention Pool if observed` (`player_agency › §1.6`). **CT-54**: `Church Attention Pool reaching 5 in assigned territory without response` (`faction_politics › §1.4 Bishop's Delegate demotion`). RDT/TD (SET-04). Thread Tension (TT): thresholds `TT 50`, deltas `−10/−6/−3/+8`, cap `max −5 per season` (`southernmost.md`), `Ob = Thread Tension ÷ 20` (`insurgency › §3.3`), `TT +2` (`worldbuilding › §6.2`), `+0.5 per season` (`faction_politics › §2.6 Broker`) — TT has no stated range or start (**GP-03.06**). Generational Shift clock: `0–10 … +1 per 5 years of game time. Threshold 2 (Year 10): … −1 to their highest attribute … Exception: TS ≥ 50 exempt. Threshold 4 (Year 20): −2 … Threshold 6 (Year 30): −3` (`settlement_layer › §7.1`) — **CT-42** with `faction_politics › Part 8` Torben trigger model. `Political Stability | 0 start, +1 per violence event | Unchanged | Cumulative. Self-regulating via victory gate (≤ 6).` (`settlement_layer › §7.1`) — a clock named nowhere else (**GP-03.07**). Coup Counter vs stages (SET-09, CT-21). Southernmost Awareness (SET-13). Jarnstal Drift `0–3, Game Master-tracked, private` (**H-10**). Deniability Debt 0–7 (SET-10). `Patience Protocol` PC (`strategic_layer › I-02`, Scenario B) — its rules are not in the corpus (**GP-03.08**). VTM — `STRUCK (PP-663)` (`tracks`, `core`) yet still load-bearing in `phases` 9b (`VTM ≥ 4`), `geography › Champion TS Values` (`VTM 3+`), `geography › Varfell Path B` (`VTM ≥ 3`), `ci_political › §6.2` (`VTM ≥ 2`), `parliament › Institutional Mandate` (`VTM for Varfell`), `player_agency › §3.3` (`advance VTM`) (**GP-03.09**).

**F-03.11 Environmental exemption.** `State-based modifiers (MS/CI/IP/PI environmental effects) do NOT count against` the Cascade cap (`clocks › Cascade Depth Cap`). → TL-06.

**Gaps.** GP-03.01 unnamed 0–5 clock. GP-03.02 Elske return rule. GP-03.03 WR "cumulative Successes". GP-03.04 Warden Emergence/Cooperation check conditions. GP-03.05 WA undefined. GP-03.06 TT range/start. GP-03.07 "Political Stability" clock. GP-03.08 Patience Protocol rules. GP-03.09 VTM struck but referenced in six live rules. GP-03.10 IP "Altonian pressure table" referenced by Accounting step 4 does not exist (`strategic_layer › G-11` says so). GP-03.11 no rule for PI values 11–19. GP-03.12 Underground Network / Altonian Governorate entities (campaign §5) have stats but no actions, priority tree or placement rule.

### SET-03 Pipelines

**PL-03.01 MS depletion to Rupture.** Entry: MS 72. Steps: per-season sources (battles, Thread ops, harvest, Strain Collapse, Thread Debt drain) and Year-End drift −1; band changes fire threshold events (Accounting step 8) and Revelation scenes (SET-13); at MS ≤ 10 Surge once; at MS = 0 `campaign ends, all factions lose`. Terminal: Rupture. Advanced by: Accounting. Reversible: MS can rise (Menders, WC 3, Weaves) — Rupture is terminal.

**PL-03.02 IP escalation ladder.** Entry: IP 20. Steps: band effects at 30/60; `IP 75` Vanguard appears at T10 (PL-03.03); `80+` skirmishes; `≥ 90` NW pass event; `100` Phase 1 → `85+ ×3 seasons` Phase 2 (Governorate, Schoenland corridor, Underground Network) → `80+ ×3 more` Phase 3. Branch: retreat thresholds `<85 / <75 / <60`; repulsion paths (military / diplomatic / resistance) → reset & freeze. Terminal: full withdrawal + freeze, or permanent occupation. Advanced by: IP delta sources (CT-26). Reversible: phases retreat; freeze is irreversible.

**PL-03.03 Vanguard advance.** Entry: IP ≥ 75. Steps: place at T10; each Accounting: if uncontested 2 consecutive seasons → move to next node on `T10→T3→T2→T1`; if a faction present → automatic Battle `defender Military vs Ob 3`; Win → held, Loss → advances; at T1 all factions `−1 Stability/season`. Terminal: elimination conditions "authorial review required" (H-08). Route violates adjacency (CT-32).

**PL-03.04 Turmoil update (Accounting 4d).** Entry: Accounting. Steps: (i) if ALL territories Accord ≥ 2 → Strain −1 (min 0); (ii) if diplomatic resolution → Strain −1 (max 1); (iii) apply threshold effects (D-03.14). Elsewhere-stated increments (+1 battle season / +2 elimination / +1 Revolt) precede — order unstated (**GP-03.15**). Terminal: new Strain value.

**PL-03.05 PI lifecycle.** Entry: PI 7. Steps: degrade (Emergency Powers −1 unless Ministry Stabilisation; Church seizure −1 unless Ministry objection; Autonomous −1; coup/Split −3) / recover (Manoeuvre success +1; Parliamentary Session policy +1; Legislative Record +1 first ruling per year; Ministry priority 1 & 4). Branches: PI ≤ 2 → CI +2, Hafenmark loses Manoeuvre, Crown governs by decree; PI = 0 → Reconstitution available (Löwenritter post-Split, `L vs Ob 3`: OW → PI 2; S → PI 1; P/F → Stability −1, F also Church L +1); `PI ≥ 20 → Crown elimination` (core). Terminal: Crown elimination (CT-27).

**PL-03.06 Public Instability revolt check.** Entry: PI(PubInst) ≥ 8 at Accounting. Steps: each faction with Stability ≤ 3 rolls Stability Ob 2; fail → PS −1; pass → Stability +1 (PP-281); at 10 → shared-loss check (Hybrid) / GM event (BG, H-07). Advanced by Accounting.

**PL-03.07 Torben Loyalty transfer.** Entry: Crown eliminated. Steps: track transfers to Löwenritter; contest by Church/Hafenmark via Senator Outward `Ob = current Torben Loyalty ÷ 2`. Terminal: none stated.

**PL-03.08 WR/WC advancement.** Entry: Expedition into T15 (SET-13 PL-13.01). Steps: Forgetting Check degree → `Cooperation +1` on Success/OW (geography) gated by `WR ≥ 2` (core — conflict); WR +1 per Overwhelming or "cumulative Successes"; WR −2 on RM emergence; WR −1 on 3+ seasons neglect; WC effects at 1/2/3. Terminal: WC 3 (MS +2/season). Reversible: WR down; WC not stated as decreasing.

### SET-03 Throughlines applied
TL-06 (environmental effects exempt from cap) asserted. TL-07 (caps): CI ±5 asserted; Consecration Crisis `CI +10 / −5 / +3` (`baralta › §2–3`) and Sovereign Authority Doctrine `CI −3` exceed or approach it with no cap note (CT-75). TL-09 (T15/T16 out of play) — Vanguard deploys from T16 (`geography` T16 special) and Phase 2 corridor "through Schoenland" (CT-18/TL-09 violation). TL-14 (stable aliases) — violated by IP double expansion and PI double meaning (CT-26, CT-27). TL-12 — H-07, H-08, H-09, H-10.
