## SET-04 — Church Influence, Piety & Seizure

### SET-04 Flattened statement

**F-04.01 Piety Track (PT) per territory.** Range 0–5; `Poles: 0 = Einhir Restoration pole. 5 = Solmund Orthodoxy pole` (quoted in `faction_politics › §4.1`); `CV ≡ PT mechanically` (`faction_politics › §4.2`). Starting values (`tracks.md › Starting Piety Track (PT) — PP-652 (provisional)`, `[PROVISIONAL — pending user review]`): `T1 3, T2 3, T3 3, T4 2, T5 3, T6 1, T7 3, T8 3, T9 5, T10 3, T11 2, T12 2, T13 1, T14 3, T15 0, T17 3` (T16 absent). `PT applies per-settlement. Province PT = floor-average.` (`fractional_province_ownership › §2.5`). Seasonal cap `±1 PT` (implied by `ci_seizure › Seizure Results` "NOT subject to the ±1 PT seasonal cap" and `faction_politics › §1.4 Deacon` "subject to ±1/season cap"). PT momentum: `+1D to PT change actions in SW ≥ 3 territories` (`geography › Spiritual Weight`) / `in high-SW territories` (`ci_political › §1`). Sources of PT change: Church buildings (F-04.06) — **CT-53** (`Cathedral +2/season` exceeds the ±1 cap); Parish/Cathedral floors (F-04.06); RM `PT reduction through cultural displacement` (`campaign_architecture › §2.2`, no rate); RM PT decay (`insurgency › §2`); Cultural Reclamation `Varfell: Influence vs Ob 2, target-territory Piety −1 toward Einhir Restoration` (`core` header comment, ED-880); CI 80 Year-End drift `+1 … unless WC ≥ 2`; Mass Seizure OW `PT +1 in target territory (consequence, not cap-governed)`; Deacon services `raises PT/CV +1 in the serving territory during service`; Community Weaving `PT −1` (`insurgency › §3.3`); RM Governance Transition `PT −1` / `0.5` (`settlement_layer › §4.3`).

**F-04.02 Spiritual Weight (SW).** `fixed attribute on the territory card … set at game start` (`ci_political › §1`). Values: `T1 2, T2 2, T3 2, T4 1, T5 2, T6 1, T7 2, T8 3, T9 5, T10 2, T11 1, T12 2, T13 1, T14 3, T15 0, T16 1, T17 2; Total 32` (`ci_political › §1`; `geography › Spiritual Weight`). Uses: `Piety Yield weighting: CI from Piety Yield = Σ(PT tier × SW factor) per prominent territory, where SW factor = SW/5`; `Church political pool … includes Σ(SW of Prominent territories) / 5 bonus dice`; `PT momentum`.

**F-04.03 Seasonal CI generation — CONTRADICTION CT-09.** Canonical sequence (`ci_seizure.md › CI Generation — Seasonal`), verbatim: `1. Conditional Passive (§3.2): CI +1 only if Church L > controlling faction L in ≥ 2 territories. 2. Piety Yield (§3.3): CI += Σ(PT tier × SW/5) per prominent territory, floored. 3. Charity Advantage (§3.4): Church Wealth spent on charity → CI +1 per 2 Wealth, cap 2/season. 4. Templar Presence (§3.5): +1 CI per territory with Church military unit AND Church Prominence. 5. Assert (§3.6): Optional Church action. Influence vs Ob 2. Success: CI +1. Failure: Stability −1. 6. Suppress (§3.7): Optional opponent action. L vs Ob = floor(Church L / 2) + 1. Success: negate Step 1. Failure: Stability −1. 7. Hafenmark Structural Suppression (§3.8): While Baralta L ≥ 4, CI −1/season.` Cap: `±3 per season from player-initiated Domain Actions. ±5 per season from all sources combined`. Competing statements: `CI advances by +1 per season from institutional momentum, regardless of Church action. … Assert (Church) +2 total (replaces passive; not additive). Suppress … Negates passive +1 for that season only. CI does not decrease. … Suppress may be declared once per season by one faction. It cannot reduce CI below its value at season start.` (`stats_1_7_scale › CI Passive Advance (PP-402)`); `Passive +1/season if Church Mandate ≥ 3` (`settlement_layer › §7.1`); `T9 … CI +1/season Church controls` (`geography › Territory Table`; also `core › Faction Elimination` "T9 CI +1/season bonus stops"). `ci_political › §0` records `CI passive | PP-402 unconditional +1 | Conditional passive per military_layer_v30 §3.2` as the change made. Piety-yield worked figures: `T9 (SW 5, PT 5): yield = 1.0 × (5/5) = 1.0; T8 (SW 3, PT 3): yield = 0.25 × (3/5) = 0.15` — the "PT tier" → {1.0, 0.25} mapping is never tabulated (**GP-04.01**). `T9 … counts against the ±5/season CI cap like any other territory. The cap is uniform. No T9 bypass.` (`ci_political › §7.1`, ED-721 Option A). Seizure CI gains: `distinct category (not DA). All CI sources subject to ±5/season combined cap. ±3 DA sub-cap does not apply to seizure.` (`stats › PP-254`). Hafenmark suppression variants — **CT-69**: `While Baralta L ≥ 4, CI −1/season` (`ci_seizure`; `stats › Sovereign Authority Doctrine` "Suppression ends if L < 4 or excommunication (CI +4 immediately)"); `RDT 4 | CI suppression extends: −1 CI/season while Hafenmark L ≥ 3 (was ≥ 4)`; vs `Baralta | Church Influence suppression | −1 Legitimacy/season to each Church-controlled settlement (settlement_layer §1.8) while Baralta L ≥ 4` (`stats › Faction NPC Trigger Conditions`). Suppress Ob — **CT-52** (SET-01 F-01.10); coalition suppression (`parliament › PP-296`) vs `once per season by one faction` (`stats › PP-402`). `Church must have more CI gain routes than Baralta has suppression capacity` (`ci_seizure › Church CI Gain — Redesign Required (ED-110)`) [ASPIRATION; resolved by `ci_political › §3.3` note].

**F-04.04 CI as political weight.** `Church's CI institutional weight = ⌊CI/20⌋, applied per the forum's resolution method` — Parliament motion `+⌊CI/20⌋ votes`; Crown Treaty targeting Church, Diplomacy vs playable faction, Treaty positioning/ratification `+⌊CI/20⌋ to margin M`; `Margin contributions are clamped by the resolver's FLOOR 0.05 / CAP 0.90 … No forum uses "bonus dice."` (`ci_political › §3.2`, ED-865/874). Magnitudes: `CI 28: +1; 40: +2; 60: +3; 80: +4; 100: +5`. Opposition: `each secular faction voting against Church reduces their effective Mandate contribution by floor(CI/30): 0–29 0; 30–59 −1; 60–89 −2; 90+ −3`; `floored at 0` (`ci_political › §3.3`). Pool statement: `Church: Mandate + ⌊CI/20⌋ votes. Non-Church faction voting against Church: max(0, Mandate − ⌊CI/30⌋) votes` (`§3.4`). **CT-64**: `Church: Political pool = L + floor(CI/20). Non-Church (anti-Church motions): Political pool = L − floor(CI/30).` (`ci_seizure › Faction Political Pool`) and `administers the CI bonus-dice pool (floor(CI/20))` (`faction_politics › §1.4 Standing 7`) and `Church gets floor(CI/20) bonus dice` (`ci_political › §7.2`, its own pre-migration audit text). **GP-04.02**: the separate `Σ(SW of Prominent territories) / 5 bonus dice` (`ci_political › §1` use case 2; `geography`) is not carried into `§3.4`. TTRPG mode: `Floor(CI/20) bonus dice on relevant Influence/Mandate rolls` (`ci_political › §7.8`). Milestone 65: two action slots for anti-Church motions; 55: `Ob +1` opposing Church DAs. Non-Church rank interactions (`faction_politics › §5.3`): Hafenmark at CI 100 `−3 penalty; Hafenmark effectively cannot pass any motion opposing Church without Extraordinary Parliamentary Manoeuvre` (undefined, **GP-04.03**); Crown Regent at CI 100 `Linder may effectively veto Crown Regent actions with CI implications`; Varfell at CI 100 `Private Collection use generates Church Attention Pool +1 per use`.

**F-04.05 Church Prominence.** `Church player marks Prominent territories on faction mat — any territory where Church global L exceeds that territory's controlling faction's global L. Updated every Accounting. Used for: Counter-Narrative target eligibility, Seizure Ob formula, Piety Spread.` (`phases › Phase 5` 4b, ED-326); `Prominence required: Church L > controlling faction's L in target territory` (`ci_seizure › Seizure Ob`); `Prominence assessed at seizure declaration. (PP-509)`. Cathedral (institutions form) gives `Church Prominence +1 in territory for seizure calculations` — additive on a boolean predicate (**GP-04.04**). "Counter-Narrative" and "Piety Spread" are referenced only (**GP-04.05**).

**F-04.06 Church infrastructure per settlement — two systems, CT-68.**
(a) Four axes (`campaign_architecture › §1.1 Four Independent Axes`; `settlement_layer › §1.5`): Axis 1 Religious Building `None 0 | 0; Chapel +0.5/season | 0; Church +1/season | −1; Cathedral +2/season, +0.5 to adjacent | −2 … Irreplaceable. Destroyed only by Mass Battle.`; Axis 2 Templar Station `+1 CI/season in territory. Can interrupt rival Domain Actions (+1 Ob, costs 1 CI). Seizure Ob −1.`; Axis 3 Inquisitor Base `Surveillance Zone: practitioners Concealment test each season. RM organizing +1 Ob. 1 Church Attention/season if RM present. Seizure Ob −1.`; Axis 4 Church Governor `Church official governs settlement. De facto Church territory. Removal: Mass Battle, Mandate Challenge (Ob 6+), or RM community action OW. Seizure Ob −2.` Combined: `Maximum per-settlement Seizure Ob modifier: −6 … Territory modifier = sum across all settlements.` (`campaign_architecture › §1.2`) vs `Cap: −4 per settlement (campaign_architecture_v1 refinement)` (`settlement_layer › §1.5`) — **CT-11**. Parish Social Services (`settlement_layer › §1.6`): `Chapel +0.5 Order/season (rounds: +1 every other season); Church +1 Order at installation (one-time); Cathedral +1 Order at installation + Order decay −1`. Pastoral Assumption (`§1.7`): no governor AND `at least a Chapel` → Church installs Church Governor `as a Domain Action at Ob 1`; revocable `(Ob = Church Influence ÷ 2, Order −1, Disposition −2 with Church)` — **CT-56** ("Church Influence" = clock 0–100 or stat 6?).
(b) Parish/Cathedral (`institutions.md › Parish / Cathedral System — Church (ED-319 RESOLVED)`): `Prerequisite: Church Consul Inward action in territory. Parish: 2 sequential successful Consul Inward actions + 1 Wealth. Effect: PT floor raised to 1 (persists unless Cathedral). Cathedral: 3 more successful Consul Inward (5 total from zero) + 2 additional Wealth. Effect: PT floor 2 + Church Prominence +1 in territory for seizure calculations. Control change: Parish survives. Cathedral degrades to Parish on control transfer. Limits: Max 1 Parish or Cathedral per territory. Max 1 upgrade attempt per territory per arc.` The tier names (Parish/Cathedral vs Chapel/Church/Cathedral), the build path, the transfer behaviour and the Cathedral's replaceability all differ between (a) and (b).
Inquisitor deployment thresholds — **CT-54** (F-03.10). `Community Organizing success: AP −2 + expels one Inquisitor if AP drops below deployment threshold.` (`tracks › PP-185`). Templar deployment requires Cardinal of Fortitude active (`institutions`; SET-10). Church settlement subnational management `+1 Piety Influence per season` (`settlement_layer › §3.3`) — "Piety Influence" undefined (**GP-04.06**).

**F-04.07 Ecclesiastical (Bishop) Appointment.** `Domain Action (Consul Outward). Available when: settlement has Church building ≥ tier 2 (Church or Cathedral) AND settlement has no governor OR settlement governor Disposition toward Church ≥ +2. Church rolls Influence vs Ob 1. Success: Church installs a bishop-governor. The settlement is now Church-governed. Its Prosperity flows to Church. Its Order is maintained by Church institutional infrastructure.` Consequences: `Province fractionalizes …; Accord in the settlement adjusts by PT alignment (high PT territory: population accepts; low PT: population resists); … Casus Belli NOT generated` (`conflict_architecture_proposal › Church Expansion: Bishop Appointment`). Bishop-governors `follow Church NPC Priority Tree` (`settlement_layer › §3.2`). "adjusts by PT alignment" has no numbers (**GP-04.07**). `Pre-Seizure accumulation path (PP-TBD): Bishop Appointment … is the settlement-level precursor to Mass Seizure.` (`ci_seizure`). Precedence between Pastoral Assumption (Chapel+, no governor, Ob 1) and Bishop Appointment (Church+, no governor or Disposition ≥ +2, Ob 1) unstated (**GP-04.08**).

**F-04.08 Seizure — five models, CT-10.**
(a) **CI 75 Territorial Seizure** (legacy): `CI 75 seizure: Church L vs Ob = Fort + 1 (PP-192/PP-421)`; `Cap: 2 territory transfers per seizure event (v04/v05 P-23). Previously set to 4 by PP-183 — reverted.` (`parliament › PP-189 Final Corrections`; `ci_seizure › CI 75 Seizure — Territorial Seizure (PP-421)`); `Church Territorial Seizure of T1 (Valorsplatz) requires removing the Ministry AP-token first. If AP-token present: seizure Ob +1` (`ministry.md`); `CI 75 Church Territorial Seizure does not target T15` (`geography`); `Church Influence 80+ triggers seizure at next Accounting. Territory roll: Church Military vs Defender Military, Ob 2. Each success: one contested territory flips. Hard cap: maximum 2 territory transfers per seizure event per faction` (`strategic_layer › P-23`); `Per-territory roll for Church at Church Influence 80: Church Mandate vs Ob 3 (contested territory) or Ob 2 (allied/neutral)` (`strategic_layer › Cascade Test 4`). `Old: Seizure gated by CI ≥ 75 (removed).` (`ci_political › §7.6`).
(b) **Mass Seizure (one-shot, probabilistic)** (`ci_seizure.md › Church Mass Seizure (one-shot, replaces PP-494)`): `Available: CI ≥ 60. Declaration probability per season: P = ((CI−60)/40)^3.3, clamped [0,1]. 1% at CI 70, 10% at CI 80, 100% at CI 100. One attempt only.` `Pool: Influence + floor(CI / 15). At CI 60: 10D. At CI 100: 12D.` `Ob: 10 − PT − infrastructure modifiers (floor 1). PT 5 with full infra = Ob 1. PT 0 no infra = Ob 10.` Table: `PT 5 (Piety) | 2; 4 | 6 (before infra); 3 | 7; 2 | 8; 1 | 9; 0 (Restoration) | 7` — internally inconsistent with `10 − PT` (which gives 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10) (**CT-10**). `Prerequisites: Church L ≥ 4. Prominence … assessed at seizure declaration. (PP-509)`. `Overwhelming: PT +1 in target territory (consequence, not cap-governed).` `Failure: Stability −1.` `Political cost: Casus Belli granted to controlling faction on every seizure attempt. (PP-510)`. `Fort Level does NOT modify Seizure Ob. Seizure is a political act. (PP-506)`. `Battle requirement: If the target territory has a garrison (Fort ≥ 1 AND at least one military unit present from the controlling faction), Church must first win a Battle before Seizure proceeds. … If Church loses: no Seizure this season; Casus Belli still granted. An ungarrisoned fortified territory (Fort ≥ 1, no units present) may be Seized without Battle. (PP-506)`. Visibility: `CI < 50: all factions observe … CI ≥ 50: only factions with Intel ≥ 3 in the target territory observe. (PP-507) [PROVISIONAL]` — "Intel ≥ 3 in the target territory" is a per-territory Intel that no document defines (**GP-04.09**). `One seizure attempt per season. Cannot target T15 (Askeheim) or T16 (Schoenland).` "One attempt only" and "one seizure attempt per season" coexist (**GP-04.10**: whether the one-shot is the declaration or each territory roll). `Riskbreaker exposure removes seized territory and prevents re-seizure for one season.` (`stats › CI 60 Territorial Seizure`). Confirmed `Seizure Ob formula … 10 − PT − infrastructure (floor 1)` (`ci_political › §7.6`, ED-NEW-CI-11).
(c) **CI 60 Territorial Seizure (Unique Action)** (`stats_1_7_scale › Church — CI 60 Territorial Seizure`): `Trigger: Church Influence (CI) reaches 60. Fires once per territory. Roll: Influence + floor(CI/15) vs Ob = 7 − PT (AUTHORITATIVE per faction_layer §2.7; supersedes the stale L-based formula). Failure → Mandate −1.` `Success: Administrative control of territory. Domain Actions vs Church authority require +2 Ob. Flat Church Influence value fires immediately.` Ob `7 − PT` contradicts (b)/(d).
(d) **CI = 100 Mass Seizure Declaration** (`campaign_architecture › §1.3`): `When CI reaches 100: every territory with at least one settlement containing a Church building (Chapel+) is targeted for simultaneous Seizure. Individual Ob per territory = 10 − PT − (sum of infrastructure modifiers), floor 1. The Mass Seizure Declaration is a mandatory Zoom In scene. Archbishop declares. All factions get 1 Emergency Session season to respond. Then each territory resolves independently. … Seized territories start at Accord 1 (military-equivalent) or Accord 2 (if PT ≥ 3).` And (`ci_political › §2.2`): `When CI reaches 100: CI does not continue to advance; Church publicly declares Papal Sovereignty …; Mass Seizure (one-shot) …; Secular factions each get one free Parliamentary motion (no card slot) to oppose Church per season while Unification is active; Mass Seizure bid fails if: Church loses 3 territories to counterattack in a single Year-End, OR Church Mandate drops to 3 or below. No second attempt.` The "Theocracy Unification victory" clause is `[SUPERSEDED-BY: GD-1]`. (b) makes mass seizure a probabilistic event from CI 60; (d) makes it mandatory at CI 100 — both are "one-shot" (**CT-10**).
(e) Church AI: `Church Seizure when CI ≥ 40 and prominent territories have PT ≥ 3` (`ci_political › §6.2`) — below every availability threshold above.
Modifiers common across models: `TD 4 | Church Seizure in Hafenmark territories: Ob +2`; `TD 5 | Church cannot seize T8 Gransol … Permanent.` (`tracks`); Ministry `procedural objection: Church Seizure in that territory delayed 1 season … AP-token … consumed` (`ministry › priority 3`); Latent RM `Resist Seizure … +1 Ob to Church seizure roll. Passive` (`insurgency › §3.3`); CI 80 `Seizure Ob reduced by 1 globally`; CI 40 `+1D on … Seizure rolls`; Cardinal Bishop's Delegate `deputize Inquisitors for Unification Seizure support` (`faction_politics › §5.1`). Post-seizure Accord — **CT-65**: `Accord 1 … or Accord 2 (if PT ≥ 3)` (`campaign_architecture`) vs `Military conquest → Accord 1. Non-military acquisition → Accord 2+.` (`tracks › Accord`) with Seizure declared `a political act` (PP-506).

**F-04.09 Excommunication.** Ob cap: `Ob = floor(target L / 2) + 1.` (`tracks › Church Excommunication Ob Cap (PP-180)`; `parliament › L Suppression`). Resolver: `contested (faction leader) M = Mandate − target Mandate, non-leader M = Mandate − 2` (`stats › Unique Actions`) — **CT-37**. Effects: `Success | Strips target's Circles bonus; −1 Legitimacy to each of the target's controlled settlements` (`stats`); `Reversal: Grand Debate (5 exchanges) or new Confessor appointed.` Procedure (`institutions › Excommunication — Canonical Procedure`): `Must pay penance: Wealth −1 per season until penance complete (3 seasons). Must perform public service: one action per season must be Senator Outward (Diplomacy) toward Church. Banishment (severe cases): faction permanently barred from Church-controlled territories.` Reversal paths (`worldbuilding › §3.5`): `Penance (voluntary): target regains standing after 1 season public service. Church Mandate +1. Grand Debate (existing): 5 exchanges. Banishment (Church Mandate vs Ob 4): permanent expulsion from public office` — **CT-37** (3-season vs 1-season penance; banishment as case vs as roll). Against Hafenmark at RDT 5: `costs +2 L`; excommunicating Hafenmark ends structural suppression and `CI +4 immediately` (`stats › SAD`). `Excommunication-related CB … for any Church-allied parliamentary faction` (`parliamentary_transfer › §3`). PC leader excommunicated → `faction reverts to NPC institutional tendency` (`stats › PP-244`). Standing consequence for a player: `Standing drops to −1` (`faction_politics › §1.0a`). Violation-score `+2` on Legitimacy (`faction_behavior › §3.5.2`).

**F-04.10 RDT / TD (Hafenmark private tracks).** RDT `0–5`: `Advances: Reformed Settlement event = +1 (max once per arc). Requires: Hafenmark controls territory where Church has Parish/Cathedral AND Hafenmark M ≥ 3 AND PI ≥ 4.` Effects: `0 | Reformed Settlement blocked: Church may Appease at no cost; 1 | Parliamentary Manoeuvre targeting CI: −1 Ob; 2 | Formal Reformed Settlement. TD track activates.; 3 | Church L −1 (institutional strain) + Diplomatic actions vs Church: −1 Ob; 4 | CI suppression extends: −1 CI/season while Hafenmark L ≥ 3 (was ≥ 4). Reformed territory PT actions +1 Ob for Church.; 5 | Excommunication against Hafenmark costs +2 L … Baralta: −2 Ob on CI Suppress actions. All diplomatic actions targeting Hafenmark from any faction: +1 Ob.` TD `0–5`: `Activates at RDT 2. Advances: +1 per arc when RDT ≥ 2 AND Church plays Assert. Church may freeze TD by playing Accommodate Reformed Settlement. Regression: TD −1 if Church Excommunicates Hafenmark AND RDT does not advance that arc.` Effects: `1 | Templar deployment costs +1 Wealth in Hafenmark-adjacent territories; 2 | Cardinal of Fortitude schism risk when Church Stability < 3; 3 | Any season Church loses PI: Hafenmark gains PI +1; 4 | Church Seizure in Hafenmark territories: Ob +2; 5 | Church cannot seize T8 Gransol … Permanent.` (`tracks.md › Hafenmark — RDT/TD Tracks (ED-321 RESOLVED)`). `Reformed Settlement Standing Effect (PP-201): When Reformed Settlement is in force (Church Influence ≥ 40 and Church has Resisted): All Diplomacy Domain Actions targeting Hafenmark: permanent +1 Ob … persists until Church Influence drops below 40 OR Reformed Settlement is withdrawn.` (`stats`). `Church NPC must respond at Accounting Step 5: Resist, Accommodate, or Ignore. … Resist: CI +3, RDT +1, Hafenmark +1 Standing vs Church, +1 Deed Token` (`strategic_layer › Cascade Test 2`; the Accommodate/Ignore branches are not stated — **GP-04.11**; "Deed Token" belongs to the retired Hollow Victory system). "TD 3 … Church loses PI" — Church has no PI (**GP-04.12**).

**F-04.11 Hafenmark Sovereign Authority Doctrine** (`stats › Hafenmark — Sovereign Authority Doctrine`): `Roll: Mandate vs Ob 4. Once per campaign arc.` `OW | CI −3; −1 Legitimacy to each Church-controlled settlement; Heresy Investigation blocked this season; +1D social vs Church for the arc; S | CI −2; −1 Legitimacy …; Heresy Investigation opens (Ob 4 to pursue); P | CI −1; HI opens immediately; CI +1; F | CI +1; HI immediate; Baralta's L −1`. Resolver status `pending decision` (GP-01.01).

**F-04.12 Heresy Investigation.** Referenced only: issued under Cardinal of Justice; unavailable during Justice gap; `one HI this season is false` if Justice compromised (`institutions`); `Ministry of Law agenda: All Heresy Investigation Obs +1` (`institutions`); Klapp Awakening precondition `no Heresy Investigation opened this season` (`worldbuilding › §3.3`); Mandatory Scene priority 2 `Heresy Investigation Target` (`player_agency › §4.2`); `Heresy Investigation that ends in Tribunal but not full Excommunication: drop 2 ranks` (`faction_politics › §1.0a`); `Investigate/Intel … +2 Ob in Church territory with Inquisitor` (`core`). No procedure, roll, or resolution is in the corpus (**GP-04.13**).

**F-04.13 Church Attention Pool resolution.** `5. Church Attention Pool: resolve threshold responses. Pool resets to 0.` (`phases`); Inquisitor thresholds (D-03.21); `+2` on operation-type reveal (`strategic_layer › Cascade Test 3`); Exposure per territory feeds it (`player_agency › §1.6`). "threshold responses" beyond Inquisitor deployment are unstated (**GP-04.14**).

**Gaps.** GP-04.01 PT-tier mapping; GP-04.02 SW-pool bonus orphaned; GP-04.03 "Extraordinary Parliamentary Manoeuvre"; GP-04.04 Prominence +1 on a boolean; GP-04.05 Counter-Narrative / Piety Spread undefined; GP-04.06 "Piety Influence"; GP-04.07 Bishop Appointment Accord adjustment magnitude; GP-04.08 Pastoral Assumption vs Bishop Appointment precedence; GP-04.09 per-territory Intel; GP-04.10 one-shot scope; GP-04.11 Accommodate/Ignore branches; GP-04.12 Church PI; GP-04.13 Heresy Investigation procedure; GP-04.14 Attention Pool responses; GP-04.15 charity spending is a Wealth sink with no action named; GP-04.16 "Sacred Assembly (Templar muster)" (`ci_political › §5.2`) undefined; GP-04.17 Templar interrupt of rival DA — timing relative to Phase 4 tiers unstated.

### SET-04 Pipelines

**PL-04.01 Seasonal CI generation.** Entry: Accounting step 4 ("CI per formula"). Steps: F-04.03 steps 1→7 in order; then clip to `±3` (DA sources) and `±5` (all). Branches: Assert/Suppress declared or not; Suppress Success negates step 1. Terminal: new CI; milestone/band crossings feed Accounting step 8. Advanced by Accounting. Reversible: CI is monotone-capped per season, not reversible.

**PL-04.02 Mass Seizure (probabilistic one-shot).** Entry: CI ≥ 60 at a season. Steps: (1) each season while undeclared, roll `P = ((CI−60)/40)^3.3` → declare or not; (2) on declaration (one attempt only): choose target (not T15/T16; one attempt per season — GP-04.10); (3) prerequisites `Church L ≥ 4`, Prominence at declaration; visibility D-04.18; (4) if garrisoned (D-04.17) → Battle first: lose → no Seizure this season, CB still granted; (5) roll `Influence + floor(CI/15)` vs D-04.13; (6) Degree: OW → transfer + `PT +1`; S → transfer; F → `Stability −1`; (7) CB granted to controlling faction regardless; (8) Ministry AP-token in target → delay 1 season (consumes token); Latent RM → +1 Ob; TD 4/5 mods. Terminal: territory transferred (Accord per D-04.20/CT-65) or consumed. Reversible: `Riskbreaker exposure removes seized territory and prevents re-seizure for one season`.

**PL-04.03 CI = 100 Mass Seizure Declaration.** Entry: CI reaches 100. Steps: (1) CI stops advancing; Papal Sovereignty declared; mandatory Zoom In scene; (2) one Emergency Session season — secular factions get one free Parliamentary motion per season while active; (3) every territory with a Chapel+ settlement resolves an independent seizure at `10 − PT − Σ infra` (floor 1); (4) seized territories → Accord 1 or 2; (5) each Year-End: bid fails if `Church loses 3 territories … OR Church Mandate ≤ 3`; `No second attempt`. Terminal: bid succeeded (territories held) or failed. Relation to PL-04.02's "one shot" unresolved (CT-10).

**PL-04.04 Bishop Appointment.** Entry: Church holds Consul card; a settlement satisfies D-04.24. Steps: `Influence vs Ob 1`; Success → bishop-governor installed (Church-governed; Prosperity flows to Church; Order by Church infra); province fractionalizes (SET-07 PL-07.01); Accord adjusts by PT alignment (GP-04.07); no CB. Terminal: Church-governed settlement. Reversible: province controller may consolidate (SET-07) or revoke (`settlement_layer › §3.3`).

**PL-04.05 Parish → Cathedral build (institutions form).** Entry: Church Consul Inward in territory. Steps: 2 sequential successes + 1 Wealth → Parish (PT floor 1); 3 more successes (5 total) + 2 Wealth → Cathedral (PT floor 2, Prominence +1); max 1 upgrade attempt per territory per arc. Branch: control transfer → Cathedral degrades to Parish; Parish survives. Terminal: Cathedral. (CT-68 vs Axis system where Cathedral is "Irreplaceable".)

**PL-04.06 Excommunication.** Entry: Church plays Unique Power; Cardinal of Justice active (gap suspends). Steps: target may Appease (Institutional Mandate, cancels, L −1) or Uphold; roll per D-04.25; Success → strip Circles bonus, −1 Legitimacy per target settlement; penance state (`Wealth −1/season` for 3 seasons; one Senator Outward toward Church per season); severe → Banishment; Hafenmark at RDT 5 → Church `+2 L` cost; Hafenmark excommunicated → suppression ends, `CI +4`; TD −1 if RDT did not advance; Punishment-mode CB to Church-allied factions. Reversal: Grand Debate (5 exchanges), new Confessor, or Penance (worldbuilding). Terminal: reversed or permanent.

**PL-04.07 RDT / TD.** Entry: RDT 0. Steps: Reformed Settlement event (D-04.27; `max once per arc`) → RDT +1; Church responds at Accounting step 5 (Resist: `CI +3, RDT +1, Hafenmark +1 Standing vs Church, +1 Deed Token`; Accommodate/Ignore: GP-04.11); at RDT 2 TD activates; each arc with RDT ≥ 2 AND Church Assert → TD +1; Accommodate Reformed Settlement freezes TD; Excommunication without RDT advance → TD −1. Terminal: RDT 5 / TD 5 (permanent effects).

**PL-04.08 Attention Pool → Inquisitors.** Entry: pool accrues during season. Steps: at Accounting step 5 resolve thresholds (`AP ≥ 3` first Inquisitor, `≥ 6` second, max 2); pool resets to 0; Community Organizing success `AP −2 + expels one Inquisitor if AP drops below deployment threshold` (ordering vs reset unstated — GP-04.20). Terminal: Inquisitors present (Surveillance Zone effects).

**PL-04.09 Sovereign Authority Doctrine.** Entry: Hafenmark, once per campaign arc. Steps: `Mandate vs Ob 4` → D-04.31 outcomes (CI deltas, settlement L, HI open/blocked, Baralta L −1 on Failure). Terminal: effects applied.

**PL-04.10 CI 80 Year-End PT drift.** Entry: Year-End with CI ≥ 80 and WC < 2. Steps: every territory PT +1 toward 5 (cap). Terminal: PT updated.

### SET-04 Throughlines applied
TL-04 (Accord semantics on acquisition) — CT-65. TL-07 (caps) — CT-53 (Cathedral +2 PT/season vs ±1), CT-75 (Consecration CI deltas). TL-08 (resolver) — Assert migrated (`M = Influence − 2`); Seizure, Suppress-by-coalition, Excommunication dice forms persist (CT-03, CT-37). TL-09 — `Cannot target T15 or T16` asserted. TL-11 (settlement granularity) — Church infrastructure is per settlement but PT/SW/Prominence/seizure are per territory; the "territory modifier = sum across all settlements" is the only bridge. TL-14 (aliases) — CT-56 ("Church Influence" clock vs stat), CV ≡ PT documented. TL-01 — the Theocracy Unification victory clause struck; `ci_political › §7.1` "Church victory requires CI ≥ 65" struck. TL-12 — none in this set beyond Attention Pool "responses" being undefined.

Four provinces.` `Each province has 1–3 settlements. … The minimum is 2 … 7 provinces at 3 settlements and 7 at 2 settlements (14 provinces total … = 35 settlements)` (`§2.1`). `Each settlement is connected by road to at least two other settlements … all 35 Kingdom settlements satisfy ≥2 connections, plus Himmelenger at 5 connections … Schoenland at 1 connection is the canonical foreign-exempt case.` (`§2.2`). `The province-level was previously called "territory" … (T1, T2, …, T17) — that nomenclature is deprecated by PP-726` (`§1.1`) vs `geography_v30 uses "territory" for the 17 top-level / province-tier map nodes (T1–T17) … kept exactly as-is` (`settlement_layer › §1.8` Map-tier terminology) — **CT-18** (17 territories vs 14 provinces + 3 specials; Himmelenger is a Church-capital territory T9 in `geography` but "not a province" in PP-726). **F-05.07 Settlement edge types** (`settlement_adjacency › §1.1`): `Road | 1 (standard) | none; River | 1 | Attacker −1D (river crossing penalty if defender holds far bank); Mountain Pass | 2 | Attacker −1D (altitude/exhaustion); Coastal | 1 (requires naval) | Attacker only via Port-type settlement; Thread-Witnessed | 0 (instantaneous, character-scale only) | n/a … Does not transfer armies.` `march_layer › §4.1` adds `gate | Askeheim Gate edges (T6↔T15, T13↔T15); marsh-entry, attacker −1D` and `river | Per bridges table; crossing penalty without bridge`. Generation rule (PP-723, `settlement_adjacency › §1.2`): hub = Seat, else `Seat > Cathedral > Fortress > City > Port > Town > Mine > Outpost`, ties by lowest S-ID; hub adjacent to every other settlement in province; hub-to-hub per territory edge; eight hand overrides (T1↔T16 via Riverside; T6↔T15 Stillhelm Watch↔Askeheim Gate; T13↔T15 Oastad↔Gate; T8↔T17 Harbor↔Mines; T3↔T17 Garrison Town↔Mines; T2↔T9 Kronmark↔Himmelenger City; T8↔T9 Market Quarter↔Himmelenger City; T1↔T5 Riverside↔Feldmark); four Thread-Witnessed edges (S-003↔S-023; S-011↔S-033; S-026↔S-025; S-029↔S-032, all old IDs). `Edge capacity: … Current spec: unlimited.` Thread-Witnessed edges: `character-scale only` (MARCH-02 open).

**F-05.08 Army movement — CT-62.** (a) `An army occupies one settlement at a time. … Intra-province: traverse 1 edge per season … Inter-province: traverse 1 edge per season … triggers Casus Belli check … Per season, an army may move up to Military ÷ 2 edges (round down, minimum 1). This replaces the current "one province per season" movement abstraction` (`settlement_adjacency › §1.3`). (b) `march_budget_pixels = Military × 100 × cavalry_modifier (1.5 if Cav ≥ 50% of army composition) × skirmish_modifier (1.3 if low-tier-only, no siege equipment)`; `Cost per traversed segment: distance_px × terrain_cost_multiplier`; `cavalry-skirmish stack: 1.5 × 1.3 = 1.95, capped at 1.7×`; `Dismounted cavalry … lose the modifier`; skirmish force `CANNOT initiate Settlement Siege … siege requires at least one Heavy Infantry or Siege unit`; `budgets do NOT pool. An army with 0 remaining budget cannot participate in a battle scheduled by another friendly army that turn`; `Combined-army engagement requires both armies arriving in the same Manoeuvre Phase tick` (`march_layer › §1`). `march_layer` declares itself the successor (`Supersedes (planned): settlement_adjacency_v30`) while `§11` says `Until then: … Mechanical mass-battle integration continues to use settlement_adjacency_v30 §2.2 modifiers as authoritative` — the movement-budget rule of (a) is neither superseded nor reconciled. `terrain_cost_matrix`, `distance_px` per edge, unit tiers (`Levy, Militia, Light Cavalry … Elite, heavy infantry`) and "Size" all reference absent data/docs (**GP-05.04**).

**F-05.09 Movement sequencing** (`march_layer › §5`): `Single march budget per army per season. Consumed across multiple edges until exhausted.`; stop at first settlement where `(a) budget is fully consumed, (b) engagement is forced (§2.4), or (c) player declines … Stopping mid-edge is not permitted`; on arrival at hostile settlement declare `ENGAGEMENT … or SIEGE …; ENGAGEMENT requires a defending army on-site; if the settlement has only garrison forces (no field army), the choice is SIEGE only — garrison cannot Manoeuvre. The choice is locked at arrival`. Route blocking (`§2.4`): `(a) hostile army occupies a settlement on the path with Size ≥ 1 (engagement is forced …); (b) sieged settlement on path is hostile-controlled (army must besiege or detour …); (c) winter weather closure on a mountain_pass edge between season Q4 and Q1 …; (d) calamity radiation band ≥ 4 (… player must opt-in)`. Bypass (`§4.3`): `+1 attrition per bypassed hostile settlement at season Accounting`. Supply (`§1.3`): `beyond a finite supply radius (default 8 settlements from a friendly Mine, Cathedral, or Port supply node) accumulate Attrition strain at +1 per season per 4 settlements over budget. Attrition strain converts to Size loss at season Accounting at 1:1 ratio (rounded down). Friendly territory traversal does NOT contribute`. `settlement_layer › §5.1` Bypass `Military > Defense by 2+` and supply-line attack `Military vs Ob 1 each season: success = invader takes −1 Discipline on all units in the province` — a third bypass-cost rule (**CT-61**). Pathfinding (`§2`): A* over terrain-cost field; cached routes rendered as roads; invalidation on `(a) settlement on the path sieged or hostile-occupied; (b) radiation band changes; (c) bridge collapses; (d) winter closure`; UI offers alternates `within +20% cost`.

**F-05.10 Vision & reconnaissance** (`march_layer › §3`): `effective_vision = base_vision × terrain_factor × weather_factor × season_factor`; `Base 240 px`; fog: `last-known state (faction control, garrison strength, Prosperity tier) persists`; `Hostile army positions in fog-of-war are HIDDEN; faction Intelligence (Investigate Strategic vs Ob 3) can reveal partial state`; Scouting `consuming half the march budget … full vision of all settlements within 2 hops … reveals army composition (size, tier mix) but NOT faction priorities or arc-state`; Counter-recon `(Intel investment ≥ 3 in territory) imposes +1 Ob on enemy scouting actions and reduces scouted vision range by 1 hop … only on contested-roll success` (the contested roll is unspecified — **GP-05.05**); Thread-Witnessed scouting `TS ≥ 50 … +1 Knot strain per observation … does NOT reveal hostile army positions`. Factor tables absent (GP-05.01). → TL-18.

**F-05.11 Gates.** `The two Askeheim Gates (Gate 1 in T13, Gate 2 in T6) are marsh-edge passage points … Each gate edge imposes attacker −1D … Gate edges are also the only land-route into T15 … Holding a gate confers +1 IP per season` (`march_layer › §7.3`) — CT-26 (IP per holder). T15 `cannot be controlled` (TL-09) yet is a march destination.

**F-05.12 Naval.** `march_layer › §6` headings only (`§6.1 Fleet composition … §6.5 Coastal-edge traversal`), empty (**GP-05.06**); `Lions' Helm can deny T10 sea access to Schoenland at IP < 75 if Crown requests` (`institutions › Löwenritter — Structure`) — T10 has no sea edge (GP-05.07). `Coastal (naval) | Attacker loses Fort-level bonus` (`settlement_adjacency › §2.2`).

**F-05.13 Fortification.** `Fort max 4` (T3, T14); `Fortify | Fort level + 1` Ob (`core`); `defending faction adds Fort level as bonus dice to defensive Military roll. Fort 3 (Ehrenfeld) = defender rolls Military + 3D` (`geography › Varfell Territorial Expansion Constraint (PP-191)`); `Tribune Sabotage, Ob = Fort level`; `Fort level is retained on the territory card` after elimination (`core › Faction Elimination`). **CT-31**: `T4 has Fort 2: +2D` (`strategic_layer › Scenario C`) vs `T4 … Fort 0` (`geography`); `Lowenskyst Fortress (S-006, Defense 4)` (`settlement_layer › §5.1`) vs T3 Fort 3 — settlement Defense and territory Fort are distinct scales with no mapping (**GP-05.08**).

**F-05.14 Implementation constants** (`march_layer › §8`): `1920×2880 ÷ 32 = 60×90 hex grid at full zoom … Strategic-to-tactical zoom = 4×`; A* precompute `~60×90 cells; cache shortest paths for 36×36 settlement pairs (≤ 630 unordered pairs)`; Godot 4.6 named. [ASPIRATION / implementation note, not a rule.]

**F-05.15 T15 / T16 special status.** `No control token can be placed in T15. No Govern, Trade, Muster, Fortify, or Decree orders execute in T15. CI 75 Church Territorial Seizure does not target T15. … Warden Cooperation track advances through successful Expedition seasons in T15, not through territorial control.` (`geography › What "No Faction Control" Means in BG`); `T15 Askeheim: no Accord. T16 Schoenland: not in play.` (`tracks › Accord`); `Should the Calamity zone heal sufficiently, settlements may emerge there and the territory may be assigned to a duchy` (`valoria_political_hierarchy › §1.2`). → TL-09 (violations: Vanguard from T16; Phase 2 corridor; Schoenland in PV table).

**Gaps.** GP-05.01 graph/terrain/vision YAML absent. GP-05.02 T12 Niflhel special stale. GP-05.03 radiation bands. GP-05.04 unit tiers/Size/distance data. GP-05.05 counter-recon contested roll. GP-05.06 naval empty. GP-05.07 T10 sea access. GP-05.08 Fort ↔ Defense mapping. GP-05.09 "Q4 and Q1" seasons vs Spring/Winter naming. GP-05.10 which settlement an army "starts" in at game start is unstated. GP-05.11 "Size" of Vanguard (3) vs army Size units. GP-05.12 T5/T11 `+1 Pros/season uncontested` — "uncontested" undefined.

### SET-05 Pipelines

**PL-05.01 Per-season march (march_layer form).** Entry: army with budget D-05.04 at a settlement. Steps: choose destination → A* route (cached) → traverse edges in order, each costing D-05.05 → at each settlement: stop if budget exhausted / engagement forced (D-05.07a) / player declines → inter-faction edge crossing → CB check (SET-14 PL-14.06) → on arrival at hostile settlement declare ENGAGEMENT (defending army present) or SIEGE (garrison only ⇒ SIEGE forced) — locked → battle per mass_battle (absent) with D-05.11/D-05.12 modifiers, or siege per SET-07 PL-07.09. Accounting: attrition D-05.08 → Size loss. Terminal: army at a settlement node. Reversible: no mid-edge; retreat is a next-season march.

**PL-05.02 Per-season march (adjacency form).** Entry: army. Steps: up to `Military ÷ 2` edges; each inter-province crossing → CB check; may Bypass; must engage or bypass the settlement entered (`§3 Invasion Sequencing`). Terminal: position. (CT-62 with PL-05.01.)

**PL-05.03 Route invalidation.** Entry: any of siege/occupation, radiation band change, bridge destroyed, winter closure. Steps: recompute only paths through the invalidated cell. Terminal: cache consistent.

**PL-05.04 Scouting.** Entry: army spends half its budget. Steps: reveal 2-hop settlements & army composition; if enemy counter-recon (Intel ≥ 3 in territory): +1 Ob (roll unspecified GP-05.05), −1 hop; defender learns only on contested-roll success. Terminal: fog state updated.

**PL-05.05 Vanguard route** — see SET-03 PL-03.03 (route violates P-05.02, CT-32).

**PL-05.06 Graph generation (PP-723).** Entry: territory adjacency + settlement registry. Steps: D-05.02 hubs → intra-province star edges → D-05.03 hub-to-hub → overrides → thread-witnessed edges. Terminal: 49-edge graph (superseded by a 56-edge PP-726 rebuild whose rule is not stated — GP-05.14).

**PL-05.07 S-ID migration.** Entry: any document referencing old S-001..S-036. Steps: map via `settlement_layer › §2.3` table (promote/demote/retain); non-substrate documents migrate "lazily". Terminal: new IDs. Unexecuted in five live documents (CT-17).

### SET-05 Throughlines applied
TL-09 (T15/T16 out of play) — asserted `geography`, `tracks`, `ci_seizure`; violated by Vanguard-from-T16, Phase 2 corridor, gates as "march-targets" into T15, PV for T16 in `core` (1) and `ci_political` (—). TL-11 (settlement = siege target, sub-features not siegeable, ≥2 connections) — asserted PP-726; violated by every old-ID rule that treats a district as a controlled settlement (CT-17). TL-14 — "territory" ≡ "province" documented; `T#` deprecation vs retention (CT-18). TL-18 (fog) — asserted `march_layer › §3.1`, `strategic_layer › §9.2`. TL-12 — `march_layer` is engine-authored throughout; no GM. TL-17 — `Q4 and Q1` (GP-05.09).

## SET-06 — Settlement Layer & Governance

### SET-06 Flattened statement

**F-06.01 Two tiers.** `The existing 17 territories become provinces — the strategic layer. Each province contains 1–3 settlements — the personal layer. … Provinces are not changed. All existing province-level mechanics (Accord, Piety Track, TCV, Fort Level, Calamity radiation, adjacency) continue to operate at the province level.` (`settlement_layer › §1.1`). Settlement types with natural manager (`§1.2`): `Seat | Province-controlling faction | Prosperity, Defense, Population; City | … or Guilds | Prosperity, Population, Trade; Town | … | Prosperity, Population; Fortress | … or Löwenritter | Defense, Garrison Capacity; Port | … or Guilds | Prosperity, Trade, Naval; Cathedral | Church (regardless of province controller) | Piety Influence, Population; Mine | … or Guilds | Prosperity (raw), Population (low); Outpost | … or Wardens | Defense (low), Special`. "Population", "Trade", "Naval", "Garrison Capacity", "Piety Influence" are named stats with no range or rule (**GP-06.01**; Population is "left unscaled (§9 PENDING)" per `§1.8`). `Village` appears in the registry and in `base(Type)` but not in the type table (**GP-06.02**).

**F-06.02 Settlement stats.** `Each settlement has 3 stats tracked on a 0–5 scale: Prosperity … Defense … Order` (`§1.3`). `Defense 0 = undefended (auto-capture). Defense 5 = major fortress.` `Order 0 = local revolt. Order 3+ = stable governance. Feeds upward into province Accord.` Derived meters: `Prosperity | Local Economy | Prosperity × 50 | Gold income contribution to faction Treasury; Defense | Garrison Strength | Defense × 20 + Fort Level × 30; Order | Public Order | Order × 20 | … below 0 triggers riot events`; `Stat damage (Prosperity −1) only occurs when a derived value hits 0 and stays through Accounting`. **Province Accord derivation:** `floor of the average Order across all settlements in the province … floor((4+2+1)/3) = floor(2.33) = 2`; `Existing Accord change rules … now operate by modifying settlement Order values`; edge cases `single-settlement: Accord = that settlement's Order. Multi-settlement: floor-average; ties broken by Seat getting +1 weight` (`§9 ED-SETT-03`) — "ties" in a floor-average is undefined (**GP-06.03**). Starting Prosperity/Defense/Order: `DEFERRED … Values stand PROVISIONAL` and none are given anywhere (**GP-06.04**). → CT-16 (Accord as direct attribute elsewhere), CT-49 (`Treasury income = Σ settlement Prosperity × 10` in `§1.8` vs `Prosperity × 50`).

**F-06.03 Facility tiers (PP-661).** Slots by type (`§1.4.1`): `Seat | Wing 3 | Suite 5 | Chamber 8 | Billet unlimited; City | 1 | 3 | 5 | unlimited; Town | 0 | 1 | 3; Fortress | 0 | 1 | 4; Cathedral | 1 | 3 | 5; Port | 0 | 1 | 3; Mine | 0 | 0 | 1; Outpost | 0 | 0 | 1`. Rules: `At Seat settlements, the faction leader occupies one Wing automatically`; `Player advancement to Standing 6+ requires an available Wing slot. If none available, advancement is "pending"`; capacity pressure options: `1. Existing Wing-holder departs … 2. Expand Institutional Capacity (Treasury −300 …, +1 Wing added; cap: +1 Wing per settlement per decade) 3. Prince-in-Waiting provisional rank — … Each season without Wing, player makes social contest (Disposition pool vs Ob 2) … Failure reverts to Standing 5.` Cross-faction: `Wing slots belong to the settlement's direct controller, not the province's controller … Cross-faction Wings may be ceded as treaty concessions`. `FacilityTier_s (0–3) — … highest facility tier present (0 billets only / 1 Chambers / 2 Suites / 3 Wings)` (`§1.8`) — whether a settlement's tier is its *capacity* or its *occupied* tier is not stated (**GP-06.05**).

**F-06.04 Settlement Legitimacy & Popular Support → Mandate (LPS-2e, Jordan 2026-05-30)** (`settlement_layer › §1.8`), verbatim core:
- `Legitimacy (L), 0–7 … Popular Support (PS), 0–7` per settlement for its controlling faction; `distinct from settlement Order`.
- `W_s = base(Type) + Prosperity_s + FacilityTier_s`; `base(Type) — Seat 3, City 3, Cathedral 3, Town 2, Fortress 2, Port 2, Village 1, Mine 1, Outpost 1`; `Range: undeveloped Outpost W=1 → fully-developed Seat W=11`.
- `q_s = 0.5·L_s + 0.5·PS_s (0–7)`, `T = Σ_s W_s · (q_s / 7)`; `Mandate = clamp( round( 7 · T / (T + K) ), 0, 7 ), with K = 6`.
- `aggregate_L = Σ W_s·L_s / Σ W_s`, `aggregate_PS = Σ W_s·PS_s / Σ W_s`.
- Cases: `A faction controlling zero settlements has T=0 → Mandate 0. An embedded faction (Löwenritter pre-coup) acts through its host (Mandate N/A until it holds settlements). Restoration is territoryless and operates at the community level via Presence markers, so its L/PS live in its Presence localities … and T is summed over those localities.`
- Feedback: `a settlement whose q_s sits ≥1 below Mandate gets L +1 (capped 7); ≥1 above gets PS −1 (capped 0); at most ±1 per settlement per season, within the ±2 faction-stat seasonal cap`.
- `Faction-level mission outcomes (PP-686 …) now apply their ΔL/ΔPS to the faction's controlled settlements (uniformly, clamped 0–7)`.
- Seed: `per-settlement L and PS seed from the controlling faction's prior authored value` (`faction_behavior › §4`).
- Consumers: strictness reads aggregates; `derived_stats_v30's faction Legitimacy meter (= Mandate × 20)`; Parliament votes = Mandate.
**CT-07**: `Mandate = round(0.5 × Legitimacy + 0.5 × Popular_Support)` (`tracks.md` header note; `core` PP-686 note) is a different function. **CT-08**: faction-level L/PS values remain the operative state in `ministry.md` (`L 3 … PS not tracked`), `insurgency › §3.2, §4.2` (`Legitimacy 1.0 Float`; Latent RM `Mandate = Count of territories with PT ≤ 1`), `settlement_layer › §6.2 Founded Faction Starting Stats` (`Legitimacy (L) 2; Popular Support (PS) 3`), `core › Faction Starting Stats` (`Legitimacy | Popular_Support` columns), and every "Mandate −1 / L −1" effect that targets a faction rather than a settlement. Mandate Recovery per settlement: `A controlled settlement with L < its seed value recovers +1 L/season AND with PS < its seed value recovers +1 PS/season independently when: No hostile Domain Action targeting that faction this season; Stability ≥ 2. Cap: … starting value [PROVISIONAL]` (`stats › Mandate Recovery (ED-066b — REVISED by LPS-2e)`); also `Govern Overwhelming in own capital: L +1 (max once/season, max to faction starting L)` (`parliament › Mandate Recovery (PP-174)`).

**F-06.05 Dual authority and governors.** `Provincial Authority | The national-level faction controlling the province | Military deployment, taxation, legal framework, Domain Actions targeting the province; Settlement Governor | An officer (NPC or player) or subnational faction | Local governance (Order), economic development (Prosperity), local NPC relationships, settlement-level scene generation` (`§3.1`). Eligibility (`§3.2`): `0–2 | Cannot be assigned; 3 (Counselor) | one Town or Outpost; 4 (Lieutenant) | one City, Fortress, or Mine; 5 (Successor) | a Seat or Cathedral (requires faction leader approval)` (CT-19: title "Successor" vs "Senior"). Player governor: `one mandatory governance action (no scene action cost) plus their normal scene actions`; companion-governor `1 free action per season — social OR governance`. Governance actions: `Develop | Cognition + Wealth-relevant History | floor(Prosperity/2) + 1 | Prosperity +1 (cap: settlement type max); Fortify | Military-relevant stat + History | floor(Defense/2) + 1 | Defense +1 (cap: settlement type max); Pacify | Charisma + local History | floor((3 − Order) + 1), min 1 | Order +1 (cap: 5); Administer | Attunement + Governance History | 2 | Maintain Order (no decay this season). Reveals one local NPC's active Conviction.` "settlement type max" values are not given (**GP-06.06**); `ED-SETT-02 … DEFERRED — proposed pool = governor's primary stat; Ob 2 standard / 3 recovery / 4 crisis` is a competing (deferred) parameterisation. NPC governor: `always prioritize Order ≥ 2 …, then Prosperity development, then Defense only if threatened`; `settlement-specific (Pacify → Develop → Fortify → Administer); faction-tree override at Stability ≤ 2` (`§9 ED-SETT-06`). Bishop-Governor per SET-04. Stature ladder (`§6.1`): `Renown 3–4 | Standing 3 | Settlement Governor. Manage one Town/Outpost. Free governance action per season; 5–6 | 4 | Multi-Settlement. Manage up to 3 settlements …; 7–8 | 4–5 | Provincial Authority. De facto province controller …; 9–10 | 5 | National Actor` — this table binds governance scope to Renown while `§3.2` binds it to Standing (both stated; no precedence — **GP-06.07**). **CT-20**: `governance_play_redesign › §1.2` [PROPOSAL] `The old "one mandatory free governance action" is removed. Instead the governor has an AP budget`. Order decay: implied by Administer ("no decay this season") and `Ministry … Order decay −1` but no baseline decay rate is stated (**GP-06.08**).

**F-06.06 Subnational management** (`§3.3` table, verbatim effects): `Church | Cathedral | +1 Piety Influence per season. Church governor uses Church stats … Province faction retains taxation rights.; Guilds | City, Port, Market, Mine | +1 Trade per season. … Wealth as primary stat …; Ministry | Seat, City | Order decay −1 … Influence as primary stat.; Löwenritter | Fortress | Defense +1 passive … Military as primary stat …; RM | Outpost, Town (in low-CV territories) | CV −1 potential per season … Only … PT ≤ 2. Province faction may not know RM is managing (covert); Wardens | Outpost (Askeheim, Stillhelm Watch) | RS effects detected 1 band earlier …; Niflhel | Any (covert) | … Intel-gathering at +1D … Detection … Evidence Track threshold 3.` (Niflhel row survives dissolution — **GP-06.09**). `RM Cell Resilience: If RM has Presence markers in ≥ 3 settlements within a province, Church/Crown suppression actions against RM in that province take +1 Ob.` `Granting management: … Domain Action (Influence, Ob 1)`; `Revoking: … Domain Action (Influence, Ob = subnational faction's Influence ÷ 2, round up). Revocation costs Order −1 in the settlement … and Disposition −2 with the subnational faction's leadership.`; `Contested management: … social contest (per social_contest_v30 §7 …)` (procedure absent).

**F-06.07 Settlement as gamespace.** `Every Scene Slate entry … is now anchored to a specific settlement`; `Travel within a province: … no scene action … between provinces costs 1 scene per province traversed` (`§4.1`); Depth Axis per settlement; Exposure per province. Visibility table (`§4.2`) — narrative. Settlement events (`§4.3`, `0–1 local events per season`): `Prosperity 0 | Famine … Order −1 automatic; Defense 0 + adjacent hostile military | Raid or siege. Mandatory scene if player present; Order 0 | Local revolt … Governor expelled unless garrison present; Order 5 + Prosperity 4+ | Flourishing … +1 Disposition with all local NPCs; RM takes control | Governance Transition scene … Disestablishment (−1 Order for 2 seasons, then Accord growth +0.5/season; PT −1 immediate); Accommodation (no penalty, PT drops 0.5 only, standard Accord); Transformation (4-season …; PT −1 and Accord +0.5/season after completion); RM-governed, emergency action | Consensus Delay … +1 season to resolve. Waivable: RM leader spends 1 Mandate + loses 1 Presence marker; Cathedral + CV change | Religious event; Mine + Prosperity 3+ | Province Treasury +50/season at Accounting; Fortress + hostile military in province | Defense pool vs Ob 2. Success: holds. Failure: attacker bypasses or captures.` (fractional Accord "+0.5" against an integer 0–3 Accord — **GP-06.10**). Local Actors (`§4.5`): `1–2 … Seat: 2. City: 2. Town: 1. Fortress: 1. Port: 2. Cathedral: 1. Mine: 1. Outpost: 0. Total: ~45–50`; profile `Name, Role (Elder/Magistrate/Merchant/Priest/Artisan/Farmer/Fisher/Miner/Scholar/Healer), one Conviction, Disposition (starting +1 toward governor, 0 toward all others)`; drivers `Player governs, Order improves: +1. Order declines: −1. Sponsor event: +1. Public combat in settlement: −2. Defend settlement from invasion: +2. Settlement changes controller: reset to 0. Player fulfills Conviction relevant to settlement: +1.`; `Recruitment pool for faction emergence (Stage 2→3 at Disposition +3)`. POI templates by type × Depth 0–3 (`§4.6`, 2–4 POIs each).

**F-06.08 Niflhel-dissolution phenomena** (`§4.7–4.9`; `conflict_architecture › Niflhel Dissolution`): Black market `Automatic when settlement Order ≤ 1 OR settlement has no governor … Disappearance: Automatic when settlement Order ≥ 3 … Settlement Wealth +0.5 … Settlement Accord −0.5` (settlements have Order not Accord; "Settlement Wealth" undefined — CT-16/**GP-06.11**). Intelligence Brokers `One broker per settlement with Prosperity ≥ 3 and no governor or governor Stability ≤ 2. Named NPCs with Dispositions … Sell faction intelligence; fabricate intel when peace reduces demand … can be killed, bought out, or turned` ("governor Stability" undefined — **GP-06.12**; fabrication rule absent, superseded 8-card version had `one attribute value is exaggerated by +2 or −2`). Thread Exploitation Sites `Any settlement with Thread Proximity ≤ 2 … RS −0.5 per harvest per season. Wealth +1 for harvesting faction. Discovery: Requires fieldwork (Investigation action)`.

**F-06.09 Church infrastructure & Parish services** — SET-04 F-04.06. `The Geneva trap` [ASPIRATION].

**F-06.10 Starting friction points** (`conflict_architecture › Starting Friction Points`): `T1: S003 Valorsplatz Cathedral is Church-held … Every Accounting, Crown runs a fragmentation check (Influence 5 vs Ob 3)`; `T8: S017 Market Quarter (Prosperity 5 …) is Guild-held`; `T14: S014 Barracks is Löwenritter-held`; `T4: S029 Lodge is RM's covert foothold`; `T13: S032 Shrine is RM's overt presence`. `Each of these fires a fragmentation check every Accounting from S1.` Under PP-726 all five are sub-features, not settlements (CT-17 / TL-11); the fragmentation check as specified in `fractional › §2.6` needs a non-Seat-held *settlement*.

**F-06.11 Player governance stakes.** `Players at Standing ≥ 3 who hold governance positions risk Renown from governance failures. Accord drops in governed territory, faction Treasury reaching 0, or battle losses in governed territory each cost Renown −1 (cap: −2/season)` (`player_agency › §5.4`). Resources `Settlement governance (+1/season per settlement with Prosperity ≥ 3)` (`player_agency › §9`).

**F-06.12 Settlement succession.** `When a settlement governor dies, is removed, or departs, the province faction must assign a new governor. If no eligible NPC or player is available, the settlement becomes unmanaged (Order −1 per season until a governor is assigned).` (`§7.2`). `Settlement governance transfers to designated protégé automatically` (`player_agency › §10`).

**F-06.13 Military at settlement scale** — SET-07 F-07.09 (Assault/Siege/Bypass/garrison).

**F-06.14 Governance play redesign** [PROPOSAL, all of `governance_play_redesign_v1.md`]: `AP = 2 + FacilityTier_s (… 0–3) → 2–5 AP/season. Standing 5 governors (Seat/Cathedral) get +1. AP do not carry over.`; companion-governors `fixed 2 AP`. Verbs (AP / roll / effect / method choice): Develop `2 | Cognition + Wealth-history vs Ob ⌊Prosperity/2⌋+1 | Prosperity +1 | Treasury / Guild charter (+1 Prosperity faster, Guild Influence +1) / Corvée (Order −1)`; Fortify `2 | Military + history vs Ob ⌊Defense/2⌋+1 | Defense +1 | Garrison / Militia (PS +1, brittle) / Walls`; Keep Order `1–2 | varies | Order +1 | Consent (Charisma, 2 AP, PS +1) / Force (Military, 1 AP, PS −1, Local-Actor Disposition −1) / Clergy (1 AP, Church infra creeps)`; Hold Court `1 | Charisma/Cognition + Governance-history vs Ob set by dispute | adjudicate; sets Precedent (±1 Ob)`; Sponsor `1–2 | auto / Wealth | durable +1 to a stat + Disposition; Debt tag (skip next year → Disposition −2)`; Treat `1 | Influence + history vs subnational leader | minor side-deal`; Levy `1 | auto | extract; L/PS −1 and/or Order −1`; Investigate `1–2 | Cognition + history vs concealment | expose / expel / co-opt / shelter`. Directive: `Comply | — | Standing +, trust + | strains settlement; Bargain | social contest vs PA | soften; Defy / Divert | — | Standing-debt (FAC-02), suspicion track +1; at threshold → recall, audit, or replacement | Local-Actor Disposition +, PS +`. Ledger tags `Precedent | Grudge | Debt | Reputation (Just / Harsh / Generous / Weak / Hated)` persist across succession. Pressure `Π_next = clamp( Π + Σ(unserved Needs) + Σ(active Grudges) + Σ(NPC ambitions in motion) + external_shock − Σ(player releases this season), 0, 10 )`; bands `0–2 Opportunity/Ambition; 3–7 Petitions/Frictions/Intrigues; 8–10 Crisis`; draw `1 + ⌊Π/3⌋ cards`; card schema (family, triggers, weight, cooldown, excludes, the_ask, responses, follow_on); families `Petition | Friction | Opportunity | Crisis | Intrigue | Ambition | Thread`; NPC dossier adds `ambition {goal, method ∈ {lawful, factional, violent, covert}, timeline, progress 0–5}` and `trajectory`; `every significant NPC advances their ambition by 1` per Accounting; prerequisites `G1 … no settlement registry (settlement.py is 1:1 territory→settlement)`. Holes: Directive `or the GM` (**H-11**), cards `GM-/sim-authorable` (**H-12**), "Ob set by dispute" (**H-13**), suspicion threshold unnumbered (**GP-06.13**).

**Gaps.** GP-06.01 named-but-unscaled stats; GP-06.02 Village type; GP-06.03 floor-average "ties"; GP-06.04 starting P/D/O; GP-06.05 FacilityTier capacity vs occupancy; GP-06.06 type max caps; GP-06.07 Renown vs Standing governance scope; GP-06.08 Order decay baseline; GP-06.09 Niflhel row; GP-06.10 fractional Accord; GP-06.11 settlement Wealth/Accord; GP-06.12 governor Stability; GP-06.13 suspicion thresholds; GP-06.14 "Treasury" as a faction resource (−300, +50) with no conversion to Wealth 0–7; GP-06.15 which settlements a faction controls at start beyond the five friction points (all others assumed = province controller [INFERRED]).

### SET-06 Pipelines

**PL-06.01 Settlement-layer seasonal resolution.** Entry: season start (first of three scales). Steps: infrastructure actions (Church building, Guild trade, Crown ministerial placement, RM organizing, governor appointments incl. bishops) → settlement Order/Prosperity update → black markets emerge/resolve (D-06.17) → governor authority checks (undefined — **GP-06.19**). Terminal: settlement state for province layer. (`phases › Three-Scale Resolution Model`.)

**PL-06.02 Governor assignment.** Entry: settlement governor slot empty or province faction chooses. Steps: eligible entity by D-06.21 (Seat/Cathedral need leader approval) → assigned; if none eligible → unmanaged (Order −1/season) → reassign. Branches: RM/Church/subnational grant instead (PL-06.04). Terminal: governor in place.

**PL-06.03 Seasonal governance action.** Entry: player governor's season. Steps: choose one of Develop/Fortify/Pacify/Administer → roll pool vs D-06.14 → Success effect (+1 stat capped; Administer no decay + reveal Conviction) → Renown risk D-06.23 at Accounting. Companion: one free action social OR governance. Terminal: stat updated. [PROPOSAL alternative PL-06.10.]

**PL-06.04 Grant / revoke / contest management.** Entry: province faction decision or subnational claim. Steps: grant `Influence, Ob 1` → subnational governor replaces faction governor, effects per F-06.06; revoke `Influence, Ob = subnational Influence ÷ 2` → Order −1, Disposition −2; conflict → social contest (procedure absent). Terminal: management state.

**PL-06.05 Mandate aggregation & feedback (each Accounting).** Entry: Accounting. Steps: compute W_s, q_s, T, Mandate (D-06.05–08); apply mission ΔL/ΔPS uniformly; apply recovery (D-06.20); apply feedback (D-06.10); recompute aggregates for strictness. Order among these four sub-steps unstated (**GP-06.20**). Terminal: Mandate for the season.

**PL-06.06 Settlement collapse (Order 0).** Entry: Order reaches 0. Steps: local revolt event; governor expelled unless garrison present; black market emerges; province Accord recomputed; RM Settlement Emergence eligible (SET-09 PL-09.09) if PT ≤ 1 and Disposition ≥ +3. Terminal: revolt state until Order raised.

**PL-06.07 RM governance transition.** Entry: RM takes control of a settlement. Steps: choose Disestablishment / Accommodation / Transformation (F-06.07 effects); Consensus Delay applies to emergency DAs thereafter. Terminal: RM-governed settlement.

**PL-06.08 Capacity pressure.** Entry: player reaches Standing 6 with no free Wing. Steps: pending → (a) wait for departure; (b) Expand Institutional Capacity (Treasury −300, +1 Wing, once per decade); (c) Prince-in-Waiting: each season Disposition pool vs Ob 2, failure → Standing 5. Terminal: Wing occupied or reverted.

**PL-06.09 Unmanaged decay.** Entry: governor slot empties. Steps: Order −1 per season until assigned; black market at Order ≤ 1; Pastoral Assumption available if Chapel+. Terminal: governor assigned.

**PL-06.10 Governor's Turn [PROPOSAL].** Entry: season open. Steps: Π recompute → deck draw `1 + ⌊Π/3⌋` → NPC ambitions +1 (threshold → Ambition card) → Directive issued → governor responds (Comply/Bargain/Defy) → spend AP on verbs (each writes Ledger tag) → Personal Phase → Accounting. Terminal: next season's trigger predicates. Reversible: no; Ledger persists across succession.

### SET-06 Throughlines applied
TL-05 (Mandate derived from settlement L/PS) — asserted `settlement_layer › §1.8`, `faction_behavior › §4`, `stats` header; violated by every faction-level L/PS/Mandate state or effect (CT-08). TL-11 — asserted PP-726; violated by friction points and old IDs (CT-17). TL-04 — Accord now floor(mean Order): the direct-Accord rules in SET-07 are not rewritten (CT-16). TL-07 — feedback "within the ±2 faction-stat seasonal cap" asserted. TL-12 — H-11, H-12, H-13 (all [PROPOSAL]). TL-03 — settlement layer first (asserted).

## SET-07 — Province Control, Accord, Fracturing & Transfer

### SET-07 Flattened statement

**F-07.01 Accord (per territory, 0–3).** `Population acceptance of current controller. Modifies effective Prosperity and TCV.` Table (`tracks.md › Accord (Per-Territory, 0–3) — PP-645`; identical in `core`): `3 | Aligned | Base (full) | Defender +1D in Battle.; 2 | Compliant | Base (full) | Normal operations.; 1 | Resistant | 0 | Govern Ob +1. Garrison required (≥ 1 unit) or Accord → 0 at Accounting.; 0 | Revolt | N/A | Territory becomes Uncontrolled. Garrison fights Popular Uprising (Military vs Ob 2).` `TCV counts only at Accord ≥ 2. Military conquest → Accord 1. Non-military acquisition → Accord 2+.` `Starting Accord: Faction capitals = 3. Home territories = 2. T15 Askeheim: no Accord. T16 Schoenland: not in play.` Accounting 4c (`phases`): `(i) Each territory at Accord 1 without garrison … Accord → 0. (ii) Each territory at Accord 0: Revolt — garrison fights Popular Uprising (Military vs Ob 2) or retreats; territory becomes Uncontrolled; Turmoil +1. (iii) Passive normalisation: each territory with garrison AND no hostile action for 2 consecutive seasons: Accord +1 (cap 2).` Govern: `Success | Accord +1 in target territory (max 3); OR Mandate recovery +1 if in capital …; Overwhelming | Both` (`ci_political › §4.2`). Strain effects (SET-03 D-03.14). Battle: `Attacker conquest: territory Accord → 1. Defender territory: Accord −1.` (`strategic_layer › PP-647`) vs `Accord drop: now applies to the settlement's Order (Order −1), not the province's Accord` (`settlement_adjacency › §2.3`) — **CT-16** (Accord is a directly-mutated territory attribute in `tracks`/`core`/`phases`/`ci_political`/`strategic_layer`/`parliamentary_transfer`/`treaty` and a derived floor-average of settlement Order in `settlement_layer`/`fractional`/`settlement_adjacency`; no document reconciles "Accord +1" against a derived value). Martial Governance `Accord +1 (cap 2)` (`core`). → TL-04.

**F-07.02 Effective Prosperity.** Accord 1 → `Effective Prosperity 0`; ≥ 2 → `Base (full)` (`tracks`). "Effective Prosperity" is then used by `conflict_architecture` ("Province PV derives from the prosperity of aligned settlements") and `settlement_layer › §1.3` ("Contributes to province Effective Prosperity") without a formula (**GP-07.01**).

**F-07.03 Province control transfer paths.** (a) Seat capture: `When the Seat is captured, provincial control transfers to the invader. Non-Seat settlements with Order ≥ 3 may resist (maintaining their current governor). The invader must reduce each resistant settlement individually or grant them autonomy` (`settlement_layer › §5.1`); superseded as *binary* by `fractional_province_ownership` (`Supersedes: Binary province-transfer rule`). (b) Uncontrolled entry: `any faction may March in freely (no Battle roll for entry, no defender)` (`core › Faction Elimination`). (c) Parliamentary Transfer (F-07.07). (d) Church seizure (SET-04). (e) Consolidation (F-07.05). (f) Treaty-based re-merge, Submit (F-07.05). (g) Löwenritter Split: `T14 becomes Löwenritter territory` (SET-09). (h) Succession split geographic division (SET-09). (i) Secession (F-07.06). "Territorial transfer" in `phases › Three-Scale` is the generic step.

**F-07.04 Fractionalization (PP-666).** `A province becomes fractional when: ≥ 1 settlement in the province is controlled by a faction that is NOT the nominal province controller, AND The provincial Seat is NOT controlled by that same faction … If the Seat changes hands, the province's nominal controller changes — it is not fractional, just transferred.` (`fractional › §2.1`). PV share: `Settlement PV share = (settlement Prosperity / sum of all settlement Prosperities in province) × province base PV` … `rounded to 1 decimal` (`§2.2`; `§5` says "currently 2-decimal" — minor internal inconsistency). `Zero-denominator guard (FRAC-01): If sum of all settlement Prosperities … reaches 0 …: PV distributes equally across settlements (floor(base PV / settlement count), remainder to Seat settlement). Province is treated as Economically Collapsed — Accord forced to 0 at next Accounting regardless of Order values.` Naming (`§2.3`): `Greater [Province] = the faction holding the Seat settlement …; Lesser [Province] = … Northern/Southern/Eastern/Western/Outer [Province]` by settlement position (no coordinates in corpus — **GP-07.02**). Effects (`§2.5`): Accord `floor-average of Order across all settlements`; `PT applies per-settlement. Province PT = floor-average`; `The faction holding the Seat issues province-level Domain Actions. Non-Seat-holders issue only settlement-level actions`; radiation whole-province; movement per graph; `Each faction taxes the settlements it controls. Province-level tax bonuses (Trade Network, Mine Income) scale by PV share`; `Each faction's PV total includes fractional shares`. **CT-45**: `valoria_political_hierarchy › §2.3`: `A province whose constituent territories are aligned to different factions is canonically fractured into smaller sub-provinces named northern/southern or western/eastern … Each sub-province retains the 1–3 territories rule and is treated as a full province for political-value purposes … Reunification is symmetric: when all sub-provinces of a fractured province return to common faction-alignment, they merge back` — no Seat condition, no 75% Consolidation, no Fragmentation Check, symmetric reunification; and its `§5` admits `specific event triggers and reunification thresholds not yet specified`. **CT-44** political value: `political_value(faction) = Σ over owned-territories(territory_value) + Σ over fully-owned provinces(province_unification_bonus)` with `Specific scalar values are TBD` (`valoria_political_hierarchy › §2.4`) vs Prosperity-share PV (`fractional`) vs fixed per-territory PV (`core`).

**F-07.05 Consolidation.** `A faction that holds ≥ 75% of a province's PV (weighted by settlement Prosperity share) may declare Consolidation as a Domain Action: Cost: Influence pool vs Ob = remaining non-faction-held PV share × 2 (round up). Effect: Province unifies under this faction. All non-faction-held settlements either: Submit: settlement transfers to consolidating faction. Order −2 in that settlement. No battle. Resist: settlement triggers mandatory siege or assault next season. Target choice: non-faction-held settlement's current controller chooses Submit or Resist within 1 season. Default on non-response: Resist.` (`fractional › §2.4`). **CT-60**: "PV share × 2" — the worked example uses absolute PV (`0.67 × 2 = 1.33 → Ob 2`), the text says "share". NPC choice rule for Submit/Resist absent (**H-14**).

**F-07.06 Fragmentation Check & Secession.** `At Accounting, each fractional province rolls a Fragmentation Check: Pool: Seat-holding faction's Influence; Ob: 2 + (number of non-Seat-held settlements)` (FRAC-03: additive regardless of faction count). `Overwhelming: Province stabilizes as fractional for 4 seasons (no further Fragmentation Check …). Success: … No change. Partial: A random non-Seat-held settlement's Order drops −1. Failure: A non-Seat-held settlement's controller may invoke Secession: the settlement becomes a de facto independent subnational holding. The original province controller loses that settlement's PV share permanently until reconquest.` `Secession candidates are limited to settlements controlled by a national faction (Crown, Hafenmark, Varfell, Church, Löwenritter, Guilds, Schoenland). … If all non-Seat alien settlements are subnational, the Failure outcome produces no Secession — treat as Partial` (`§2.6`). "may invoke" — NPC decision rule absent (**H-15**). `conflict_architecture` frames the same check as `Crown runs a fragmentation check (Influence 5 vs Ob 3)` at T1 and `Fragmentation checks at T14 Ob +1` under Löwenritter Restless. Dice-form only (CT-03).

**F-07.07 Parliamentary Territory Transfer (Pass 2h).** `Available to | All parliamentary factions …; Frequency | 1 per arc per faction; Prerequisite | Casus Belli against the territory holder; Pool | Proposer Influence; Ob | Holder Legitimacy + PARL_MAJORITY_OB_BONUS (default 2); Cost | Action slot + CB consumption (whether transfer succeeds or fails)` (`parliamentary_transfer › §1.1`). Outcomes: `Overwhelming | Territory transfers … Holder Legitimacy −1. Transferred territory Accord = 1.; Success | … Accord = 1.; Partial | Transfer fails … Proposer gains CB vs holder …; Failure | Transfer fails. Proposer Stability −1. Holder Legitimacy +1`. Protections: `Last-territory protection … blocked at the action-declaration stage`; `GD-3 extra-parliamentary block`; `Self-transfer block`. Modes (`§2`): Adversarial / Consensual (`Holder Legitimacy not damaged on Failure`) / Punishment (`no L+1 — instead Holder Standing −1`) / Appeasement (`Accord granted +2 instead of +1 on Success`) — `[PROVISIONAL …]`; **CT-58** (Appeasement "+2 instead of +1" vs base "Accord = 1"). Resolution wrapper (`§4`): declare (territory + mode + CB source) → `Vote contest opens … All parliamentary factions vote. Voting blocs computed by faction Standing relative to proposer + holder` (**H-16**: bloc algorithm absent; "Standing" here is the faction-level term, CT-57) → `Majority bloc favoring proposer: Pool +1D; … holder: Pool −1D; Split: no modifier` `[PROVISIONAL]` → `Pool-vs-Ob roll … Continuous Engine + degree table` → outcome → `Parliamentary Stay … may be invoked by any parliamentary faction post-vote, post-roll. Stay invocation suspends transfer pending external resolution … stay-lift mechanism per §10.1` (absent — **GP-07.03**). "Holder Legitimacy" is a faction-level L (CT-08). Validation: `Parliamentary Transfer fires ~2-4 times per campaign … Crown initiates ~60% … Adversarial ~75%; Consensual ~15%; Appeasement ~5%; Punishment ~5%`; `PARL_MAJORITY_OB_BONUS = 2 … Increasing to 3 reduces … success rate by ~40%`.

**F-07.08 Faction elimination & vacuum.** `When a faction is eliminated (Stability 0 and no recovery action taken): All territories previously held enter Political Vacuum for 1 season (PP-500). During Vacuum: no faction may March in; Fort level retained; tokens removed. After 1 season: … Uncontrolled … Ministry AP-tokens and Guilds CP-tokens … removed immediately. Löwenritter Autonomy: if Crown is eliminated, Löwenritter immediately advances to Split (Stage 4). T14 becomes Löwenritter territory. CI, IP, MS effects tied to eliminated faction's territory holdings: cease immediately` (`core › Faction Elimination — Territory Status (ED-333)`). `Stability 0 at Accounting end = faction eliminated. Units remain on board until Muster order removes them (holding ground, now Masterless). Other factions may Claim (Domain Action) Masterless units.` (`strategic_layer › P-24`). `On Faction Collapse, Mandate drops to 0 immediately … All OTHER attributes freeze` (`strategic_layer › I-04 / P-15`). **CT-46** with `settlement_layer › §6.3` (`When a national-level faction loses all its provinces, it does not vanish. It contracts.` … city-state; `Full collapse: If the faction leader is killed or captured and no successor exists with Standing 4+, the faction dissolves`), `baralta › §2` (`Crown faction is eliminated (Mandate 0 + Loyalty 0)`), `insurgency › §6.2` (`Legitimacy < 1.0 AND territorial count < 2`), `strategic_layer › §9.10` (`On faction reaching Stability 0: … Player may attempt to reconstitute the faction via Influence Domain Actions (Ob 4, multi-season)`; `Reconstitute: M = Influence − 6` in `stats`), and `faction_succession_split › §2.5` (`50% of frozen parent values` reconstitution per `faction_layer §1.5 Step 4`, absent). "no recovery action taken" is undefined (**GP-07.04**). Elimination trigger of Strain +2 (SET-03). **CT-67**: Church `anti-death-spiral floor — Stability cannot drop below 0, but at 0 the Church is functionally a subordinate institution for remainder of game` (`baralta › §3`) vs elimination at Stability 0.

**F-07.09 Settlement-scale military.** (`settlement_layer › §5.1–5.2`): `Assault | Military vs Defense + garrison | Success: settlement captured. Failure: attacker repelled, takes casualties.; Siege | Military ≥ Defense | … Each season: defender Order −1 (starvation/pressure). When Order = 0, settlement surrenders. Attacker cannot move … while besieging.; Bypass | Military > Defense by 2+ | … Bypassed settlement remains hostile — its garrison can attack the invader's supply line (Military vs Ob 1 each season: success = invader takes −1 Discipline on all units in the province).` `A Fortress settlement … cannot be bypassed unless the invader's Military exceeds the Fortress Defense by 3+. Lowenskyst Fortress (S-006, Defense 4) requires Military 7+ to bypass`. `Ungarrisoned settlements with Defense 0 are auto-captured on any hostile military entry`. `ED-SETT-04 | Siege duration 4 seasons for Order 4 Fortress. | RESOLVED — 4 seasons intended.` Settlement-scale battle (`settlement_adjacency › §2`): declaration `Assault, Siege, or Bypass`; `mass_battle_v30 Part B` resolves (absent — **GP-07.05**); consequences `MS −1 to −2 …; Strain +1 …; IP +2 …; Accord drop → settlement Order −1; Settlement Prosperity −1 on Assault outcome Partial or worse`; Siege `defender Order −1/season until Order = 0 → surrender`. **CT-61** (march_layer siege requires Heavy Infantry/Siege unit; ENGAGEMENT needs a defending army, SIEGE only vs garrison; no `Military ≥ Defense` gate; bypass cost via attrition). **CT-34** with territory-scale battle: `Battle Ob = floor(defender Military / 2) + 1. Attacker rolls: Military pool vs Ob` (`ci_seizure › Battle Ob Formula (PP-499)`; `Weight-of-numbers (PP-570): attacking with Military ≥ 2× defending … forces the defender to Stability −1 at Accounting`) vs `Both sides roll simultaneously. Compare net successes … use the DIFFERENCE as the effective surplus` (`strategic_layer › P-16`) plus `Drawn battle: On exactly tied net successes: … Both sides take Discipline −1 and hold position` (`PP-034`) plus `Fort … bonus dice` (`geography`). `Unit cap at Military: Number of units deployed cannot exceed current Military stat` (`strategic_layer › P-18`). `At Military 0, Muster actions produce no units and may not be taken` (`PP-039`).

**F-07.10 Occupation.** `Occupation (ongoing) | −1 [Wealth] per occupied territory per season | faction_layer_v30 §2` and `PV used for: victory threshold checks, occupation PV zeroing` (`ci_political › §4.1, §7.3`) — the occupation mechanic itself (faction_layer §2) is absent (**GP-07.06**).

**F-07.11 Victory scoring over territory — CT-28 / CT-18.** GD-1: `Peninsular Sovereignty (control 11+ of 15 territories)` (`core` header comment), `control 11/15 territories sustained 2 seasons, treaties counting` (`ci_political › §2.2`), `hold 11+ territories sustained 2 seasons` (`insurgency › §7`); `The Church still needs all 15 territories at Accord ≥ 2 for Peninsular Sovereignty` (`campaign_architecture › §1.3`); `Universal victory still requires Accord ≥ 2 in all provinces` (`settlement_layer › §8.1`); `Universal Victory Condition (Peninsular Sovereignty) re-scored with fractional PVs` (`fractional › §2.5`); `Political Stability … victory gate (≤ 6)` (`settlement_layer › §7.1`). Which 15 of the 17 (T15 and T16 excluded [INFERRED]), whether "control" counts fractional PV, Accord ≥ 2, or Seat-holding, and whether treaties count all coexist unresolved (**GP-07.07**).

**Gaps.** GP-07.01 Effective Prosperity formula. GP-07.02 settlement coordinates for directional names. GP-07.03 Parliamentary Stay lift. GP-07.04 "recovery action". GP-07.05 mass_battle resolution absent. GP-07.06 occupation rules absent. GP-07.07 victory denominator/measure. GP-07.08 Popular Uprising outcome on garrison loss/retreat. GP-07.09 who is "controller" of a fractional province for Strain/Accord effects. GP-07.10 Casus Belli check on inter-province movement — the "check" is a CB grant, not a roll (see SET-14).
