### SET-07 Pipelines

**PL-07.01 Fractionalization → Fragmentation → Consolidation/Secession.** Entry: a settlement changes controller (Bishop Appointment, assault, Secession, RM emergence, defection). Steps: evaluate D-07.04 → if fractional: rename (D-07.08), recompute PV shares (D-07.03), Accord/PT floor-averages; each Accounting: Fragmentation Check (Seat-holder Influence vs D-07.07): OW → 4-season stabilization; S → nothing; P → random non-Seat settlement Order −1; F → Secession may be invoked (H-15) → PV share lost "permanently until reconquest". Any faction at ≥ 75% may declare Consolidation (Influence vs D-07.06) → each other settlement's controller chooses Submit (transfer, Order −2) or Resist (mandatory siege/assault next season; default Resist) (H-14) → province unifies. Terminal: unified province or persistent fractional/secession. Reversible: yes via reconquest/Consolidation. (CT-45: PP-726's fracturing is a different state machine.)

**PL-07.02 Parliamentary Territory Transfer.** Entry: proposer holds CB vs holder; parliamentary status; not this arc yet; target not holder's last territory; not own. Steps: declare (territory, mode, CB) → Vote contest (all parliamentary factions; blocs by Standing; H-16) → modifier D-07.10 → roll Influence vs D-07.09 (dice / resolver — CT-03) → outcome (OW/S: transfer, Accord = 1 (+2 Appeasement — CT-58), holder L −1 on OW; P: proposer gains CB; F: proposer Stability −1, holder L +1 (Punishment: holder Standing −1 instead; Consensual: no L damage)) → CB consumed either way → optional Parliamentary Stay suspends pending external resolution (lift rule absent). Terminal: transferred / failed / stayed. Advanced by: proposer action, once per arc. Treaty partners: initiating Transfer against a treaty partner is a treaty violation (SET-14).

**PL-07.03 Accord checks (Accounting 4c).** Entry: Accounting. Steps: (i) Accord 1 & no garrison → 0; (ii) Accord 0 → Revolt: garrison fights Popular Uprising `Military vs Ob 2` or retreats (outcome of fight unspecified — GP-07.08); territory → Uncontrolled; Turmoil +1; (iii) garrison AND no hostile action 2 consecutive seasons → Accord +1 (cap 2). Terminal: Accord updated / territory Uncontrolled. Under settlement-derived Accord, step (iii) has no defined target (CT-16).

**PL-07.04 Faction elimination → Vacuum → Uncontrolled.** Entry: Stability 0 at Accounting end with no recovery action (GP-07.04). Steps: Mandate → 0, other attributes freeze (P-15); units Masterless; territories → Political Vacuum 1 season (no March in; Fort retained; tokens removed) → Uncontrolled (free entry); AP/CP tokens removed; Crown → Löwenritter Split; territory-tied clock effects cease; Strain +2; Torben Loyalty transfer (Crown). Terminal: faction removed (or contracted to city-state per CT-46). Reversible: `reconstitute … (Ob 4, multi-season)` / `Reconstitute: M = Influence − 6`.

**PL-07.05 Province capture via Seat.** Entry: invading army reaches Seat settlement. Steps: capture Seat (Assault/Siege) → provincial control transfers → non-Seat settlements with Order ≥ 3 may resist (holdouts) → reduce individually or grant autonomy. Terminal: province controlled (fractional if holdouts remain — PL-07.01).

**PL-07.06 Assault.** Entry: army adjacent, declares Assault. Steps: `Military vs Defense + garrison` (D-06.13) → Success: captured (Prosperity −1 if Partial or worse per adjacency §2.3) / Failure: repelled with casualties. Consequences: MS −1, Strain +1, IP +2 (CT-12/CT-14), settlement Order −1, Church CB if Cathedral, captured Prosperity if Mine. Terminal: control or repulse.

**PL-07.07 Siege.** Entry: `Military ≥ Defense` (settlement_layer) / any army with Heavy Infantry or Siege unit (march_layer) at hostile settlement. Steps: attacker fixed; each season defender Order −1; at Order 0 surrender; bypassed garrisons raid supply (D-07.14 note). Terminal: surrender or siege lifted. (CT-61.)

**PL-07.08 Bypass.** Entry: D-07.14. Steps: move past; bypassed garrison `Military vs Ob 1 each season: success = invader −1 Discipline on all units in the province` (settlement_layer) / `+1 attrition per bypassed hostile settlement` (march_layer). Terminal: army beyond. (CT-61.)

**PL-07.09 Territory battle (legacy).** Entry: contested entry. Steps: `Battle Ob = floor(defender Military / 2) + 1`; attacker `Military pool vs Ob`; degree per PP-249; or opposed P-16 comparison; Fort dice to defender; Accord 3 defender +1D; weight-of-numbers Stability −1; conquest → Accord 1, defender Accord −1; MS/IP/Strain consequences. Terminal: territory transfer or hold. (CT-34.)

**PL-07.10 Occupation.** Entry: territory occupied (rules absent). Steps: Wealth −1 per occupied territory per season; PV zeroed. Terminal: unspecified (GP-07.06).

### SET-07 Throughlines applied
TL-04 — asserted (Accord ≥ 2 counts; conquest → 1); violated by CT-58 (+2 Accord) and by `insurgency › §5.1` (Accord ≥ 4). TL-01 — Parliamentary Transfer and Treaty explicitly "GD-1 binding (no victory trigger)"; F-07.11 shows four incompatible sovereignty measures (CT-28, CT-18). TL-08 — Transfer roll, Fragmentation, Consolidation, Uprising all dice-form (CT-03). TL-11 — Seat/settlement granularity asserted by `fractional`, `settlement_layer`; territory-scale battle (PL-07.09) predates it. TL-15 (ratchets) — Secession "permanently until reconquest"; Consolidation ends fractional state. TL-16 (CB) — consumed on use whether success or fail; Partial regenerates. TL-12 — H-14, H-15, H-16.

Crown covert actions … use the Influence pool at +1 Ob` (`stats › Crown Covert Actions (PP-236)`) — contradicts Crown Intel 3 in the same document (**GP-08.01**). Seeds: `Legitimacy_init = L value in column 2; Popular_Support_init = PS value in column 3 … Range [0, 7] … Factions with no L+PS at start (Restoration, Löwenritter) start with Legitimacy = Popular_Support = 0` (`core › Legitimacy + Popular Support — Starting Values`). Founded faction stats (`settlement_layer › §6.2 ED-790`): `L 2; PS 3; Influence floor(Renown ÷ 2); Wealth 2 + (# settlements controlled − 1), capped at 5; Military 1; Intel 2; Stability 3`. Latent RM / Insurgency baselines in SET-09.

**F-08.03 Faction Conviction texts** (`core › Faction Conviction Texts`): Crown (Almud) `"Order is not made — it is maintained. Everything depends on not flinching."`; Church (Himlensendt) `Faith`; Hafenmark (Baralta) `"Faith is not mediated — it is lived. …"`; Varfell (Vaynard) `"The strongest thread is the one others cannot see — …"`; Restoration (Erikssen collective) `"The community is the only legitimate political unit."`; Löwenritter (Ehrenwall) `"Valoria endures, whatever the cost."` [reference text; no mechanic attaches].

**F-08.04 Faction behaviour architecture (PP-686 v2).** Four components: Mission, Cascade, Public Expectation, Legitimacy + Popular Support (`faction_behavior › §1`). State schema (`§2`): authored `id, role, mission {text, primary_objective, beneficiary, aligned_categories, contradicted_categories, authored_at, prior_mission}, leader, organizational_hierarchy {nodes, edges (npc, supervisor), cascade_roots}, institutional_culture <-0.2..+0.2>`; derived `aggregate_effective_convictions, cascade_fidelity <-1..+1>, expected_convictions, aggregate_legitimacy, aggregate_popular_support, strictness <0..1>, mandate <0..7>`. Mission alignment: `if da_outcome_key.type in mission.aligned_categories: return -1; if … contradicted_categories: return +1; return 0`; categories reference `da.public_governance, da.covert_betrayal, da.diplomatic_alliance, da.antinomian_action, da.economic_intervention` (PP-687, absent — **GP-08.02**). Mission shift triggers: `1. Victory-track milestone passes/fails …; 2. Leader replacement … succession_mode in [contested, emergency, imposed]; 3. Mission failure threshold reached — ≥4 consecutive seasons of Mission-contradicting outcomes …; 4. Authored event in scenario`. Missions for the six factions are not authored in the corpus (Löwenritter's is quoted once: `Mission "Honour the Crown's institutional authority"` in `stats › ~~PP-246~~`) (**GP-08.03**).

**F-08.05 Cascade.** `effective_convictions(npc) = α(npc) × personal_convictions(npc) + (1 − α(npc)) × effective_convictions(supervisor(npc))`; root: `= personal_convictions(root)`. `α(npc) = clamp(α_base + α_seniority(npc.standing) + α_institution(faction.institutional_culture), 0, 1)`; `α_base 0.4; α_seniority -0.2 to +0.4 (Standing 1: -0.2; Standing 7: +0.4, linear); α_institution -0.2 to +0.2 (Hafenmark = -0.2; Crown = 0; Restoration = +0.1; Lowenritter = -0.1)`. Orphan: `α = 1.0`. Zero-vector leader (`magnitude < 0.1`): `Provisional state where succession is forced next Accounting`. Cycle → validator rejects. Multi-root: `Faction aggregate = weighted average of per-root aggregates, weighted by role-extent within faction (default uniform)`. Timing: `re-resolves at each Accounting … or on state.succession … DAs fired within a season use the cascade resolved at the prior Accounting`; damping `drift_coef = 0.6 per season`; `leader.scars >= 3 → cascade damping is suspended`. Aggregate: `normalize( Σ npc.standing × effective_convictions(npc) )`. Cascade fidelity `= cosine_similarity(aggregate_effective_convictions, expected_convictions(role))` in 13-Conviction space (taxonomy PP-684 absent — **GP-08.04**; NPC `personal_convictions` vectors absent — **GP-08.05**). Role templates (`§3.3.1`): `sovereign | Virtue 0.30, Authority 0.30, Honor 0.20, Faith 0.10, Warden 0.10; ecclesiastical | Faith 0.40, Authority 0.20, Scholastic 0.20, Precedent 0.10, Virtue 0.10; mercantile-procedural | Order 0.35, Utility 0.25, Scholastic 0.20, Liberty 0.10, Equity 0.10; intelligence-diplomatic | Scholastic 0.30, Utility 0.30, Authority 0.20, Precedent 0.10, Identity 0.10; reformist | Equity 0.30, Liberty 0.25, Community 0.20, Virtue 0.15, Scholastic 0.10; military-order | Honor 0.30, Authority 0.25, Virtue 0.15, Identity 0.15, Order 0.15`. Edge cases (`§3.9`): no leader → `α=1.0 each; Legitimacy decays at +λ_violation × 0.5/season until succession`; zero-magnitude cosine → `cascade_fidelity = 0`.

**F-08.06 Popular Support and Legitimacy dynamics** (`§3.4–3.5`, per controlled settlement after LPS-1): `ΔPopular_Support per season = α_temperament × attributed_mission_outcome(faction) + β_temperament × cascade_fidelity(faction) × outcome_polarity_gate + γ × (random shock; events)`; `attributed_outcome = raw_outcome × (1 − 0.5 × max(0, leader.self_other_orientation))`; `outcome_polarity_gate ∈ {0.5, 1.0}: if attributed_mission_outcome < 0, gate = 0.5`; `α_temperament + β_temperament = 1`; `γ: … default 0.5`. `ΔLegitimacy per season = +λ_continuity × seasons_in_role_uninterrupted + λ_procedural × procedural_event_score + λ_expectation × cascade_fidelity − λ_violation × violation_event_score` with `λ_continuity = 0.05, λ_procedural = 0.3, λ_expectation = 0.1, λ_violation = 0.6`. Procedural events: `da.diplomatic_alliance (treaty signed) → +1; state.succession normal → +1 (one-time); mechanical.mission_shift authored → +0.5; Religious sanction … favorable → +1; Formal acknowledgement by foreign power → +0.5`. Violation events: `da.covert_betrayal exposed == true → +1; state.coup_attempted → +1; meta.miraculous_event unfavorable (excommunication, heresy declaration) → +2; state.standing_change magnitude == 3 (Total Dismissal) involving faction leader → +1; low Cascade Fidelity for ≥4 consecutive seasons → +0.5/season`; `Multiple violations same season sum`. Temperament typology (`§3.4.1`; `territory_temperaments › §1`): `pragmatic 0.7/0.3; traditional 0.3/0.7; balanced 0.5/0.5; principled 0.2/0.8; outcomes-only 0.9/0.1`; `Faction effective temperament = population-weighted average across faction's territories` (uniform approximation used; population data absent — **GP-08.06**). Per-territory assignments (`territory_temperaments › §2`): `T1 pragmatic; T2 traditional; T3 balanced; T4 traditional; T5 traditional; T6 outcomes-only; T7 traditional; T8 pragmatic; T9 principled; T10 balanced; T11 traditional; T12 balanced; T13 outcomes-only; T14 balanced; T15 outcomes-only; T16 pragmatic; T17 balanced`; aggregates `Crown α≈0.50; Church α=0.20; Hafenmark α≈0.55; Varfell α≈0.50`. Drift: `territory.temperament_drift = clamp(territory.temperament_drift + 0.1 × strain_delta, -1, +1)` on `env.peninsular_strain_shock` with positive delta — how drift converts to α/β shift is unstated (**GP-08.07**). These continuous deltas (0.05, 0.3…) are applied to integer 0–7 settlement L/PS with no rounding rule (**GP-08.08**).

**F-08.07 Strictness and Ob modifier.** `strictness(faction) = clamp(base_strictness + 0.5 × (aggregate_Legitimacy / 7) − 0.3 × (aggregate_Popular_Support / 7), 0, 1)`, `base_strictness = 0.4`. `expectation_alignment_modifier(da, faction) = sign(da.cascade_alignment_with_role) × strictness(faction) × {1, 2}` (`+1 if ±1 from role expectation, +2 if ±2`). `Ob_modifier = clamp(mission_alignment_modifier + cascade_alignment_modifier + expectation_alignment_modifier, −2, +2)`; `Modifier rounding: ≥0.5 rounds to ±1; ≥1.5 rounds to ±2`. `cascade_alignment_modifier` is named (`-1, 0, +1 — reads PP-684 13-Conviction projection`) but its rule is not stated (**GP-08.09**). NPC context flag `Key.payload.role_acting` selects effective vs personal convictions.

**F-08.08 Legacy Ethical Framework modifiers** (struck; retained in `faction_politics` — CT-40): `Crown (Virtue) −1 Ob public / +1 Ob covert; Church (Faith) −1 doctrine-aligned / +2 thread-supporting; Hafenmark (Categorical Imperative) −1 procedural / +1 ad-hoc; Varfell (Scholastic) −1 evidence-based / +1 emotional; Restoration (Equity) −1 community / +1 hierarchical; Löwenritter (Honor) −1 loyal / +2 disloyal` (`core › ~~Ethical Framework Modifiers~~`).

**F-08.09 Stat change paths** (`ci_political › §4.1`, verbatim rows): Mandate `Govern OW in own capital +1 (max once/season; max = starting Mandate); Crown Treaty Success/OW −1 to target; Crown +1 (OW); Parliamentary Censure −1; Parliamentary Outlawry −2; Turmoil Mandate check Failure → −1; Seizure Failure −1 to Church; Diplomatic success vs NPC +1`. Stability `Governed exclusively by Triggers 1–5 per faction_layer_v30` (absent — **GP-08.10**) `Plus Accounting Stability Check (≥2 attribute loss) and Consolidation recovery (+1 clean season)`. Wealth `Trade Success +1; Occupation −1 per occupied territory per season; Blockade −2/season; Wealth Zero → Professional unit Discipline −1/season; Tributary +1/year (receiver)`. Military `Battle loss (defender) −1; Battle loss (attacker, margin −2+) −1`. Influence `Spy/Intel Success +1; Officer capture +1 to captor`. Govern full effect (`§4.2`): `Failure | No change; Stability −1 if Prosperity was 0 …; Partial | Accord maintained …; Success | Accord +1 (max 3); OR Mandate recovery +1 if in capital …; Overwhelming | Both` (vs `strategic_layer › P-22` `Govern success … increases Prosperity by 1 … failure decreases it by 1` — **CT-73**). Trade (`§4.3`): `Failure | No income; Partial | Wealth maintained; Success | Wealth +1; Overwhelming | Wealth +2 (capped by seasonal cap of +2)`. Hafenmark Wealth sink `Wealth above 5 may be spent as bonus dice … 1 Wealth token → +1D on Trade or Diplomacy (max +2D per action) [PROVISIONAL]` (`stats`). `Leadership Deviation Stability Check Obs: Crown 2 | Church 3 | Hafenmark 2 | Varfell 2 | Guilds 2 | Restoration Movement 2 | Löwenritter 2` (`stats`) — the check itself is undefined (**GP-08.11**).

**F-08.10 Institutional Mandate — Uphold / Appease.** `Trigger: L ≥ 4 AND Domain Action directly challenges faction core institutional authority. Uphold | Before roll | Roll proceeds | None; Appease | Before roll | Action cancelled | L −1. NPC: Appease if L ≥ 4 AND Stability ≤ 3.` (`stats › Institutional Mandate — Uphold / Appease (PP-189)`); `Trigger scope (ED-324): Fires when a Domain Action directly targets the faction's L stat OR their unique clock/track — CI for Church, PI for Hafenmark, VTM for Varfell. Stability, Influence, Wealth, Military do not trigger. Deterministic — no GM discretion.` (`parliament`); `targeted faction Mandate ≥ 4` (`strategic_layer › Institutional Mandate`); per-faction core authorities (`stats › ED-003`): `Crown: sovereignty or legal authority; Church: spiritual authority or excommunication power; Guilds: trade monopoly or taxation; Hafenmark: guild autonomy; Varfell: territorial governance; Restoration: community organizing; Löwenritter: military authority`. **CT-55** (L vs Mandate; "targets L stat" vs "challenges core authority" — the second needs a classifier no document supplies, **GP-08.12**).

**F-08.11 NPC faction AI — posture stack** (`ci_political › §6.1`): `Priority 1 — EXISTENTIAL (Stability ≤ 1 or territory count = 1): … Govern, Trade, Recess … Do not attack. Priority 2 — DEFEND (enemy unit adjacent to own ungarrisoned territory): Muster …; if no Legionary card, Recess. Priority 3 — CONSOLIDATE (Stability ≤ 2 OR Wealth ≤ 1 OR Mandate ≤ 2): Govern + Trade. Priority 4 — COUNTER-THREAT (CI ≥ 55 AND faction is HF or Crown): Hafenmark: Suppress … mandatory …; Crown: Crown Treaty if Mandate ≥ 4 and PV ≥ 10. Priority 5 — EXPAND: Military expansion if border units available. Seizure (Church). Intel operation (Varfell). Priority 6 — OPPORTUNISTIC: Govern in capital, Trade.` Faction-specific (`§6.2`): Crown `Mandate ≥ 4 … Govern (capital) … if Mandate < 4; Crown Treaty targets: PLAYABLE factions only, Mandate ≥ 2. Prefer … lowest PV; defend T1, T14, T2, T3 …; if PI ≥ 10, … Parliamentary actions`; Hafenmark `Suppress … at CI ≥ 40; Trade when Wealth < 4, Govern when Mandate < 4 …; T8 and T10 must remain garrisoned. T7 is expendable; Parliamentary Manoeuvre: … whenever PI ≥ 5`; Church `Assert when CI < 60 and Legionary cards unavailable; Church Seizure when CI ≥ 40 and prominent territories have PT ≥ 3; Govern in T9 …; Templar units in PT ≥ 3 territories`; Varfell `Intel ops against Crown …; Military expansion only when VTM ≥ 2 (Warden path) or territory count ≤ 3` (VTM struck — GP-03.09). Other NPC rules: `NPC factions Appease if L ≥ 4 AND Stability ≤ 3`; Ministry tree (SET-10 PL-10.06); Latent RM AI `Reduce PT in adjacent territories. Protect MS. Oppose Church and any faction with Mandate ≥ 5.` (`insurgency › §3.4`); Löwenritter reactive `if any bordering faction's Military exceeds Lowenritter Military, … prioritises Military Consolidation … the following season. … a guidance rule, not a threshold mechanic` (`stats › PP-238`) (**H-17** — "guidance" is not executable); NPC governor priority (SET-06). `Threat-aware, stat-priority ordering` replaces `Greedy / single-turn evaluation` (`ci_political › §0`). No AI is stated for Guilds, Schoenland, Altonia, Wardens, Riskbreakers beyond fragments (**GP-08.13**).

**F-08.12 Fog of war on stats.** `All faction stats (Mandate, Influence, Wealth, Military, Intelligence, Stability) displayed to non-owning players in four qualitative states: In ruins 1; Poor 2–3; Good 4–5; Excellent 6–7. Own faction: exact values visible. Intel stat: always hidden from all players` (`strategic_layer › §9.2`); `without successful Intel action, enemy faction stats are hidden. Intel reveals them`; `Counter-espionage detection at Intel ≥ 4: on enemy Spy attempt in your territory, roll Intel vs Ob 3. Success = you learn the spy action occurred` (`stats › Stats`). → TL-18.

**F-08.13 Faction-level Standing / tokens.** `Standing tokens` against Crown (`institutions › Royal Deposition`: `at least 2 other player factions have active Standing tokens against Crown`), `Honour: +1 Standing` (Open Pledge), `Hafenmark +1 Standing vs Church` (Reformed Settlement), `Target faction Standing +1` (Senator Outward OW), `Standing −2` (treaty violation), `Holder Standing −1` (Punishment transfer) — a faction-pair "Standing" with no defined range, storage or decay (**GP-08.14**; CT-57 name collision). `Guild Favour ≥ 5 in target territory (1–7 territory track)` (`stats › Guilds — Economic Leverage`), `Guild Favour +1` (`tracks › PP-178`), `Church has Favour ≥ 3` (`institutions › Prudence`) — per-territory Favour tracks with no ownership model (**GP-08.15**).

**Gaps.** GP-08.01 Crown Intel 3 vs "NO Intel stat". GP-08.02 PP-687 da subtypes. GP-08.03 Missions unauthored. GP-08.04 13-Conviction taxonomy. GP-08.05 NPC conviction vectors. GP-08.06 population weights. GP-08.07 drift → α/β. GP-08.08 continuous Δ onto integer stats. GP-08.09 cascade_alignment_modifier rule. GP-08.10 faction_layer Stability Triggers 1–5. GP-08.11 Leadership Deviation check. GP-08.12 "challenges core authority" classifier. GP-08.13 missing NPC AIs. GP-08.14 faction Standing model. GP-08.15 Favour tracks. GP-08.16 `PP-241` Crown→Löwenritter covert delegation: what "delegates" means as an action. GP-08.17 `Claim (Domain Action) Masterless units` roll unspecified.

### SET-08 Pipelines

**PL-08.01 Cascade re-resolution.** Entry: Accounting, or `state.succession` Key. Steps: validate hierarchy (orphans α = 1; cycles reject); compute α per NPC; propagate effective_convictions root→leaves; per-root aggregates → faction aggregate; damp toward new equilibrium (0.6/season; bypass at scars ≥ 3); compute cascade_fidelity; emit `mechanical.cascade_resolution`. Mid-season succession → new values apply only to subsequent Keys. Terminal: aggregates for next season.

**PL-08.02 Mission shift.** Entry: any of four triggers. Steps: set `prior_mission`, new Mission (authoring source unspecified for triggers 1–3 — **GP-08.19**); emit `mechanical.mission_shift`; recompute alignment interpretations; procedural +0.5 L if authored. Terminal: new Mission.

**PL-08.03 Seasonal PS/L update.** Entry: Accounting. Steps: gather `da_outcome.*` stream → attributed outcome (D-08.09) → ΔPS (D-08.08) per controlled settlement; procedural/violation scores → ΔL (D-08.10) per settlement; clamp 0–7; strain shocks → drift (D-08.15) → faction temperament (D-08.14). Terminal: settlement L/PS → SET-06 PL-06.05.

**PL-08.04 Domain Action Ob assembly.** Entry: a DA declared by a faction. Steps: base Ob (SET-01 F-01.10) → territory/clock modifiers → D-08.13 Ob_modifier (±2) → floor 1. Terminal: final Ob (or difficulty via D-01.05).

**PL-08.05 NPC faction turn.** Entry: Phase 1 for an NPC faction. Steps: evaluate posture D-08.16 (first true) → choose card/action per faction-specific rules → Institutional Mandate responses when targeted (D-08.18) → Ministry-style redirect if territory already acted on. Terminal: declared plays. (Card-cooldown constraints apply; "Recess if neither card available".)

**PL-08.06 Institutional Mandate response.** Entry: a DA that triggers (D-08.17). Steps: targeted faction chooses Uphold (roll proceeds) or Appease (action cancelled, L −1); NPC per D-08.18. Terminal: DA proceeds or is cancelled.

**PL-08.07 Accounting Stability check.** Entry: `≥2 attribute loss this season`. Steps: `Stability pool vs Ob = loss magnitude` / `M = Stability − loss_magnitude`; Failure → Stability −1 (per CC-4); Stability 0 → elimination check (SET-07 PL-07.04). Terminal: Stability updated.

### SET-08 Throughlines applied
TL-05 — asserted (Mandate derived) but F-08.02 authored L/PS columns and every "Mandate −1" effect treat it as primitive (CT-08). TL-10 — asserted `faction_behavior › §3.7`; violated by CT-40. TL-18 — asserted (`§9.2`, Intel hidden). TL-08 — Govern/Trade/Assert migrated; Institutional Mandate and posture logic method-agnostic. TL-12 — H-17 (guidance rule). TL-14 — "Standing" collision (CT-57), "L" vs "Mandate" vs "Legitimacy" (CT-55).

## SET-09 — Succession, Splits, Emergence & Collapse

### SET-09 Flattened statement

**F-09.01 Universal succession trigger.** `When a faction leader is eliminated (killed, captured without ransom paid within 2 seasons, deposed, or permanently incapacitated), a Succession Contest opens at next Accounting UNLESS the faction has a canonical sole heir with Disposition ≥ +3 to faction apparatus.` Matrix (`faction_succession_split › §2.1`): `Alive and in power | — | No contest.; Eliminated | Yes, Disposition ≥ +3, Standing ≥ 4 | Smooth succession. Heir inherits. Faction Stability −1, no contest.; Eliminated | Yes, Disposition < +3 OR Standing < 4 | Contested …; Eliminated | No heir | Contested …; Eliminated | Multiple claimants of comparable Standing | Contested (mandatory regardless of Disposition).` "comparable Standing" unquantified (**GP-09.01**). Ransom mechanic absent (**GP-09.02**).

**F-09.02 Contenders and resolution (FSS-1, 2026-05-30).** Claim bases: `Rank in the faction's Inner Circle … with Standing ≥ 3; Blood claim (named canonical heir …); Institutional claim (holds a ministerial / committee / dicastery / council position …); External backing (another faction's Diplomat action declaring support — costs Diplomatic Token)`. Strength (`deterministic; no roll`): `Blood heir | Mandate + Influence; Institutional candidate | Influence + faction's rank-specific stat (Crown=Mandate, Hafenmark=Influence, Church=Influence, Varfell=Intelligence); External-backed candidate | Diplomatic backer's Influence + own Standing`. `Stage 1 — Who leads (contested resolver). Between the two highest-strength contenders, resolve M = strength(top1) − strength(top2) via the deterministic+stochastic resolver … Success/Overwhelming → top1 leads; Failure → the runner-up (top2) takes the lead instead … Multi-contender fields resolve pairwise from the top down. Tie in strength: faction's dominant stat decides which is "top1" …` `Stage 2 — Whether it splits (deterministic strength gap G = strength(top1) − strength(top2))`: `G ≥ 3 → UNIFIED. … Stability −1, Mandate −1. Dice tail: a Stage-1 Failure by the leader → fractious Regency this Accounting instead.`; `G = 2 → UNIFIED, fractious. … Runner-up Disposition check: Disposition ≥ 0 → accepts; below 0 → splits. Stability −1, Mandate −1.`; `G ≤ 1 → SPLIT. … leader holds the formal faction name and ~60% of assets; the runner-up … ~40%. Dice tail: a Stage-1 Overwhelming by the leader → UNIFIED instead.`; `No qualifying contender …: Regency. Faction operates under NPC council governance. No leader stat bonuses. … another contest at next Accounting. If 3 consecutive Accountings produce no winner: faction collapses per faction_layer §1.5.` `(Ob 3 / net-success counting is retired for the succession fork …)`. **CT-23**: the same document's `§3` worked example rolls `7d10 vs Ob 3 → 3 net successes (Success)` etc., and `baralta_crown_claim › §2` resolves by `Each claiming faction rolls their pool vs Ob 3 (TN 7). Highest net successes wins. Ties: Löwenritter wins military ties … Hafenmark wins political ties`. Partial result for a third contender (`RM gets a one-season Ob −1 on all actions against either Varfell splinter`) exists only in the example. Open: `Simultaneous succession: … resolve in descending pre-loss Mandate order.`; `Player-led contender: … deferred` (**H-18**: `Player selects backing`).

**F-09.03 Split assets** (`§2.4`, verbatim): `Provinces | Geographic split. Winner takes … historical capital province + adjacent provinces … winner holds the capital Seat; each contested non-capital province goes to the nearer contender (proximity measured through settlement adjacency graph).; Settlements | Follow province unless a settlement's governor has Disposition ≥ +3 toward a specific contender …; Mandate | Winner: 60% (round down). Splinter: 40% (round down). Remainder burns; Influence | Winner: 60% … Splinter: 40% … Remainder lost; Wealth | Winner takes 70% (round down). Splinter takes 30% (round down).; Military units | Each unit rolls Loyalty check: Loyalty = (Commander Disposition toward contender) + (unit Discipline). Higher-Loyalty contender wins unit. Ties: unit disbands.; Stability | Winner: current − 1. Splinter: 2; Rank ladder NPCs | Each Inner Circle NPC chooses based on their Disposition. Non-Inner-Circle NPCs default to the contender whose province they're located in.; Faction name | Winner retains original. Splinter renames`. Splinter (`§2.5`): `Partial faction sheet (… 50% of frozen parent values, or as allocated per §2.4). Stability 2. Victory conditions of the parent faction available but reset. Ability to pursue Stage 2→5 emergence`; may `Re-merge with parent via treaty (… requires both at Mandate ≥ 3)`. "Mandate 60%" splits a derived value (TL-05 violation; CT-08); "nearer contender" needs the contender's location (**GP-09.03**); IC NPC choice threshold absent (**H-19**); "Loyalty check" is a comparison, not a roll, despite "rolls".

**F-09.04 Crown-specific succession (Baralta Crown Claim).** Trigger: `Crown faction is eliminated (Mandate 0 + Loyalty 0)` OR `Royal Deposition succeeds` (`baralta › §2 Trigger`). `All major factions with Mandate ≥ 3 may stake a claim.` Candidates: `Löwenritter | Military 5 + Stability | Löwenritter Autonomy ≥ 3; Hafenmark | Mandate + Influence | Baralta alive + Hafenmark Mandate ≥ 4; Church | Mandate + Influence | CI ≥ 40`. `If Hafenmark wins: Consecration Crisis fires. If Church wins: Theocratic regency. CI +10. All factions face Stability check Ob 2.` Baralta Claim Precondition (ED-409): `Preconditions: Crown Mandate ≤ 2 AND PI ≥ 5 AND Baralta alive; Pool: Hafenmark Mandate + Influence (4 + 4 = 8D …); Ob: Crown Mandate + 1 (minimum Ob 2); [Success:] When Crown Succession Contest fires, Hafenmark gets +2D on the contest roll; Failure: Hafenmark Stability −1 … cannot be repeated for 2 seasons.` Consecration Crisis (`§3`): `Church Stability ≥ 4: Himlensendt Refuses Consecration. CI +3; Crown Mandate inherited by Hafenmark at −2; … If Baralta governs successfully for 3 consecutive seasons without Church consecration (Mandate does not drop below 3): … CI passive advance reduced to +0.5/season …; If Baralta's Mandate drops below 3 during the 3-season window: … Succession Contest reopens. Baralta is excluded.` `Church Stability ≤ 3: Himlensendt Consecrates Under Duress. CI −5; Church Stability −3; At Stability 0: Church anti-death-spiral floor applies …; Baralta takes Crown with full consecration.` `Override: Arc 11 Originary Lock Encounter. … GM discretion.` (**H-20**). `§5`: Option A merger vs Option B persistence — `ED-411 — … Option B (Hafenmark persists, PP-487 fires)`: `At PI ≥ 4: institutional successor takes over, Hafenmark continues as an NPC-controlled faction allied with Crown. At PI < 4: Hafenmark fractures` (PP-487 itself absent — **GP-09.04**); successor stats `Mandate ≈ PI/2, Stability reduced` (`faction_politics › §6.1`). `faction_succession_split › §6`: `this ED's generalized Succession Contest subsumes baralta_crown_claim_v30 §2. Baralta's specific Deed-claim and Consecration Crisis remain as Crown-specific modifiers` — but the candidate list, pools and Ob-3 resolution were not rewritten (CT-23). Scenario C concurrent contest: `Hafenmark, Torben, and Löwenritter (if Löwenritter Autonomy ≥ 2) all stake claim. Torben's pool = Mandate + Bloodline Legitimacy bonus (+2D …). If Torben wins: … Baralta reverts … Stability −1 …; Löwenritter Autonomy +1` (`baralta › §7.3`) — "Autonomy +1" on a staged enum (CT-21). Rank cascades on each outcome (`faction_politics › Part 6`; SET-11).

**F-09.05 Löwenritter — graduated autonomy vs Coup Counter (CT-21).** Stages (`core › Löwenritter Graduated Autonomy`; `conflict_architecture › Graduated Löwenritter Autonomy`): `Loyal | Start | S014 Barracks answers to Crown via Ehrenwall …; Restless | Crown Stability ≤ 3, OR no military action 4+ seasons, OR Crown loses a province | S014 follows Löwenritter orders for defensive actions. Crown +1 Ob offensive deployment. | Fragmentation checks at T14 Ob +1.; Autonomous | Crown Stability ≤ 2, OR Ehrenwall Disposition toward Almud < 0, OR 4+ seasons Restless without resolution | S014 does not respond to Crown. … | Crown Military reduced by T14 garrison. Cannot access Fort 3. PI −1.; Split | Crown attacks Löwenritter, OR Crown eliminated, OR 4+ seasons Autonomous without resolution | T14 becomes Löwenritter territory. Löwenritter = separate faction (L3/PS3/Inf2/W3/Mil5/Stab5). PI −3. | Crown Military drops to 2 AND loses most units. Crown loses T14, PV drops by 3.` `Reversal: Stages 1–3 reversible. Crown can return to Loyal by: raising Stability above 3, conducting military action validating Löwenritter identity, or improving Ehrenwall Disposition … Stage 4 (Split) is irreversible without reconquest.` A fifth row in `core` only: `Coup | Löwenritter loses faith in monarch AND has candidate (Torben, Elske, or a Duke with Löwenritter support) | T14 under Löwenritter. Crown faction SUSPENDED. Löwenritter installs new monarch. | If Torben/Elske/Lenneth installed: Crown resumes under new monarch; Löwenritter recedes … If Duke (Vaynard/Baralta) installed: Crown absorbed … Crown Military was 5 only because it WAS Löwenritter — without them, Crown Military = 2.` "loses faith" is undefined (**GP-09.05**). Counter model (`stats_1_7_scale › Löwenritter — Martial Law / Coup Trigger`): `Graduated Autonomy increments (+1 each): Church Influence reaches 40 while Crown took no action to reduce it that season; Torben's loyalty reaches 3–2 or lower; Crown loses 2+ territories in one season without a military response Domain Action. Counter never decrements. Fires at next seasonal accounting once at 4. (PP-577 …)`; `Löwenritter Coup Counter immediately set to 4 (coup fires next season)` (`institutions › Royal Deposition`); `if Counter reaches 3, Coup fires` and `If Counter was at 2 when this trigger fires, Coup fires immediately` (`baralta › §7.1, §7.4`); `Coup Counter +0.5` (`early_game` T8, superseded). "Reversal … reversible" (stages) vs "Counter never decrements". `Löwenritter T14 on Coup (PP-569, ED-348): … Löwenritter assumes immediate full control of T14 … Crown retains no claim.` (`parliament`). Martial Law (`stats`): `All non-Military Domain Actions in Crown territories require secondary Military check (Löwenritter Military pool, TN 7, Ob 2); failure blocks the action. Persists until players remove it (Influence vs Ob = Löwenritter Military ÷ 2, round up, min Ob 3) or Church Influence drops below 40.` Ehrenwall Split card fires at Split stage (`worldbuilding › §4.3`). Residency/Elske (SET-03). Riskbreaker post-Split resource (SET-10).

**F-09.06 Deposition procedures — CT-77.** (a) `Royal Deposition: Fires if ALL of: PI ≥ 5, Church L ≥ 5, Crown L ≤ 1, AND at least 2 other player factions have active Standing tokens against Crown. Effect: Crown player must pass the Crown to Löwenritter (Split stage triggers) OR call an emergency Parliamentary Session (Senator Inward, Ob 3) to restore legitimacy. If neither: Crown faction is eliminated; Löwenritter Coup Counter immediately set to 4` (`institutions › Parliament Deposition Mechanic (PP-194)`; `baralta › §1` quotes it as `PI ≥ 5 + Church Mandate ≥ 5 + Crown Mandate ≤ 1 + 2 Standing tokens`). (b) `Motion of No Confidence: 1. Parliamentary Vote | Influence vs Crown Mandate (existing §8.11) 2. Holy See concurrence | Confessor chooses: concur or refuse. Deposal: Crown Mandate → 1, Stability −3. Succession: Torben if available and loyal; if not, IP +10; if no heir, interregnum (Crown actions +2 Ob). Holy See refusal: CI +3, Thread Tension (TT) +2.` (`worldbuilding › §6.2`) with card `Constitutional Crisis | Trigger: Crown Mandate drops to 1 OR Crown loses 3+ territories in a single season | Motion of No Confidence automatically raised …` (`§6.3`). (c) universal contest (F-09.01) on "deposed". Three procedures with different preconditions and outcomes; "Confessor chooses" has no NPC rule (**H-32**); "available and loyal" unquantified (**GP-09.06**).

**F-09.07 Insurgency pipeline (GD-3, Pass 2i).** Stage 1 (`insurgency › §2`): `RM_BASE_STRENGTH 1; RM_GROWTH_PER_ARC 1; RM_PT_DECAY_CHANCE 0.35; RM_VARFELL_COOPTION_BONUS 0.1`; per arc: `chance = min(0.8, RM_PT_DECAY_CHANCE × (RM_BASE_STRENGTH + RM_GROWTH_PER_ARC × (arc − 1)))`; for each territory PT > 0: skip Church-owned; skip Inquisitor present; `If Varfell adjacent AND Varfell I ≥ EINHIR_I_GATE: chance += RM_VARFELL_COOPTION_BONUS × rm_strength (capped at 0.9)`; `roll uniform [0, 1); if roll < chance: PT − 1` (`EINHIR_I_GATE` value absent — **GP-09.07**). Stage 2 Latent RM (`§3`): trigger `WA ≤ −2; At least 3 territories have PT ≤ 1; MS ≤ 50`; stats `Mandate | Count of territories with PT ≤ 1 (min 2, max 5); Influence 4; Wealth 1; Military 0; Stability 3`; `exerts influence within territories but does NOT control them … +1 Ob to any Church action in that territory`; actions `Community Weaving | Influence vs Ob = Thread Tension ÷ 20 (round up, min 1). Requires TS 30+ practitioner affiliated. | OW: MS +2, PT −1 …; Success: MS +1, PT −1; Partial: no effect; Failure: Stability −1, MS −1. Co-Movement card drawn.; Grassroots Organising | Influence vs Ob 2 | Success: RM Mandate +1 in territory with PT ≤ 2 (caps at Mandate 5); Resist Seizure | … +1 Ob to Church seizure roll. Passive`; suppression `WA returns to ≥ 0; All territories rise to PT ≥ 2; RM Stability reaches 0`. Stage 3 Insurgency (`§4`): `2+ contiguous territories at Uncontrolled status, sustained 2 consecutive seasons` (contiguity per settlement adjacency); baseline `Legitimacy 1.0 (float); Influence 2-3; Stability 2; Wealth 0-1; Military Variable; Status non-parliamentary [PROVISIONAL]`; CAN: hold territory, invade (Military Conquest), be invaded, generate/receive CB, be excommunicated, informal treaties, be Transfer target; CANNOT: vote, initiate Transfer, sign formal Crown Treaties, invoke Stay; `Insurgencies can invade ANY faction, including the parent faction`. Stage 4 Promotion (`§5`): `L ≥ 3; 2+ territories held; Accord ≥ 4 averaged across held territories; Sustained 2 consecutive seasons` — **CT-43** (Accord max is 3); status `PT < 3 average → RM-variant … extra-parliamentary; PT ≥ 3 → parliamentary`; `persistent — once set … retains that status` [PROVISIONAL]; RM-variant `Inherits Latent RM stat profile if Latent RM existed at promotion (Wealth / Stability carry over)`; Convictions by emergence conditions (algorithm absent — **GP-09.08**). Dissolution (`§6.2`, ED-881 ratified): evaluated in order `1. Military defeat — territorial count drops to 0 …, OR Legitimacy < 1.0 AND territorial count < 2; 2. Sponsor withdrawal — … sponsor faction collapses, signs a treaty renouncing support, or is itself militarily defeated — … Legitimacy decays −0.5/season, and it dissolves on crossing the Legitimacy floor with a shrunk base; 3. Amnesty / negotiated — … (parent Mandate vs Insurgency Resolve, via the Domain Action Resolver …) …; 4. Persist (stalemate)`; `P(sponsor-loss/season) is the key tuning value` (unset — **GP-09.09**); "Insurgency Resolve" undefined (**GP-09.10**); "sponsor" via "informal alliance" undefined (**GP-09.11**). Promoted Faction (`§6.3`): same four paths; `a Promoted Faction at Stability 0 also follows the standard Collapse Exit Procedure, faction_layer §1.5 / ED-675` (absent); `does not demote back to Insurgency`. `§6.4 … unidirectional except for Stage 2 ↔ Stage 1`. GD-2 cross-binding: `Insurgency formation triggers GD-2 mandatory threat response by the parent faction … Standard GD-2 mandatory action triggers apply if Accord drops below threshold` (GD-2 absent — **GP-09.12**). GD-1: `Insurgencies and Promoted Factions can WIN via peninsular_sovereignty … once they hold 11+ territories sustained 2 seasons`.

**F-09.08 RM emergence — three models (CT-63).** (a) Renown ladder (`settlement_layer › §6.2`): `Stage 1 — Cell (Renown 0–2) … Stage 2 — Organization (Renown 3–4): Assigned to or seize a settlement … Stage 3 — Movement (Renown 5–6): Multiple settlements … partial sheet … Stage 4 — Faction (Renown 7–8): Provincial authority … full faction stat sheet … Stage 5 — Hegemon (Renown 9–10)`; requirements `2 → 3 | Control 2+ settlements. Renown 5+. At least 2 NPC officers with Disposition +3.; 3 → 4 | Control 4+ settlements across 2+ provinces. Renown 7+. Declare faction formally (Domain Action: Influence pool = Renown ÷ 2, Ob 3). At least 1 province Seat controlled.; 4 → 5 | Control 2+ province Seats. Renown 9+. Full faction stat sheet. Participate in Parliament …`; Founded Faction stats (SET-08 F-08.02). (b) RM-specific (`faction_succession_split › §4`): `Stage 1 Cell: RM exists as Presence markers … Stage 2 Organization: RM captures one settlement (via peninsular_strain §2.5 RM exception or Secession …). Stage 3 Movement: RM controls 2+ settlements … Stage 4 Faction: RM controls 4+ settlements across 2+ provinces OR holds 1+ provincial Seat via Cultural Uprising (victory §3.5 Phase 2). Stage 5 Hegemon: RM controls 2+ provinces.`; `Settlement Emergence: If a settlement's Order = 0 (not merely ≤ 1) AND PT ≤ 1 AND local Disposition toward Yrsa Vossen is ≥ +3, RM may declare Settlement Emergence — taking governance of that settlement … at most once per province per 4 seasons.` (c) GD-3 pipeline (F-09.07) in which Latent RM `does NOT control` territory and formation needs two Uncontrolled territories. Also RM is `statless … 5 players only` (`core`) yet has stats in (c) and `Mandate 2, Influence 4, Wealth 2, Stability 3` in `strategic_layer › Scenario B` (CT-24). RM identity (`campaign_architecture › Part 2`): `RM does not believe threadwork is real`; `"Community Organizing" for RM is political organizing, NOT a Thread operation … Uses social stats (Charisma, Attunement), not Thread stats (Spirit) … Does NOT produce MS change` — vs Latent RM `Community Weaving … MS +2` (CT-25); crisis paths `Embrace / Denial / Schism … Player influence determines which path` (**H-21**). RM leader: `Yrsa Vossen` (`stats › ED-005 Resolution (PP-286)`; `faction_succession`) vs `Elder Solvei Kaldring` proposed (`worldbuilding › §8`) vs `MARET VOSSEN` (`stats › Restoration Movement — Named NPCs (ED-005 resolved)`) — three names for the RM contact (**GP-09.13**). `Erikssen collective` in the Conviction table (`core`).

**F-09.09 Warden faction activation** (`campaign_architecture › Part 6`): start `~8–15 Einhir … not a faction`; paths `A — Recruited by Varfell. WR 3+ → institutional branch of Varfell …; B — Recruited by RM (post-revelation). RM Embrace arc + Vossen alliance …; C — Recruited by player. High WR + player's Founded Org …; D — Recruited by Lenneth. Elske Loyalty Track + Schoenland diplomacy …; E — Independent (crisis-driven). MS ≤ 20 → … Declare Mending Sanctuaries …` If activated: `Governance stat = average Spirit of active Menders. Acquisition via Mending Sanctuaries … Victory path: still Peninsular Sovereignty`. "High WR", "Vossen alliance", "Schoenland diplomacy", "Mending Sanctuaries" have no rules (**GP-09.14**).

**F-09.10 Faction collapse → city-state → dissolution** (`settlement_layer › §6.3`): `1. Province lost: … Settlements … with governors loyal to the collapsing faction may resist the new controller (Order ≥ 3 + governor Disposition ≥ +3 toward the faction) … holdout settlements. 2. Last province lost: … if the faction leader … is alive and located in a settlement they personally control …, the faction survives as a city-state … 3. City-state mechanics: … partial stat sheet (Influence, Wealth, Stability — no Mandate, no Military unless garrisoned). It cannot issue national-level Domain Actions but can: defend its settlements, trade, form alliances, participate in subnational politics, and work toward re-emergence … 4. Full collapse: If the faction leader is killed or captured and no successor exists with Standing 4+, the faction dissolves.` **CT-46** with Stability-0 elimination (SET-07 F-07.08) and `strategic_layer › §9.10` hybrid (`Player Character continues … unaffiliated; … reconstitute … (Ob 4, multi-season)`).

**F-09.11 Generational Shift (Torben) — CT-42.** `faction_politics › §8.1`: fires on first of `1. Campaign Elapsed Seasons ≥ 24; 2. Accumulated Generational Readiness ≥ 5 (Protection Duties +1, Tutoring/Mentoring scenes +1, successful Crown stabilizing actions with Torben witnessing +1; Max +2 Readiness per year-arc); 3. Crown Stability ≤ 1; 4. Almud's death`. Outcomes by Disposition to player (`§8.2`): `+3 or higher | Loyal Heir | … Stability +1 on succession events; +1 to +2 | Cooperative Heir | Stability −0; 0 | Neutral Heir | Stability −1; −1 to −2 | Wary Heir | … Player's Crown Std 6+ is under active challenge. Stability −2; −3 or lower | Hostile Heir | … Rival Standing track (SUC-02). … Stability −3`. Readiness track (`§8.3`): `0–10 … At Readiness ≥ 7, Torben exits "ward" status … treated as an NPC at Crown Std 5+`; `If Torben reaches Readiness 7 while the player holds Crown Std 7 …: … If Torben's Disposition to the player is ≥ +2, the player may remain as co-regent …; otherwise, Std 7 is contested and must be resolved via SUC-03.` Trigger at Readiness ≥ 5 vs ward-exit at ≥ 7 vs `settlement_layer › §7.1` clock (+1 per 5 years; thresholds 2/4/6 with attribute penalties; TS ≥ 50 exempt; `applies to PCs` per ED-SETT-07). Interactions (`baralta › §7.1`): `Seasons ≥ 24 | Any | ≥ 4 (Crown-aligned) | No effect; Seasons ≥ 24 | Any | ≤ 3 (Altonian) | +1 [Autonomy]; Readiness ≥ 5 | ≥ +1 | Any | No effect; Loyalty transfers stabilize; Readiness ≥ 5 | ≤ 0 | Any | +1; Crown Stability ≤ 1 | Any | Any | +1 automatic (… in addition to Loyalty-based increment); Almud death | Any | Any | Succession Declaration Scene takes precedence; no automatic Coup increment`. `If IP ≥ 60 … Torben's Loyalty threshold for Altonian-alignment drops from ≤ 3 to ≤ 4`; `If IP ≥ 75 AND Torben Disposition to player ≤ 0 …: Torben becomes formal Altonian diplomatic contact point … Scene Slate at Priority 2`. Sequencing (`§7.4`): `1. Succession takes precedence — … pauses other clock resolutions within 1 season. 2. Löwenritter Autonomy resolves next — if Counter reaches 3, Coup fires after Succession declared but before IP effects apply. 3. IP effects apply last`. Torben's `Conviction Continuity … Resonant Style Evidence` (`faction_politics › §1.1d`).

**F-09.12 Hochjarl incapacity assessment** (`faction_politics › §1.3a`): triggers `Physical incapacity | Any Senior Jarl | Witnessed injury or illness lasting ≥ 2 seasons; confirmed by at least 1 other Senior Jarl; Mental incapacity | … 2 consecutive Assembly sessions where … orders are incoherent or contradicted …; witnessed by majority; Prolonged absence | automatic | absent … 3 consecutive sessions without designating a Regent; Conviction collapse | … Certainty reaches 0 AND Conviction Scar count ≥ 3`; process `1. Invocation … Disposition −1 …; 2. Deliberation: All Senior Jarls vote. Majority (3 of 5) required … defense (Social Contest, Ob 2; Success: one Senior Jarl switches vote …); 3. Duration: … minimum of 2 seasons … Regent appointed …; 4. Outcomes: recovers (injury heals, Certainty ≥ 1) → Reinstated; does not recover after 4 seasons → Permanent removal. Succession fires per SUC-01; dies → Immediate succession`; `Vaynard-specific: … Hochjarl … automatically becomes Regent … If no Hochjarl …, the Senior Jarl with highest Military contribution`. "incoherent or contradicted" is not machine-evaluable (**H-33**).

### SET-09 Pipelines

**PL-09.01 Universal Succession Contest.** Entry: leader eliminated at Accounting N. Steps: D-09.01 → smooth (heir inherits; Stability −1) or contest at Accounting N+1 → contenders declare (claim basis; external backing costs Diplomatic Token) → D-09.02 strengths → Stage 1 pairwise from top (D-09.03 via PL-01.02) → Stage 2 D-09.04 → UNIFIED (Stability −1, Mandate −1) / fractious Regency / SPLIT (PL-09.02) / Regency (no contender; retry; 3 failures → collapse). Terminal: leader installed or faction collapsed. Advanced by Accounting. Reversible: split can re-merge (treaty, both Mandate ≥ 3).

**PL-09.02 Faction split.** Entry: SPLIT outcome. Steps: provinces by D-09.07; settlements follow province unless governor Disposition ≥ +3 to a contender; Mandate/Influence 60/40; Wealth 70/30; units by D-09.06; Stability winner −1 / splinter 2; IC NPCs choose (H-19), others by location; names; splinter gets parent victory conditions reset, Stage 2→5 pathway. Terminal: two factions. Reversible: re-merge.

**PL-09.03 Crown Succession Contest & Consecration Crisis.** Entry: Crown eliminated (`Mandate 0 + Loyalty 0`) or Royal Deposition succeeds. Steps: contest at next Accounting among factions with Mandate ≥ 3 meeting candidate conditions (Löwenritter Autonomy ≥ 3; Hafenmark Baralta alive & Mandate ≥ 4 (+2D if positioned); Church CI ≥ 40; Torben if concurrent Shift) → `pool vs Ob 3`, highest net, tie rules (CT-23) → Church wins: Theocratic regency, CI +10, all Stability check Ob 2, secular ranks suspended → Löwenritter wins: Löwenritter Crown, Baralta reverts (Stability −1) → Hafenmark wins: Consecration Crisis D-09.10 (refuse: CI +3, Crown Mandate inherited −2, 3-season validation D-09.11; duress: CI −5, Church Stability −3 floor 0, full inheritance; Arc 11 override H-20; +1 Ob if Torben mature & Disposition ≤ 0) → Hafenmark Option B (D-09.12) → Recognition Ceremony (SET-11 PL-11.12) → Crown rank re-evaluation. Terminal: new Crown. Reversible: contest may reopen (validation failure).

**PL-09.04 Löwenritter graduated autonomy.** Entry: Loyal. Steps: each Accounting evaluate D-09.13 predicates → Restless (T14 fragmentation Ob +1; Crown offensive +1 Ob) → Autonomous (Crown Military reduced by T14 garrison; no Fort 3; PI −1) → Split (T14 Löwenritter; new faction with CT-05 stats; PI −3; Crown Military 2; PV −3; Ehrenwall Split card) → optional Coup (candidate; Crown SUSPENDED; monarch installed; Crown resumes or is absorbed). Reversal at 1–3 by D-09.14. Parallel counter model (D-09.15) fires Martial Law at 4 (or 3). Terminal: Split irreversible without reconquest. → TL-15.

**PL-09.05 Royal Deposition.** Entry: D-09.17 true. Steps: Crown chooses pass Crown to Löwenritter (Split) OR emergency Parliamentary Session `Senator Inward, Ob 3` (success restores legitimacy — magnitude unstated GP-09.19) → neither: Crown eliminated; Coup Counter 4 → PL-09.03. Terminal: Crown transferred/eliminated.

**PL-09.06 Motion of No Confidence / Constitutional Crisis.** Entry: raised by Parliamentarian (Std 4+) or auto on D-09.19. Steps: Parliamentary Vote `Influence vs Crown Mandate` at next Accounting → Crown loses → Confessor concur (Deposal: Mandate → 1, Stability −3; Torben/IP +10/interregnum) or refuse (CI +3, TT +2). Terminal: deposal or refusal. (CT-77 with PL-09.05.)

**PL-09.07 Insurgency pipeline.** Entry: campaign start (Stage 1 runs every arc). Steps: Stage 1 PT decay per D-09.21 → Stage 2 Latent RM on D-09.22 (actions once per season; +1 Ob to Church actions; suppression back to Stage 1) ∥ Stage 3 Insurgency on D-09.23 (holds/invades territory; non-parliamentary limits; GD-2 response) → Stage 4 promotion on D-09.24 (impossible as written) → status by D-09.25 (persistent) → dissolution by D-09.26 (military / sponsor / amnesty / persist) → Promoted Faction collapse via faction_layer §1.5 (absent). Terminal: dissolution to non-existence, or GD-1 victory at 11+ territories. Reversible: only Stage 2 ↔ 1.

**PL-09.08 Faction emergence (Renown ladder).** Entry: player/NPC at Renown 0–2. Steps: Stage 2 (assigned or seized settlement) → Stage 3 (2+ settlements, Renown 5+, 2 officers Disposition +3; partial sheet) → Stage 4 (4+ settlements across 2+ provinces, Renown 7+, 1 Seat; Declaration DA `Influence pool = Renown ÷ 2, Ob 3`; ED-790 stats) → Stage 5 (2+ Seats, Renown 9+; Parliament). Terminal: national faction. Reversible: collapse (PL-09.10).

**PL-09.09 RM Settlement Emergence.** Entry: D-09.28 at a settlement. Steps: RM declares (H: RM as NPC — decision rule absent, **H-34**); governance transfers; Governance Transition scene (SET-06 PL-06.07); province fractionalizes (SET-07); cooldown 4 seasons per province; WR −2 on "RM emergence". Terminal: RM-governed settlement.

**PL-09.10 Faction collapse → city-state → dissolution.** Entry: province lost. Steps: holdouts (Order ≥ 3 & governor Disposition ≥ +3) → last province lost & leader alive in own settlement → city-state (partial sheet; no national DAs) → re-emergence via PL-09.08, or leader killed/captured with no Standing 4+ successor → dissolution (settlements unmanaged; officers free agents). Terminal: dissolved. (CT-46 with Stability-0 elimination.)

**PL-09.11 Generational Shift.** Entry: first of D-09.31. Steps: Succession Declaration Scene (Almud death) takes precedence (pauses other clocks 1 season) → outcome tier by D-09.32 (Stability delta; rival track at ≤ −3) → Autonomy effect D-09.33 (may fire Coup immediately if Counter at 2) → IP effects (Altonian threshold ≤ 4 at IP ≥ 60; diplomatic contact at IP ≥ 75 & Disposition ≤ 0) → Readiness ≥ 7 later: ward exit; challenge to player Std 7 (co-regent if Disposition ≥ +2 else SUC-03). Terminal: Torben active political actor. Three-clock order: Succession → Autonomy → IP.

**PL-09.12 Hochjarl incapacity.** Entry: a trigger (F-09.12). Steps: invocation (Disposition −1) → vote 3 of 5 (defense Social Contest Ob 2 flips one) → removal ≥ 2 seasons, Regent → recovery (reinstated) / 4 seasons (permanent; SUC-01) / death (immediate). Terminal: reinstated or succeeded.

**PL-09.13 Warden faction activation.** Entry: any path A–E condition. Steps: activation; Governance stat D-09.35; acquisition via Mending Sanctuaries (rules absent). Terminal: Warden faction pursuing Peninsular Sovereignty. (GP-09.14.)

### SET-09 Throughlines applied
TL-01 — Insurgency and Promoted Factions explicitly GD-1-eligible; Theocratic regency, Warden "victory path" phrasing retained. TL-05 — split "Mandate 60%/40%", Latent RM authored Mandate, Insurgency Legitimacy float, Founded L/PS violate derived-Mandate (CT-08). TL-08 — FSS-1 uses the resolver; Crown contest, Baralta Claim, Popular Uprising, Reconstitution use dice (CT-03, CT-23). TL-15 — Split irreversible; pipeline unidirectional; WR "ever returned to 0" lock. TL-04 — CT-43. TL-12 — H-18, H-19, H-20, H-21, H-32, H-33, H-34. TL-14 — "Coup Counter" vs "Autonomy" both live (CT-21).

## SET-10 — Kingdom Institutions (Church, Löwenritter, Ministry, Guilds, Parliament)

### SET-10 Flattened statement

**F-10.01 Church of Solmund.** `Holy See = elected from among the Bishopry. Second only to the Monarch.` Four Cardinals: `Fortitude | Commands Knights Templar | Templar deployment; Justice | Grand Adjudication + Inquisitors | Heresy Investigations; excommunication recommendations; Prudence | Tithes collection + Charities | Wealth generation; Temperance | Universities + Monasteries + Observatories | Scholarly institutions; Altonian diplomacy` (`institutions › Four Cardinals`); named `Osten Jarnstal / Arnlod Olafsson / Aldric Tormann / Magnus Klapp` (`worldbuilding › §3.1`; `faction_politics › ED-639`). Standing mechanics (`institutions › Cardinal-specific BG mechanics`): `Fortitude: Templar deployment requires Cardinal of Fortitude to be active (Church Stability ≥ 2). At Stability ≤ 1: Templars do not deploy regardless of card play.; Justice: … If Cardinal of Justice is compromised (via Varfell Intel action on Church …): one Heresy Investigation this season is false (all factions notified …); Prudence: Church Wealth generation: +0.5 Wealth/season from tithed territories (rounds down at Year-End). Territories where Church has Favour ≥ 3 contribute; Temperance: … While Church controls T9 …, Cardinal of Temperance may pursue Altonian diplomatic engagement at Phase 1. Specific hooks TBD` (**GP-10.01**). **CT-39**: `Cardinal BG modifiers replaced with Named Character Event cards … Church no longer gets passive bonuses in BG. Cardinals are event triggers only.` (`worldbuilding › §12`). Schism — **CT-41**: `When Church Stability drops below 3, one Cardinal may challenge the Confessor … acts on their own priority for one season` vs `Cardinal schism trigger: If Church Stability = 2 AND any faction played a Senator action targeting Church this season: one Cardinal (random or GM discretion) challenges the Confessor` (both `institutions`; **H-22**) vs recommendation `Church Stability ≤ 3` (`early_game › §9`). `Cardinal Independence Check: Cardinal acts against explicit Holy See instruction: relevant pool vs Ob 3. Success: action proceeds, Church Stability −1. Failure: blocked.` (`worldbuilding › §3.2`). `Jarnstal Drift (0–3, Game Master-tracked, private): Increments each season Jarnstal deploys Templars without Holy See authorisation. At 3: Church Military controlled by Jarnstal, not the Confessor. Church Stability −2, Church Influence (CI) +2.` (**H-10**) with card `Jarnstal Independence … Counter-play: Church player may spend 1 Influence + 1 Stability to recall Jarnstal and reset the counter. … once per game.` Klapp Awakening (trigger `Thread op within 1 territory of Himmelenger, CI ≥ 30, no Heresy Investigation this season`; `Klapp Active State: +1D all Investigate, Heresy Investigations cost 0 Wealth`; Trajectory A `CI +1, card removed` / B `CI −1 … Discovery Event Roll Church Mandate vs Ob 2 … Prosecute: Stability −1, AER −1, CI −2 / Protect: Himmensendt Renown −1` / C `WC +1, CI −2, Church Stability −1, … one Senate Market Pontifex/season` — `strategic_layer › Scenario A`, worked example). Olafsson Exposure card `Church Stability −2, CI −3. Olafsson removed (Inquisitor operations suspended for 2 seasons). … +1 Ob until replacement … Counter-play: … pre-emptively remove Olafsson (spend 1 Stability at Accounting)`. Prudence Crisis card `Trigger: Church Wealth ≤ 2 | Church Mandate −1 in each territory where Prosperity ≤ 3. Restoration Movement gains +1 Influence in those territories. Counter-play: Crown or Guilds may spend 1 Wealth … in one territory`. Cardinal death (`institutions › Cardinal Death — Succession (ED-336)`): `No hard cap` on deaths per season; `Gap period: The Cardinal's BG mechanic is suspended until Year-End Accounting. Fortitude: Templar deployment requires Legionary card (no free deployment). Justice: Heresy Investigations cannot be issued. Prudence: Tithe income halved. Temperance: Altonian diplomatic action unavailable.`; `At Year-End Accounting, Holy See appoints a replacement Cardinal. The new Cardinal is anonymous … Full mechanics restored immediately`; `Church player may designate the new Cardinal as Focused (PP-430)` (absent — **GP-10.02**). Church Levies — **CT-38**: `Crown may, once per Year-End, raise Church levies: Crown gains Military +1 for one season …; Church Military −1 for that season. Church cannot refuse if Crown L ≥ 4.` (`institutions`) vs `Levy system cut entirely` (`worldbuilding › §12`). College of Prelates (5; `3 of 5 … canonical (Jarnstal, Olafsson, Klapp); 2 need naming (ED-649)`); Dicasteries (`faction_politics › §7.3`): `Defense of the Faith (Fortitude); Doctrinal Adjudication (Justice); Temporal Affairs (Prudence); Doctrine and Archives (Temperance)`, `Each Dicastery has Competence and Corruption tracks` (rules `MIN-02, MIN-03` absent — **GP-10.03**); `A Confessor can rearrange Dicastery jurisdictions (radical act — Stability cost)` (unquantified). Consecration of a Confessor (`§1.4c`): `1. Holy See concurrence (… GM-determined +1 Ob on the Consecration ceremony roll for each year-arc the Church's CI has been below 30 in the preceding 3 year-arcs) 2. College of Prelates unanimity … 3. Consecration ceremony (… mandatory Zoom In)` (**H-23**; the roll's pool/Ob absent). **F-10.02 Löwenritter.** Structure (`institutions › Löwenritter — Canonical Structure (PP-194)`): `Lions' Table: Military arm …; Lions' Helm: Naval arm. (BG: … Löwenritter Helm can deny T10 sea access to Schoenland at IP < 75 if Crown requests.); Riskbreakers: Sub-unit of Löwenritter, NOT independent faction …; Civic Arm: Knights of the Peace: … when Löwenritter is active (any phase), one territory per season has its March Ob −1; Royal Investigators: … once per season, Löwenritter may cancel one successful Intel action targeting Crown`. Riskbreakers: `Pre-Split (Loyal/Restless/Autonomous): … fire under Löwenritter NPC AI Priority Tree (not independent). Post-Split: … once per season, may execute one Priority Tree action at no card cost.` (the Priority Tree itself is absent — **GP-10.04**); `3 tokens per year and a priority tree that fires at Phase 4 start` (`early_game › §2.1`); `hidden Conviction: Valoria (nation as idea). When ordered to act against this conviction: Game Master rolls Riskbreaker Intel vs Ob 2. Success: comply. Failure: refuse or sabotage.` (`worldbuilding › §4.2`, **H-24**); identity (`stats › Riskbreakers — Identity Confirmed`). Deniability Debt (`faction_politics › §2.2b.ii`, `0–7, cap 7`): accrual `Partial result on any Riskbreaker covert mission +1; Failure +2; Evidence trail generated (Intel roll vs Ob 3 after the operation — failure creates evidence) +1; Witnessed by unintended non-asset NPC (GM discretion …) +1; Operation uses Löwenritter resources … and is traced +2; Each Shadow Renown point that spills past cap 10 +1`; reduction `One full season passes with no Riskbreaker operations −1 (… not if Debt ≥ 5); Counter-intelligence operation succeeds (Intel vs Ob 3, once per season, Squad-Leader or higher) −1; Commander spends 1 Shadow Renown to purge records −1 (once per campaign arc); Parliamentary inquiry concluded in Riskbreaker favor … Reset to 2`; thresholds `0–1 No effect; 2 +1 Ob on all Riskbreaker covert actions; 3 Riskbreaker Exposure card triggers … Crown Domain Actions against non-Crown factions +1 Ob; 4 Cooling-off reduction disabled; 5 Parliamentary inquiry opens — Grand Debate (Crown Influence + Mandate at stake); 6 Riskbreaker operations require Commander personal approval. +2 Ob on Rb-Std 1–3 independent operations; 7 (cap) Riskbreaker Commander demotion mandatory at next Seasonal Accounting. All Rb-Std ranks demoted by 1.` (**H-35** GM-discretion witness). Lions' Table Mutiny card `Trigger: Löwenritter Military drops to 2 or below AND Ehrenwall has been removed or neutralised | Löwenritter fragments. Military halved.` Reconstitution (`institutions › Löwenritter — Reconstitution (ED-331)`): `Senator Inward (Löwenritter only, post-Split). Available only when PI = 0. Roll: L vs Ob 3. Once per season. OW | PI restored to 2 … S | PI restored to 1 … P | PI = 0. Stability −1. F | PI = 0. Stability −1. Church L +1`. Year-End: `Löwenritter (if active): raise one free unit; Prosperity −1` (`phases`). Reactive guidance (`stats › PP-238`, H-17). Crown covert delegation (`stats › PP-241`): `roll uses Lowenritter Intel pool. The Crown covert +1 Ob penalty does NOT apply … Political consequences … fall on Crown`. Ladder (SET-11).

**F-10.03 Ministry.** Identity: `Ministers nominated by Parliament, confirmed by Monarch. Named ministries: Ministry of Law, Ministry of Guilds, Ministry of Logothetes, Ministry of Granaries, Ministry of Pure Water` (`institutions › Ministry — Canonical Identity`). `Ministry is always NPC-controlled. It has no player victory condition and cannot be captured` (`ministry.md › Ethical Framework`). Stats `L 3 | Influence 4 | Wealth 2 | Military 0 | Stability 5` (`PS not tracked`). Tokens: `Administrative Presence tokens (AP-tokens …). One AP-token per territory … Starts with AP-tokens in T14 (Ehrenfeld), T2 (Kronmark), T5 (Feldmark), T1 (Valorsplatz). (PP-204 corrected.)` PI mechanics: `Ministry Stabilisation (fires at Accounting Step 11 …): Each season Ministry has an AP-token in T1 (Valorsplatz, Parliament seat): PI degradation from Crown Emergency Powers is reduced by 1 (to a minimum of −0 …)` (**CT-70** step 11 dissolved; **CT-33** T1 here, `T13` in every other rule: `Consul Inward (Govern) in T13`, `AP-token in T13: Hafenmark Parliamentary Manoeuvre Ob −1 … absent from T13: … +1`, `Church Territorial Seizure of T1 … If seizure succeeds …: Ministry AP-token in T13 is removed`, `Löwenritter Coup fires: Ministry AP-tokens in T13 and T12 are removed`); `Legislative Record: At each Year-End Accounting: … Any Parliamentary Manoeuvre that succeeded (Hafenmark) this year is recorded as a Parliamentary Ruling … the first time each year …, PI +1`; countersignature `If Ministry L < 2 …: Crown Policy actions cost +1 Ob … If Ministry L = 0: Crown Policy actions unavailable`; seizure of Parliament seat `seizure Ob +1 … AP-token … removed. All Crown Policy actions are now +1 Ob until Ministry reestablishes presence … (requires 1 season of Ministry NPC action …)`; coup `Ministry L −2. PI −3 … Ministry Stabilisation does not fire next season … re-establish AP-tokens … at rate of 1/season`. Priority tree (`Phase 4, Priority 4 — Domain Actions tier`): `1 | PI ≤ 3 | Consul Inward (Govern) in T13: roll Ministry L (3D) vs Ob 1. Success: PI +1; 2 | T13 has no Ministry AP-token | Consul Inward in T13: … Success: AP-token placed in T13; 3 | Any territory with AP-token has PI loss pending from Church Seizure | … procedural objection: Church Seizure in that territory delayed 1 season. No roll required. AP-token … consumed; 4 | Crown L ≥ 4 AND PI < 5 | Senator Inward (Decree support): PI +1 …; 5 (default) | … Consul Inward in highest-Prosperity uncontested territory with AP-token: Prosperity maintained.` Conflict redirect (`parliament › PP-480`). Corrupt Ministry: `Consul Outward, any faction, Ob = floor(Ministry L / 2) + 1: Success: Ministry NPC Priority 4 fires in favour of the corrupting faction this season …; Overwhelming: As above + Ministry AP-token in one territory of choice acts as if that territory is the corrupting faction's capital for one season (−1 Ob on all their actions there); Failure: … Corrupting faction Stability −1. Ministry sends record to Riskbreakers (Riskbreaker Priority 6 now includes the corrupting faction's territory).` Collapse: `Ministry Collapse (L 0): Ministry ceases NPC actions for 2 seasons. All Ministry AP-tokens removed. During collapse: Crown Policy +1 Ob, Parliamentary Manoeuvre +1 Ob, all Hafenmark Deed 3 (Parliamentary Consolidation) checks suspended. Collapse exit: Hafenmark or Crown plays Govern Inward in T13 (Ob 2). Success: Ministry L returns to 1, AP-token placed in T13, collapse ends.` Ministry of Guilds: `If Ministry L ≥ 2 AND Guilds NPC is active: Guilds may not be targeted by Economic Leverage from other factions without a free Ministry counter (… +1 Ob …). If Ministry L ≤ 1: … all Trade Ob in Guilds-controlled territories +1`. Parliament Nomination (`institutions`): `Once per Year-End: Parliament (= Hafenmark Parliamentary Manoeuvre success this year + Crown not in Emergency Powers) may nominate a Ministerial agenda. Choose one: Ministry of Law agenda: Ministry L +1. All Heresy Investigation Obs +1; Ministry of Guilds agenda: Guilds NPC Wealth +1. Ministry receives 1 Wealth; Ministry of Logothetes agenda: All faction Intel orders next season: −1 Ob … Ministry Influence +1 for 1 season. Crown must confirm: if Crown L ≥ 3, confirmation is automatic. If Crown L < 3: roll L vs Ob 2. Failure: nomination blocked.` Ministry Collapse card (`worldbuilding › §6.3`): `Trigger: Crown Stability drops to 2 or below. Law: no legal proceedings …; Taxation: Crown Wealth −1; Guilds: Guilds Wealth +1 …; Water/Granaries: one Crown territory −1 Prosperity. Counter-play: Crown spends 1 Influence to restore Ministry at next Accounting.` Crown Ministries (`faction_politics › §7.1`): `Haushalt, Kriegsamt, Kirchenamt, Gerichtsamt, Schattendienst, Markamt` + proposed `Hochschule … Seasonal cost 1 Wealth` (MIN-01..06 absent — GP-10.03). Ministry as subnational manager `Order decay −1` (`settlement_layer › §3.3`).

**F-10.04 Guilds.** NPC (`core`); stats `3 | 3 | 4 | 6 | 2 | 4 | 5`; `Guilds CP-Token Starting Positions (PP-203): T11, T8, T3, T1, T9` (`ministry.md`). Economic Leverage (`stats › Guilds — Economic Leverage`): `Trigger: Guild Favour ≥ 5 in target territory (1–7 territory track). Resolution: d+σ resolver — contested: M = Wealth − target faction's Wealth. OW | Target loses 1 Wealth + 1 Prosperity in that territory; S | Target faction loses 1 Wealth for 1 season; F | Guild Favour −1 in that territory. Cannot target factions in territories where Guild Favour < 5.` Cards (`worldbuilding › §5.3, §10`): `Guild Schism | Trigger: Guilds Stability drops to 2 or below | Wealth −1, Influence −1 for 1 season. One territory Guild Favour → 0. Counter-play: any faction may spend 1 Influence to mediate (Guilds Stability +1, mediating faction gains +1 Influence with Guilds for 1 season)`; `Guild Forum Revolt | Trigger: Crown raises Guild taxation (Crown plays Economic Leverage or Policy Instrument targeting Guilds) AND Guilds Stability ≤ 3 | Guilds withdraw from Ministry oversight. Crown loses Ministry of Guilds benefits …. Guilds Wealth +1 (no taxes). Restoration Movement gains +1D Influence in Guild-heavy territories`. Structure: `Guildmaster Council as collective leadership`; `Burgher status: Guild Masters and Free Masters qualify for political participation`; `Journeymen Years: … 3+ territories … over 2+ seasons. Qualifies for Free Master. Broadens Circles (+1D guild contacts)`. Guild ladder (SET-11). "Guild-heavy territories", "Crown raises Guild taxation" via Policy Instrument — undefined classifier (**GP-10.05**).

**F-10.05 Parliament.** PI (SET-03). Bodies (`worldbuilding › §6.1`): `Imperial Court | Advisory; Court Parliament | Recommends policies, nominates Ministers/Rectorates/Magistrates; Ministries; Rectorates | Execute law; Praefectures | Administer regions` [reference]. Parliamentary Manoeuvre (Hafenmark): `Ob floor(opponent Influence / 2) + 1` (`core`); `PI +1` on success (`parliament`); `Ob −1/+1` by Ministry token (`ministry`); `+1 Ob` at PI 3–4; lost at PI ≤ 2; interrupt scope P-19; `RDT 1 | Parliamentary Manoeuvre targeting CI: −1 Ob`. Parliamentary Vote: `Influence vs Crown Mandate (existing §8.11)` (`worldbuilding`); `Parliament votes = current Mandate (faction_layer §5.3)` (`settlement_layer › §1.8`) with CI weights (SET-04); `Parliamentarian-rank actors (Std 4+) are the direct participants` (`faction_politics › Part 11`). Motions named: `Censure −1 [Mandate]; Outlawry −2; Blockade −2 [Wealth]/season; Embargo` (`ci_political › §4.1`; faction_layer §5 absent — **GP-10.06**); `Motion of Censure` as demotion cause. Emergency Powers (`PI −1`; `Crown not in Emergency Powers` condition) — activation rule absent (**GP-10.07**). Committees (`faction_politics › §7.2`): `Supreme Judiciary | … | 0; Trade & Guild Relations | … | 1 Wealth/season; Defense | 1 Wealth; Foreign Affairs | 1 Wealth; Constitutional Review | Almstedt chairs permanently | 0; Internal Affairs | 1 Wealth`; `Competence (0–3) and Corruption (0–3) tracks identical to Crown Ministries … unfunded Committees decay … funding allocation is itself a Parliamentary vote`. Diplomatic Alignment (SET-14). Crown Policy → Opposition → Censor (SET-01 PL-01.04). `Parliamentary Rebuttal — Overwhelming → +1 effect retained` (`stats`; the action absent). Parliamentary Stay (SET-07). Varfell equivalent: Jarl Assembly councils (`faction_politics › §7.4`): `Council of the March; Highlands; Skald; Warden; Voices` with `Readiness Track (0–3) that declines without session and increases when the Council acts. Readiness 0: … defaults to Vaynard's personal decision. Readiness 3: … chair may authorize action … without Vaynard's approval` (rates unstated — **GP-10.08**).

**F-10.06 Named Character Event Cards — catalogue** (`worldbuilding › §10`), triggers verbatim: `Klapp Awakening | Church | Thread op within 1 territory of Himmelenger, CI ≥ 30, no Heresy Investigation this season; Ehrenwall Split | Löwenritter | Autonomy track reaches Split; Riskbreaker Exposure | Crown | Deniability Debt reaches 3/5; Jarnstal Independence | Church | Jarnstal Counter reaches 3 | Church Military splits. Church Stability −2, CI +2; Olafsson Exposure | Church | Overwhelming Intel action vs Church in a settlement with intelligence-broker presence | Church Stability −2, CI −3. Inquisitor operations suspended 2 seasons; Prudence Crisis | Church | Church Wealth ≤ 2 | …; Lions' Table Mutiny | Löwenritter | Military ≤ 2 AND Ehrenwall removed | Löwenritter fragments. Military halved; Guild Schism | Guilds | Stability ≤ 2 | …; Guild Forum Revolt | Guilds | Crown taxation action AND Guilds Stability ≤ 3 | …; Constitutional Crisis | Crown | Mandate ≤ 1 OR loses 3+ territories in one season | …; Ministry Collapse | Crown | Stability ≤ 2 | One Ministry ceases function for 1 season.` (§6.3 text says the four listed sub-effects, not "one Ministry" — minor internal inconsistency). Cards are state-triggered, not drawn; the "deck" is a trigger table.

**F-10.07 Schoenland / Altonia.** `T16 Schoenland | Neutral NPC`; `Schoenland | Active spoiler | Various faction disruptions` (`stats › Faction NPC Trigger Conditions`); `Schoenland demands 1 Wealth at Year-End OR blocks next Trade` on Partial Trade (`strategic_layer › Scenario C`); `Almaic Kyriakos envoy` at `IP 50: Church chooses cooperate (IP −3, CI +2) or refuse` (`worldbuilding › §3.6`, `[FLAG]`). No AI or stat block (GP-08.13).

**Gaps.** GP-10.01 Temperance hooks. GP-10.02 PP-430 Focused. GP-10.03 MIN-01..06 / Competence-Corruption rules. GP-10.04 Riskbreaker/Löwenritter priority tree. GP-10.05 taxation/Guild-heavy classifier. GP-10.06 Parliamentary motions procedure. GP-10.07 Emergency Powers. GP-10.08 Readiness rates. GP-10.09 "Hafenmark Deed 3 (Parliamentary Consolidation)" — Deed system dissolved. GP-10.10 Riskbreaker Priority 6. GP-10.11 Ministry "highest-Prosperity uncontested territory" — "uncontested" undefined. GP-10.12 Klapp mechanics exist only inside a worked scenario.

### SET-10 Pipelines

**PL-10.01 Cardinal death → gap → replacement.** Entry: Cardinal killed (Zoom In scene). Steps: arm mechanic suspended until Year-End (gap effects D-10.01/02, HI unavailable, Altonian action unavailable) → Year-End: Holy See appoints anonymous replacement → mechanics restored → Church may designate Focused next season (PP-430 absent). Terminal: replaced. Multiple deaths independent.

**PL-10.02 Cardinal schism / independence.** Entry: D-10.04 (contradictory). Steps: one Cardinal (random / GM — H-22) challenges → `acts on their own priority for one season` → `That Cardinal's faction sub-action fires as an NPC Priority regardless of the Church player's card plays`; independent action vs Holy See instruction → D-10.03. Jarnstal path: Drift +1 per unauthorised Templar deployment → at 3 D-10.05 (Jarnstal Independence card) → Church may recall once per game (`1 Influence + 1 Stability`). Terminal: schism season / Military split.

**PL-10.03 Church Levies.** Entry: Year-End, Crown chooses. Steps: D-10.06 applied for one season. Terminal: reverts. (CT-38.)

**PL-10.04 Deniability Debt ladder.** Entry: Riskbreaker operation resolves. Steps: accrue per D-10.07 → thresholds: 2 (+1 Ob) → 3 (Exposure card; Crown DAs +1 Ob) → 4 (no cooling) → 5 (Parliamentary inquiry Grand Debate; win → reset 2) → 6 (Commander approval; +2 Ob low ranks) → 7 (Commander demotion; all ranks −1; new Commander). Reduction paths D-10.07. Terminal: none (cycles).

**PL-10.05 Löwenritter Reconstitution.** Entry: PI = 0, Löwenritter post-Split, once per season. Steps: `L vs Ob 3` → D-10.08. Terminal: PI 1–2 restored (Manoeuvre re-enabled) or Stability −1.

**PL-10.06 Ministry NPC turn.** Entry: Phase 4, Domain tier. Steps: evaluate priorities 1→5 (D-10.14), first true fires (priorities 1–2 `L (3D) vs Ob 1`; 3 no roll, consumes token; 4 PI +1; 5 Prosperity maintained) → conflict redirect (PP-480) → if corrupted this season, priority 4 fires for corrupting faction. Terminal: action resolved. Ministry Stabilisation and Legislative Record at Accounting/Year-End.

**PL-10.07 Corrupt Ministry.** Entry: any faction plays Consul Outward on Ministry. Steps: D-10.13 → S/OW/F effects (F → Riskbreaker Priority 6 includes corruptor's territory — absent tree). Terminal: one-season effect.

**PL-10.08 Ministry Collapse.** Entry: Ministry L reaches 0. Steps: 2 seasons no actions; tokens removed; Crown Policy +1 Ob; Manoeuvre +1 Ob; exit when Hafenmark or Crown `Govern Inward in T13 (Ob 2)` succeeds → L 1, token in T13. Terminal: collapse ends.

**PL-10.09 Parliament Ministry Nomination.** Entry: Year-End; Manoeuvre succeeded this year; Crown not in Emergency Powers. Steps: choose agenda (Law / Guilds / Logothetes) → Crown confirm (D-10.16) → effects. Terminal: agenda applied (durations per agenda).

**PL-10.10 Guild cards.** Entry: D-10.18. Steps: Schism → `Wealth −1, Influence −1 for 1 season; one territory Favour → 0`; mediation option (1 Influence → Stability +1, +1 Influence with Guilds 1 season). Forum Revolt → withdraw from Ministry; Guilds Wealth +1; Crown loses Guild tax; RM +1D Influence in Guild-heavy territories. Terminal: card resolved; re-oversight rule absent (GP-10.13).

**PL-10.11 Martial Law.** Entry: Coup fires (Counter model). Steps: every non-Military DA in Crown territories requires Löwenritter Military check `TN 7, Ob 2`; removal `Influence vs Ob = Löwenritter Military ÷ 2 (min 3)` or CI < 40. Terminal: lifted.

**PL-10.12 Consecration of a Confessor.** Entry: Confessor-Presumptive (Std 7). Steps: Holy See concurrence (GM +1 Ob per low-CI year-arc, H-23) → College unanimity → ceremony Zoom In (roll unspecified) → within 2 seasons or position defaults to next College-majority candidate. Under duress variant when Baralta requires consecration (PL-09.03). Terminal: Confessor.

**PL-10.13 Event card resolution.** Entry: D-10.22 predicate true at Accounting (timing unstated — GP-10.14). Steps: apply effect; offer counter-play (costs); mark fired (once? unstated). Terminal: effect in force for stated duration.

### SET-10 Throughlines applied
TL-12 — H-10, H-22, H-23, H-24, H-35. TL-14 — "Church Influence (CI) +2" in Jarnstal card = clock (fine); "Church Mandate −1 in each territory" (Prudence Crisis) — Mandate per territory does not exist (TL-05 violation). TL-07 — Prudence Crisis / Olafsson `CI −3` within cap. TL-17 — "once per game", "once per campaign arc", "per year" units. TL-13 — Solmund naming asserted (`institutions`), no violations in this corpus (the only "Galbadian" strings are quotations of the violation being fixed in `faction_politics › §4.1`).
