## SET-11 — Player Character Agency, Standing, Renown & Rank

### SET-11 Flattened statement

**F-11.01 Convictions.** `Three Convictions at any time. Each Conviction is one sentence: a subject, an action or stance, and an implicit obstacle.` Creation: `write 3 Convictions. One should relate to a personal goal, one to a faction concern, one to a relationship or NPC.` Momentum: `Pursuing a Conviction in a scene grants +1 Momentum. The action must be a genuine step toward the Conviction, not a retroactive justification. Momentum is spent for +1D or to invoke a Stunt.` (**H-25**: "genuine step" is a judgment). `Fulfillment grants a one-time +2 Momentum`. Strain: `Three strains on one Conviction: it must transform or be abandoned.` Resolution states (`player_agency › §2.3`): `Fulfilled | achieved | +2 Momentum …; Failed | pursued but circumstances prevented | +1 Momentum …; Transformed | evolved | +2 Momentum. … new Conviction (does not count against the 3-Conviction cap for one season); Unresolved | abandoned without pursuit | No Momentum … If all 3 … Unresolved, … "drift" warning`. `Sufficient Scope gating: … only counts for Portrait and retirement purposes if the player took ≥ 2 scene actions pursuing or responding to the Conviction.` Campaign form (`campaign_architecture › §7.3`): `Fulfilled — ≥2 scene actions … external world state aligned; Failed — … (≥1 scene action); Transformed — … (≥1 Conviction-adjacent scene …); Unresolved …; Deliberate self-contradiction does not produce valid resolution (exploit gate …)` (**H-26**). Validator (`§4.2 Step 4`): match categories `Named NPCs; Faction references; Territory references (T1-T17 + colloquial names); System keywords (~25) … ; Role references (11 mappings: "the king" → Almud; "the Confessor" → Himlensendt; "the duchess" → Baralta; "the duke" → Vaynard (or Maret Uln post-succession); "the queen" → Lenneth; "the prince" → Torben; "the princess" → Elske; "the grandmaster" → Ehrenwall; "the spymaster" → Thale; "the lord treasurer" → Reichard; "the royal marshal" → Voss)`; `Strong match: capitalized exact-name; Weak match: lowercase …; 0 matches: … warning; Weak match only: … suggestion …; Strong match: no warning`. Duty/Conviction contradiction: `the player must choose which to pursue in any scene where both are relevant`.

**F-11.02 Duties.** `At the start of each season, the player's faction leader evaluates the faction's AI priority stack and assigns one Duty … based on the highest-priority unaddressed need that matches the player's capabilities` (location / skills / urgency). `Duty assignment requires Standing ≥ 1. At Standing 0 (Petitioner), the player receives the Initiation Duty only`. Types (`§3.3`): `Investigate | … | Evidence Track reaches threshold | Rival action proceeds undetected; Diplomacy | … | Improve Disposition with target NPC to threshold | …; Governance | Territory Accord declining | Maintain or improve Accord | Territory may Revolt; Protection | … | Prevent assassination, arrest, or seizure | NPC lost …; Reconnaissance | … | Survey territory, identify threats | …; Subversion | … | Reduce target territory Accord or target NPC Disposition | Player's cover blown; Exposure consequences; Thread Operation | (Crown/Varfell only) | Mend Gap, restore MS, or advance VTM | …; Escort | … | Move NPC/asset … without interception | …` (thresholds unspecified — **GP-11.01**; VTM struck). Completion: `Success: Standing +1 …; Failure: Standing −1, floor-protected above Standing 1 …; Exceeding: … Standing +2` (**H-28**: "additional value" is a judgment; examples `discovered a conspiracy, turned an enemy NPC, generated Casus Belli`). Unpursued Duty-aligned scene: `Duty not completed. Standing −1 at Accounting` (`§4.5`).

**F-11.03 Scene Slate.** Deterministic generation (`§4.2`). Anchoring: `Every Scene Slate entry specifies a settlement … Province-level events … anchor to the province's Seat settlement by default … Travel within a province is free; travel between provinces costs 1 scene action per province traversed.` Step 1 Mandatory (Priority 0): `Player is in or adjacent to a territory at Accord 0; Player is the target of an active Heresy Investigation; A mass battle is occurring in the player's territory; Player's faction leader is assassinated, overthrown, or incapacitated`; ordering hint `1. Faction Leader Removal 2. Heresy Investigation Target 3. Stability Crisis 4. Mass Battle 5. Knot Partner in Crisis 6. Companion Arc Trigger 7. Settlement Revolt / Accord 0 8. Rank Advancement Recognition Event` (items 3, 5, 6, 8 are not in the Step 1 condition list — **GP-11.02**). Witness Mode when mandatory count > budget: `One free Read or Appraise action — single roll required …; Ob 1 …; One narrative input opportunity … (one sentence, GM may incorporate or reject; videogame: pre-scripted dialogue branch tagged to player Conviction) (**H-27**, videogame variant stated); … resolution proceeds via NPC AI …; does NOT generate Domain Echo; does NOT consume Momentum or Coherence`. Step 2 Crisis (Priority 1): `territory within 2 adjacencies … at Accord ≤ 1; NPC with Disposition ≥ +1 … Scar count ≥ 2; Knotted NPC arc branch trigger; global clock crossed a band threshold this season (MS, CI, IP); NPC with Disposition ≥ +2 has an active Conviction that conflicts with their faction's current priority`. Step 2b Thread-State: `MS ≤ 20 in current territory → Thread Crisis (1, mandatory); MS dropped below threshold band → Threshold-Crossing (2); Active Gap → Investigation/Mending Opportunity (2); Active Lock → Governance Rigidity (3); WC advanced → Warden Cooperation (3); Max 1 … Highest priority fires`. Step 3 Duty-aligned (1–2 scenes). Step 4 Conviction-aligned (max 3). Step 5 NPC Outreach (Disposition ≥ +2 & Conviction relevant & priority-tree action; or Disposition ≤ −2 & authority & action vs player's faction → summons; `Declining a demand: Disposition −1 …, +1 Exposure`). Step 6 Territorial (1–2). Step 7 Ambient (1). Sizes: `Normal: 5–7 opportunities, 4 scene actions; Hard: 7–9, 3; Narrative: 4–5, 5`. Pruning (`§4.3`): mandatory first; `remaining_slots = slate_target_size − count(mandatory)`; `≤ 0 → mandatory-only. Witness Mode`; else sort `(step_number ascending, internal_index ascending)`; internal indices `Step 2: proximity …, then trigger order; 2b: table order; 3: … target territory proximity; 4: Conviction declaration order; 5: NPC Disposition descending, then Renown descending; 6: trigger condition order; 7: single`. `slate_target_size` within the 5–7 band is not fixed (**GP-11.03**). Unpursued consequences (`§4.5`): `NPC arc moment | NPC resolves …; Territory crisis | Faction AI … Govern roll …; Clock threshold | propagate; Duty-aligned | Standing −1 at Accounting; NPC Outreach | Disposition −1 if NPC Disposition was ≥ +3; NPC Demand | Disposition −1 … +1 Exposure … escalation at NPC discretion per priority tree; Territorial | lost; Ambient | none`. Presentation: NPC, location, description, tags, priority.

**F-11.04 Standing ladder (0–7) and rank mechanics.** `Standing 0 Petitioner … 1 Operative … 2 Agent … 3 Counselor … 4 Lieutenant … 5 Senior … 6 Prince/Chancellor/Senior-Jarl/Cardinal … 7 Regent-Designate` (`player_agency › §5.1`); `Standing 2 — faction intelligence access; 3 — council observation, specialty branch selection, Formal Recognition Event required; 4 — NPC officer sub-commands, +1 scene action; 5 — treaty-level standing, inner-circle adjacency; 6 — inner-circle voting, +2 scene actions, Hall Tier residency; 7 — succession-eligible, Regent-Designate authority` (`§3.4`). **CT-19**: `Standing (0–5)` (`§5.4`), `Standing: Cannot exceed 5` (`strategic_layer › P-15`), `Standing track (0–5 per faction)` and `5 (Successor)` (`settlement_layer › §6.1, §3.2`), `Standing 5 enables succession` (`player_agency › §8`) coexist with 0–7 (and `−1` Dismissed). Skyrim Eight per rank (`faction_politics › §1.0`): Title, Initiation Gate, Access, Obligations, Hall Tier, Livery, Mentor (`+1D on one specified roll per season while the mentor is alive and at Disposition ≥ 0`), Demotion Trigger; cross-rank: Rival Cohort (Std 2–4; `Rival Standing 3 … triggers a pre-emption challenge`), Rank-Restricted Dialogue (`Minimum Engagement Scale`), Dismissal (`Dishonored flag`). Initiation Duties: Crown `Carry the King's Word — deliver a formal correspondence … across at least one provincial boundary … successful fieldwork (Interview or Observe) in both origin and destination, and no Exposure event fires`; Hafenmark `File the Petition — … accepted for consideration … one successful fieldwork action …, one social scene with a sitting Burgher at Disposition ≥ 0, … genuine civic matter`; Varfell `Answer the Muster — … one military operation … Survival is sufficient; glory is exceeding`; Church `Witness the Solmund Codex — … travel to Himmelenger T9, social attendance, and one doctrinal Interview passed`. Per-faction advancement gates (numeric extracts): Crown `Std 2: 2 Duties (one outside origin province); Std 3: Formal Recognition Event + branch; Std 4: 2 Banneret Duties + Public Deed (Renown +2); Std 5: 3 Lieutenant Duties + Recognized Deed (+1 Legitimizing Authority token); Std 6: all 5 inner-circle NPCs Disposition ≥ 0; Std 7: Succession Declaration Scene OR 3-of-5 inner-circle supermajority`; Hafenmark `Std 2: 2 Duties + Burgher's Examination (oral, Ob 2, 3 sitting Burghers); Std 3: council vote 2/3 supermajority, no outstanding Obligations with rival factions; Std 4: carried motion (mass social contest) + 3 Aldermanic Duties; Std 5: 3 Duties + committee passes 2 motions + Recognized Deed; Std 6: all 4 IC ≥ 0 via formal Parliamentary vote; Std 7: Baralta AND Parliament endorsement`; Varfell `Std 2: 2 Duties + mass battle S/OW; Std 3: Land-Grant + 3 Thane Duties incl. one Exceeding; Std 4: defended settlement 2 year-arcs OR adjacent settlement OR named battle victory; Std 5: raised by a Jarl OR unanimous Senior Lendmänner + 3 Duties + Recognized Deed; Std 6: 4 year-arcs as Jarl OR 2 campaigns + unanimous Senior Jarls ≥ 0; Std 7: Assembly election, 3 of 5 Senior Jarls ≥ +1 + Deed/Grand Contest`; Church `Std 2: 2 Duties + Scriptural Examination Ob 2; Std 3: Investiture by Cardinal + 3 Duties + one Thread reporting with Inquisitorial follow-up; Std 4: 3 Duties + Public Doctrinal Defense Ob 2 + Cardinal endorsement; Std 5: all 5 Prelates ≥ 0 + Recognized Deed (or AEA ≥ 3 — track absent); Std 6: vacancy + College 2/3 + 4 year-arcs as Prelate + endorsement; Std 7: Confessor vacancy + College majority`. Obligations examples: Crown Std 6 `remain in Valorsplatz for at least 2 of 4 seasons per year`; Hafenmark Std 4 `abstention costs Standing −1`; Varfell Std 5 `missed [Assembly] attendance … Standing −1`; Church Std 4 `Church Attention Pool reaching 5 … without response` demotes (CT-54). Banner (`§1.1c`): `+1D on all Crown-context Intimidate rolls; −1D on all Subterfuge rolls while visible`. Branch switching: `demotion to Standing 3`. Demotion magnitude (`§1.0a`): default one rank; severe: `Public scandal: drop 2 ranks, minimum Standing 1; Heresy Investigation … Tribunal but not full Excommunication: drop 2 ranks; if rank 5+, drop to Standing 3; Faction-faction defection …: drop 3 ranks or to Standing 1, whichever is higher`; total: `Excommunication: Standing drops to −1 (Dismissed-with-Dishonor …); Treason …; Permanent Conviction shift to opposing-framework Conviction: … Standing 0; Cardinal … catastrophic event — … falls to Standing 3 … UNLESS … Heresy or Treason`; flow `does NOT pass through intermediate ranks … Hall Tier and Livery … resync … within 1 season`; appeal `within 2 seasons …: faction-internal Piety Track (Standard Contest), Adjudicator = supervising Standing-7 NPC … Successful appeal reverses … no re-appeal in same campaign year. … unavailable for Standing 0 (Dismissal)` ("Piety Track (Standard Contest)" as a contest type is a garbled reference — **GP-11.04**). Sub-office ladders (`Part 2`): Löwenritter 0–7 (Squire→Grand Master); Riskbreaker 0–6 (Candidate→Commander; entry `Knight-Commander rank minimum … Conviction-check … Intel vs Ob 2, Overwhelming required`); Inquisitor 0–5; Templar 0–5; Guild 0–6 (`Std 6 … extraordinary and normally omitted`; Masterpiece Examination `Ob 2`); Niflhel per-arm 0–6/7 (`Broker: Standing 4 in two arms … +1D on all Niflhel-related Intel rolls … Thread Tension +0.5 per season`); Warden `0 Thread-Touched TS ≥ 15; 1 Apprentice-Warden TS ≥ 30 …; 2 Warden 2 ops, TS ≥ 40; 3 Senior TS ≥ 50, 4 ops, trained 1; 4 Warden-Captain TS ≥ 60, completed Southernmost Expedition; 5 Warden-Senior TS ≥ 70, 6 year-arcs; 6 Designate; 7 Warden-Chief`. `A sub-office rank is not a primary-faction rank … tracked separately`. Parity (`Part 9`): `Std 3 +0 scene actions, 1 NPC officer delegation …; 4 +1, 2 NPC officers; 5 +1; 6 +2; 7 +2; succession-eligible`. CI × rank (SET-04 F-04.04).

**F-11.05 Leadership acquisition.** `If the player is Standing 7 …: they are offered leadership … issue Domain Actions directly, play the faction's card hand, and lose the Duty system …; Standing 5–6: succession contest per SUC-01 through SUC-03 …; below Standing 5: … canonical NPC rules`. `Leadership can also be seized: a Standing 4+ character can call a leadership challenge at any time via social contest against the current leader, with the faction council as expert adjudicator (Cognition-based). Success: player becomes faction leader. Failure: Standing drops to 2, Disposition with deposed leader drops to −4.` (`player_agency › §5.2`; social contest procedure absent — **GP-11.05**; SUC-01..03 absent — GP-09.15). Independent path (`§5.3`): no Duties, Convictions only, `no faction stat pool … until Renown 7+`, no Parliament/military. Recognition Ceremony (`faction_politics › §6.4`): `player at Hafenmark Std 7 … provisional Crown Std 5 … if majority [of 5 IC] accepts, … permanent Crown Std 5. If majority rejects, … Crown Std 2 …, and their Hafenmark rank reverts to Chancellor (Std 6)`; Crown officers under Baralta `Disposition ≥ 0 … remains …; If negative, … demoted to Std 3 (Banneret) within 1 season`.

**F-11.06 Renown (0–10) and Shadow Renown.** Sources (`§5.4`): `Conviction resolved (Fulfilled or Transformed) +1; Duty exceeded +1; Domain Echo produced +1; NPC arc influenced +1 (Conviction Scar); Investigation resolved (Complex+) +1 (Evidence Track threshold 5+); Mass battle participated +1; Territory Accord improved +1; Knot formed +1`; `Cap: +2 Renown per season maximum. Renown does not decay naturally.` Effects: `3+ | NPCs at neutral Disposition start at +1 instead of 0; 5+ | +1D on Impress actions. Cross-faction Domain Action suggestions; 7+ | Independent Domain Action: floor(Renown ÷ 2) as pool; 9+ | May call Grand Contest at any time. All factions evaluate player at Priority 2`. Governance risk (SET-06 D-06.23). Caste (`faction_politics › §3.3`): `Southern Einhir: Renown gains from public actions in Northern/Central Einhir territories are halved (round down)`. Shadow Renown (`faction_politics › §2.2b.i`): sources `Covert mission succeeds +1; Covert assassination / sabotage success +1 (cap once/target/season); Intelligence delivered … verified actionable +1; NPC recruited as Shadow Asset +1; Black-market trade closed at Overwhelming +1; Riskbreaker promotion event +1`; `Cap: +2 … per season … does not decay`; effects `3+ Covert NPCs … +1; 5+ +1D on Interrogate, Extort, and Black-Market-Contact; 7+ Independent covert Domain Action: pool = floor(Shadow Renown ÷ 2) …; 9+ May initiate an Exposure Event`; `citing converts the spend to public Renown at a 2:1 loss, and inflicts +1 Deniability Debt for each citation. Shadow Renown above the cap (10) spills into Deniability Debt at 1:1`; exposure: `each +1 Shadow Renown earned from the now-public action converts to −1 public Renown (Infamy penalty) and +1 Deniability Debt`.

**F-11.07 Scene actions & Resources.** Budget: `Narrative 5 | 4–5; Normal 4 | 5–7; Hard 3 | 7–9`; modifiers `Standing 4–5: +1 …; Standing 6–7: +2 …; Knot with a local NPC: +1 scene action in that NPC's territory; Out of Breath (Stamina 0): −1; Wounded (2+ Wounds): −1`; `Scenes that require extended engagement … may consume 2 scene actions` (**H-36**: which scenes). Resources (`§9`): `0 Destitute … 5 Magnate`; starting `Crown officer: 2. Hafenmark merchant: 3. Varfell scholar: 2. Church functionary: 2. RM organizer: 1. Independent: 1`; sources `Faction salary (+1/season at Standing 2+, +2 at Standing 4+). Settlement governance (+1/season per settlement with Prosperity ≥ 3). Trade action (… Ob varies by settlement Trade stat, in Port/City). Guild contracts (+1 to +3, one-time). Loot (+1 per valuable). Gifts received (+1)`; uses `Equipment (1). Bribe/gift (1). Hire agent for 1 season (2). Fund settlement development +1D (2). Military equipment upgrade (3). Sponsor settlement event, Order +1 (1). Economic Leverage via personal Resources (3)`. Stipends in ranks (`1 Wealth/year-arc`, `2 Wealth/year-arc`) use faction Wealth units, not Resources (**GP-11.06**).

**F-11.08 Caste integration** (`faction_politics › Part 3`). Castes: `Northern Einhir … unstigmatized; baseline low TS …; Central Einhir …; Southern Einhir (T4 Grauwald, T6 Stillhelm, T13 Oastad; …) stigmatized; higher baseline TS`. Advancement matrix (Southern column): Crown `Std 0→3 possible …; Std 3→4 requires Public Deed that mitigates caste (Exceeding-level …); Std 5→6 requires Extraordinary Circumstances (… burns ~3 Disposition with a rival IC NPC); Std 7 effectively closed unless bloodline-extinction or deed-claim`; Hafenmark `Std 2→3 … +1 Ob … (Heljason can mitigate if Disposition ≥ +2); Std 4+ … caste-neutral`; Varfell `Std 3+ preferential if aligned with Maret Uln`; Church `Deacon requires … personal sponsorship; Canon+ structurally difficult …; Temperance branch caste-neutral; Inquisitor and Templar … closed`; Löwenritter `Std 3 … Ehrenwall historically withholds`; Riskbreakers `favored`; Guilds `Free Master's Examination … +1 Ob …; Guild Master typically closed …; Comptroller effectively closed`; Niflhel favored; Warden `Northern Gated … Southern Favored`; RM `Northern Ideologically suspect … Southern favored` — "effectively closed", "typically closed", "structurally difficult" are not predicates (**H-37**). Initiation Ob: `Crown Ob 2 / Southern Ob 3; Hafenmark 2 / 3; Varfell 2 / 2 (possibly 1); Church 2 / 3; Löwenritter 2 / 2; Guilds 2 / 3`. Inner-circle default Dispositions for a Southern PC (`§3.5`): `Almud −1; Voss −1; Reichard −1; Thale 0; Linder −2; Kreutz −1; Baralta 0; Almstedt 0; Feldhaus −1; Heljason 0; Geirson 0; Vaynard 0; Maret Uln +1; Hann 0; Torben (Sr Jarl West) 0; Ehrenwall (Skald) 0; Himlensendt −1; Jarnstal −1; Olafsson −2; Tormann −1; Klapp 0; Edeyja +1`; `Central Einhir start all values +1 from the Southern column; Northern Einhir … +2`. Caste-transgressive mechanisms (`§3.6.1`): `Witness Spirit Ob increase | … raises to Ob 2 …; Conviction Shake duration | … extends to 2 seasons; Doubt Marker self-application | … PC's next winning exchange has its margin reduced by 2 …`; Reformation arc (`§3.6.2`): `3+ caste-transgressive engagements within … 4 consecutive seasons → … Spirit pool, TN 7, Ob 3. Success: … consolidates … Renown +1 in aligned factions; −1 in caste-of-origin; Partial: … shifts back …; Standing … converts to half value; Conviction Scar permanently logged; Failure: … Piety Track resets to neutral (5/5/5). All faction Standing in non-caste-aligned factions drops 1 rank …; … NPC-state-equivalent for 2 seasons` ("Piety Track … (5/5/5)" is not the territory PT — **GP-11.07**). Viability Matrix `4-faction × 3-caste × specialty-branch grid … Favored (★★★), Standard (★★), Gated (★), or Closed (✕)` referenced to `throughline_resolutions_v1 §1.3` (absent — **GP-11.08**); advisory text names Niflhel as an open covert path (struck).

**F-11.09 Character creation** (`player_agency › §7.1`): `1. Write 3 Convictions 2. Choose caste background 3. Review Viability Matrix 4. Choose faction alignment (or independent) 5. … specialty branch … selected at Standing 3 …; at character creation the player may only indicate intent for GM use 6. Derived: Starting Standing = 0 (Petitioner) …; independent characters have no Standing. 7. Display Initiation Duty preview`.

**F-11.10 Generational transition & lineage.** `generational_transition_v30 › Trigger`: `PC death OR Portrait Retirement (≥2 Convictions resolved, player chooses to retire). Conviction Legacy fires (T8)`. PRESERVE: `Evidence Tracks; All faction stats …; All derived values …; All clocks (MS, CI, IP, PI, Strain, VTM, Altonian diplomacy, Coup Counter); All settlement states …; All NPC states (Disposition toward OTHER NPCs, Scars, Beliefs, arc position); Territory control, Accord, PT …; Church infrastructure (4 axes …); RM Presence markers; Card hands`. TRANSFORM: `One Conviction: Legacy Conviction …; Resources: floor(predecessor's Resources / 2) + new character's starting Resources`. RESET: `Disposition toward PC: all NPCs reset to faction-default; Standing: reset to 0 …; Coherence: reset to 10; Thread Sensitivity: per … lifepath origin; Certainty: per … origin; Wounds: 0; Stamina: full; Momentum: 0; Combat Reputation: 0; Exposure: 0 in all territories; Skills: per new character lifepath; Attributes: per …`. BREAK: `All Knots: rupture … (Knotted NPCs receive Knot rupture strain …); Companion relationships: departure scene … Companion returns to NPC pool`. TRANSFER: `Active Obligations: transfer to new character's faction …; Renown: reset to 0, but predecessor's Renown ≥ 7 grants +1 starting Renown`. **CT-50**: `Renown inherited: floor(predecessor ÷ 2)` (`player_agency › §10`); `inheriting Renown ÷ 2 (round down)` (`settlement_layer › §7.2`). **CT-51**: worked example `Resources 6` exceeds cap 5. Legacy Conviction transformations (`§10`): unfulfilled `→ "I will complete what [predecessor] began" or "I will understand why [predecessor] failed"`; fulfilled `→ "… protect …" or "… surpass …"`; relationship `→ "… honor …" (alive) or "… carry [predecessor]'s grief" (dead)`; `Dispositions: predecessor's allies (+3) start at +1; enemies (−2) start at −1; others at 0. Knotted NPCs: Knot does not transfer but +1D on first Connect … Settlement governance transfers to designated protégé automatically` (vs RESET "all NPCs reset to faction-default" — **CT-78**). Lineage Acts (`§11`; `campaign_architecture › §7.4`): `Mentorship | Skills at 60% …, Founded Organization membership, 1 Close Knot (Disposition −2 …) | mentee … Disposition ≥ +3; Succession | Social standing, titles, faction affiliation, estates. Settlement governance transfers | declared heir; Thread Legacy | WR at half (rounded down), Thread knowledge, Knot Legacy … | Embed knowledge into a Knot. Requires WR ≥ 2 … discoverable … (Evidence Track threshold 3 …)`; `No Lineage Act: … no mechanical inheritance`. Protégé (`settlement_layer › §7.2`): `named NPC companion or officer with Disposition +4 and Standing 4+ … new character — starting at Standing 0 but inheriting Renown ÷ 2`. Portrait Retirement: `After ≥ 2 of 3 starting Convictions resolved (Fulfilled/Failed/Transformed), the option "Conclude this story" appears at any season transition … Portrait Sequence: Opening Line, Conviction Chapter (3 arcs), World Footprint (counterfactual impact), Relationship Map (Knots), Final State, The World They Leave`; `Draft Portrait: Available from the main menu at any time`. Hybrid PC death (`strategic_layer › §9.9`): `faction loses 1 Stability, 1 Mandate at next Accounting; Player takes over the highest-loyalty NPC officer …; No new Player Characters introduced mid-season`.

**F-11.11 Governance role** (SET-06): governor eligibility by Standing; free governance action; [PROPOSAL] AP economy; Directive; Ledger. **F-11.12 Torben Readiness** (SET-09). **F-11.13 Referenced-only player systems**: Legitimacy Token (POW-03), Availability State, Ceremonial Scene category, Patronage vs Knot, Evidence Track, Depth Axis, Exposure, Knots, Companions, Certainty, Coherence, TS, Momentum, Stunts — all consumed here but specified elsewhere (**GP-11.09**).

**Gaps.** GP-11.01 Duty thresholds. GP-11.02 mandatory-list vs ordering-list mismatch. GP-11.03 target size within band. GP-11.04 appeal contest type. GP-11.05 social contest procedure. GP-11.06 stipend units. GP-11.07 "Piety Track (5/5/5)". GP-11.08 Viability Matrix. GP-11.09 personal-scale primitives absent. GP-11.10 "Recognized Deed" endorsement predicates (Church/Parliament/commander "publicly endorses"). GP-11.11 "Legitimizing Authority token".

### SET-11 Pipelines

**PL-11.01 Character creation.** Entry: new character. Steps: 1–7 of F-11.09 (Convictions → caste → Viability Matrix (absent) → faction/independent → branch intent → Standing 0 → Initiation Duty preview); starting Resources by origin; caste Disposition floors applied. Terminal: Petitioner at S1.

**PL-11.02 Season personal loop.** Entry: Phase 1a. Steps: Duty assigned (Standing ≥ 1; else Initiation) → Slate generated (PL-11.03) → scene actions spent (each scene 1–3 mechanical interactions; some 2 actions) → unpursued consequences (`§4.5`) → Accounting: Standing update (Duty result), Conviction review/revision, Renown cap, Resources income. Terminal: next season.

**PL-11.03 Scene Slate generation.** Entry: Personal Phase start. Steps: Step 1 mandatory (conditions) → Step 2 crisis → Step 2b thread-state (max 1) → Step 3 Duty (1–2) → Step 4 Conviction (≤ 3, validator) → Step 5 outreach/summons → Step 6 territorial (1–2) → Step 7 ambient (1) → tag (step, index) → pruning D-11.06 → if mandatory > budget: choose attended; rest Witness Mode. Terminal: presented Slate. Deterministic.

**PL-11.04 Duty lifecycle.** Entry: assigned. Steps: pursue via scenes → success/exceeding/failure/unpursued → D-11.04 at Accounting → floor 1. Terminal: Standing updated.

**PL-11.05 Conviction lifecycle.** Entry: written. Steps: pursued (Momentum +1 per genuine step) → NPC Resonant Style contest wins add strains → 3 strains: transform or abandon → resolution state (D-11.01) → new Conviction written (Transformed exempt from cap one season) → at retirement/death: one designated Legacy Conviction transforms. Terminal: resolved / legacy.

**PL-11.06 Rank advancement.** Entry: Petitioner (sponsorship / residence / presence / instruction per faction). Steps: Initiation Duty (Ob 2; Southern 3) → Std 1 → per-rank gates D-11.10 → Std 3 Formal Recognition Event (mandatory Zoom In; branch chosen; Banner/Land-Grant/council seat/Investiture) → Std 4 Public Deed → Std 5 Recognized Deed → Std 6 unanimous inner circle + Wing slot (pending / Prince-in-Waiting) → Std 7 succession-designation. Sub-office ladders in parallel (Löwenritter from Crown Martial Std 2; Riskbreaker from Löwenritter 3; Inquisitor/Templar from Church Canon 3; Guild via Hafenmark Commercial; Warden by TS). Terminal: Std 7 (or Leadership via PL-11.08). Reversible: demotion PL-11.07.

**PL-11.07 Demotion / dismissal / appeal.** Entry: a Demotion Trigger. Steps: magnitude D-11.11 → direct drop (no intermediate ranks) → mentor void, Hall/Livery resync within 1 season → appeal within 2 seasons (Std ≥ 1 only): success reverses; failure permanent for the campaign year → Dismissal (−1): Dishonored flag; re-entry only via a different branch. Terminal: new Standing.

**PL-11.08 Leadership acquisition / challenge.** Entry: leader vacancy or player call. Steps: vacancy: Std 7 → offered; Std 5–6 → SUC contest (absent); < 5 → NPC rules. Challenge: Std 4+ → social contest vs leader, council adjudicator → success: leader (DA authority, card hand, Duty system replaced by advisor AI) / failure: Std 2, Disposition −4. Terminal: leadership status.

**PL-11.09 Portrait Retirement.** Entry: D-11.18 at a season transition. Steps: "Conclude this story" → Portrait Sequence (six parts) → Lineage Act + Legacy Conviction → new character (PL-11.10). Terminal: retired PC becomes NPC.

**PL-11.10 Generational transition.** Entry: PC death or retirement. Steps: apply PRESERVE / TRANSFORM / RESET / BREAK / TRANSFER partition; Knot rupture strain on NPCs; companion departure scenes; Obligations transfer to faction; Lineage Act inheritance (Skills 60% / titles & governance / WR half + Knot Legacy); Legacy Conviction; Dispositions per `§10` (CT-78 vs RESET); Renown per CT-50; Resources per D-11.19. Terminal: successor at Standing 0.

**PL-11.11 Caste Reformation arc.** Entry: 3+ transgressive engagements in 4 seasons. Steps: mandatory Priority 0 scene → Spirit TN 7 Ob 3 → S/P/F (D-11.17). Terminal: consolidated / reverted / crisis (2 seasons NPC-state).

**PL-11.12 Hafenmark-to-Crown Recognition Ceremony.** Entry: Hafenmark victory in Crown contest with player at Hafenmark Std 7. Steps: presentation at Valorsplatz → provisional Crown Std 5 → IC majority evaluation → D-11.21. Terminal: rank consolidated or reverted.

**PL-11.13 Hybrid PC death succession.** Entry: PC dies. Steps: faction Stability −1, Mandate −1 next Accounting → player takes highest-loyalty NPC officer, else new PC (GM approval, H-06) at next season boundary. Terminal: new PC.

### SET-11 Throughlines applied
TL-12 — H-25, H-26, H-27 (videogame variant given), H-28, H-36, H-37. TL-14 — "Standing" (PC) vs faction Standing tokens (CT-57); "Piety Track" as contest type (GP-11.04/11.07). TL-15 — Renown and Shadow Renown never decay; Dishonored persists; branch switch costs demotion to 3. TL-07 — Renown +2/season; Shadow +2/season. TL-11 — Slate anchoring to settlements asserted; travel cost per province. TL-03 — no conflict.

## SET-12 — Conflict Ignition & Campaign Arc

### SET-12 Flattened statement

**F-12.01 Diagnosis** [ANALYSIS, not rule]: `every canonical mechanic is a pressure accumulator, not a trigger … The rational move for all four factions is to sit, build, and wait` (`early_game_ignition › §1`). Superseded conclusion: `The early-game ignition problem was never a missing system. … the starting settlement map already contains five governance disputes. The game starts on fire.` (`conflict_architecture › Core Insight`).

**F-12.02 Ignition by settlement friction.** Five friction points (SET-06 F-06.10) each `fires a fragmentation check every Accounting from S1`; T1 check `Influence 5 vs Ob 3`; on failure `the Cathedral district moves toward formal Church governance` — "moves toward" has no state (**GP-12.01**; under `fractional › §2.6` Failure is Secession-by-choice). `No new system is required`.

**F-12.03 Tensions Deck — CT-22.** (a) `6 cards, draw 1 at game start. Each card is a fuse (condition established S0, event fires S8+)` (`conflict_architecture › Tensions Deck (Rescoped)`): `1 Royal Crisis | T1 Crown-Church friction + succession | One Royal family member assassinated (sub-roll for target); 2 Feldmark Famine | T5 | Prosperity collapse in Crown's food supply → economic crisis; 3 Cardinal Independence | T1 + T9 | Rogue Cardinal unilaterally appoints bishop-governor in Crown settlement; 4 Guild Fracture | T8 | S017 Guild schism → Market Quarter becomes contested; 5 Einhir Incident | T4 + T13 | Public Einhir cultural confrontation forces all factions to declare position; 6 Ministry Crisis | T14 + Crown governance | Ministry bureaucracy collapses → Crown governance vacuum → Church fills`; `Every card creates a visible fuse from S1 … The player can investigate and attempt intervention during S1–S7. The event fires if not averted.`; `Remaining 5 cards written and integrated during smoke testing. Low priority` — cards 2–6 have one-line effects only (**GP-12.02**); averting procedure absent (**H-29**). (b) `phases › Game Setup`: `Draw 1 card from 6`. (c) `early_game › §4.1`: `8 Tension Cards, of which 2 are drawn`: `T1 Disputed Succession | Torben Loyalty starts at 3 (not 7). Lenneth publicly declares sympathy for Einhir restoration. … Coup Counter +1 at S4; T2 Feldmark Famine | T5 Feldmark Prosperity drops to 2 (from 5). … +1D on all Senator actions for 4 seasons [food-surplus faction]; T3 Heresy at Himmelenger | Olafsson … Heresy Investigation in T9 … Church must Uphold (AER −1) or Appease (Mandate −1 …). Varfell … Tribune action unlocked toward Church territory from S1; T4 Border Incident at Ehrenfeld | … Both Varfell and Crown receive Casus Belli … If neither acts … within 3 seasons, Coup Counter +1 … AND Varfell Accord −1 in T4; T5 Guild Schism | … Guilds Stability −2. If no faction intervenes within 4 seasons, S017 becomes Uncontrolled; T6 Altonian Ultimatum | … Torben must be sent … by Season 8, or IP +10 …; comply (Torben Loyalty drops to 1 …); T7 Einhir Cultural Ban | Varfell Accord in T4 and T13 … −1. RM Popular Will +1 … every season … RM PW +1 …; Lifting … CI +1; T8 Löwenritter Exercises | … Mandate check (Ob 1) at S2 … If Crown fails: Coup Counter +1 … If no faction fails: … Coup Counter +0.5`. (d) `early_game` header: `Tensions Deck rescoped to 6 external bilateral cards, draw 2`. Three deck sizes/draws; "RM Popular Will (PW)" track undefined (**GP-12.03**); "Coup Counter +0.5" fractional on an integer counter.

**F-12.04 Royal assassination fuse.** `One assassination event per campaign, firing at S8+ minimum. Target determined at game start (Tensions Deck draw or randomized). The assassination succeeds when it fires — no attempt/failure variance. … S0: the player receives signals … S1–S7: escalation visible. Player can investigate (costs card slots). If the player identifies and stops the plot: assassination averted … If the player doesn't intervene: the event fires at S8+ (exact season randomized within S8–S12 window).` Branches (`conflict_architecture › Royal Assassination as Fuse`): `Lenneth dies → Almud revenge arc. … Crown governance suffers … Investigation arc opens … Crown's Einhir policy hardens. RM PW advances. Southern Accord erodes.`; `Torben dies → Elske retrieval. … Retrieval requires military deployment to T4 (Varfell territory) … direct provocation against Varfell AND an Altonian diplomatic crisis (IP spike).`; `Almud dies → Lenneth takes the throne. … Crown becomes an ally of Varfell and RM … heresy target for Church. Löwenritter must decide …`. All three branches are prose with no numeric effects (**GP-12.04**); target sub-roll distribution absent (**GP-12.05**). `phases` setup step 3 names targets `Lenneth/Torben/Almud`. Priority 0 scene when `faction leader is assassinated` (SET-11).

**F-12.05 Superseded provocation mechanics** (`early_game › §4.2–4.3`): Niflhel Priority 2b `If no inter-faction battle has occurred for 3 consecutive seasons AND no Tension Card is active: … Fabricated Intelligence … Dossier … one attribute value is exaggerated by +2 or −2 … sells it to the faction most likely to act against the target … discovered by … intelligence action`; Löwenritter Priority 3b `If no battle … adjacent to T14 for 4 consecutive seasons AND Coup Counter < 3: … deploy to one random territory adjacent to T14 (T1, T4, T5, or T9) … Crown-held: authorize (no cost) or recall (Coup Counter +0.5); faction-held: deploy defensive forces: Strain +1; no response: Löwenritter remains for 2 seasons`; milestones `S4: Torben's Name Day …; S8: Harvest Accounting …; S12: Winter Council … unresolved Casus Belli must be adjudicated or escalate` (Priority C). All `[SUPERSEDED]`; the successor keeps only "intelligence brokers … can fabricate intel" without the ±2 rule (GP-06.12).

**F-12.06 Thread revelation curve** (`campaign_architecture › Part 4`): `At game start (MS ~72), threadwork is folklore.` Visibility: `100–80 | Nothing. Folklore. | None; 79–60 | Subtle anomalies near Southernmost … | Mild unease …; 59–40 | Observable. Gaps as physical distortions. Disappearances. | Fear. Church scrambles. Crown must act. Varfell gains value. RM denial harder; 39–20 | Peninsula-wide … Threadcut beings encountered by ordinary people | Crisis of understanding …; 19–1 | Undeniable … | Existential reorientation`. Triggers: `renowned character performs threadwork publicly; Threadcut being encountered in populated settlement; Edeyja's Mending produces visible results; mass battle involving Thread operations; RM discovers governance node IS a Threadweaving site. Each trigger fires a Revelation Event scene — mandatory Scene Slate entry`. "Political Impact" column is prose (**GP-12.06**); "renowned" unquantified.

**F-12.07 Named Character Event Cards** (SET-10 F-10.06) and `Klapp Awakening` worked scenario (`strategic_layer › Scenario A`: `Klapp Active State: +1D all Investigate, Heresy Investigations cost 0 Wealth`; `Trajectory Choice` A/B/C with CI ±1, Discovery Event `Church: Mandate 5 vs Ob 2`, Prosecute/Protect, Klapp's Awakening Milestone `Ritual research at Warden Cooperation 2 instead of 3`). Accounting step 8 `draw one Event Card per threshold crossed` (deck absent, GP-02.10).

**F-12.08 Phased Altonian invasion and repulsion** — SET-03 F-03.04. Almaic Kyriakos envoy `IP 50` (`worldbuilding › §3.6`). Vanguard (SET-03).

**F-12.09 RM and Warden arcs** — SET-09 F-09.08/09.09; `campaign_architecture › §2.3` crisis paths gated on `When Thread phenomena become publicly visible (see Part 4)`.

**F-12.10 Worked scenarios** (`strategic_layer › Part Six`): Scenario A (Klapp), B (Patience Protocol — `[HISTORICAL EXAMPLE — PP-664]`), C (IP crisis with AER — obsolete); Part Five cascade tests. Non-normative; retained numbers: Hafenmark Wealth 5 Trade at `Ob 3` under IP disruption; `Institutional Pressure −5 (military rebuff)` on Vanguard defeat (a rule stated only here — **GP-12.07**); `Löwenritter March T5→T4: Military 6 vs Ob 2` (March has "No roll" per `core` — CT-72 extension).

**F-12.11 Pace analysis** (`strategic_layer › Part Eight`): `Starting Church Influence 22. Target Church Influence 70. Gap: 48 points … Estimated victory: Season 35–45` — stale values (CI start 28; no CI victory) [reference only]. `Restoration Victory … Mending Stability ≥ 50 must be met at the moment of victory declaration` (`P-17`) — CT-28.

**F-12.12 Hollow Victory** — `[DISSOLVED]` (`phases` step 11) vs `Hollow Victory applies only to Deed-counting factions (Hafenmark, Guilds …)` (`strategic_layer › PP-035`), Year-End step 6, `Hafenmark Appease count … Effective Deeds` (`strategic_layer › Cascade Test 2`), `Hafenmark Deed 3 (Parliamentary Consolidation)` (`ministry.md`) — **CT-71** (dissolved system still referenced by four live rules).

**F-12.13 Long campaign.** `Age track (optional Long Campaign only)` (`phases › Year-End` 8); `10–30 Year Games` recalibration (`settlement_layer › §7.1`); `typical campaign is ~S14–S20` (`faction_politics › A-10`); `20-season game` (`strategic_layer › Here I Stand`). Campaign length is never fixed (**GP-12.08**).

**Gaps.** GP-12.01 "moves toward". GP-12.02 cards 2–6 mechanics. GP-12.03 RM Popular Will. GP-12.04 assassination branch effects. GP-12.05 target distribution. GP-12.06 revelation political impact. GP-12.07 IP −5 rebuff. GP-12.08 campaign length. GP-12.09 "investigate (costs card slots)" — which card/action investigates a plot. GP-12.10 Tensions "resolution condition" per card (early_game §6) not written for the 6-card deck.

Terminal: event resolved; consequences persist. One per campaign.

**PL-12.03 Tension card lifecycle.** Entry: drawn. Steps: amplify friction point from S1 → S8+ event (D-12.02) → resolution condition (absent for the 6-card deck) → ongoing effects end; territorial consequences persist. Terminal: resolved.

**PL-12.05 Klapp Awakening.** Entry: D-12.06 predicate. Steps: card drawn → Klapp Active → next season Trajectory Choice (A Suppress: CI +1, removed; B Investigate: CI −1, Discovery roll `Church Mandate vs Ob 2` → Prosecute (Stability −1, CI −2, removed) / Protect (Renown −1, one more season); C: WC +1, CI −2, Stability −1, Church gains one Senate Market Pontifex/season; milestone WC 2 for Ritual research). Terminal: card resolved.

**PL-12.06 Event card firing** — SET-10 PL-10.13.

**PL-12.07 Superseded provocation** — D-12.07/D-12.08 (recorded, not live).

## SET-13 — Southernmost, Thread & Warden Interface

### SET-13 Flattened statement

**F-13.01 Askeheim access (board form).** `T6 Stillhelm = normal territory. … T15 Askeheim = the Southernmost proper. Not a normal territory. Cannot be controlled by any faction through standard mechanics. … any military force that enters encounters Thread operations at TS 75–80 scale.` Expedition (`geography › Expedition Procedure (T6 → T15)`): `1. Stage in T6: Faction must control T6 AND have a Champion with TS ≥ 30 present there for at least 1 full season. Champion must not have been used for military orders that season. 2. Declare Expedition: Phase 4, Praetor or Tribune Inward in T6. Roll: Champion pool (TS ÷ 10, rounded down, min 1D, max 3D) vs Ob 3. Modifiers: Restoration Weaver with Presence in T6: −1 Ob. VTM ≥ 2: −1 Ob. Failure: … Try again next season. 3. Forgetting Check (on entry into T15): Same pool vs Ob 2 … Failure | … retains nothing. No Warden contact. No Cooperation advance. … returns to T6 next season; Partial | Emotional impressions retained. Warden Emergence fires (if not active). Cooperation +0; Success | Facts retained. Warden Emergence fires. Cooperation +1. VTM +1 (Varfell only, if VTM ≥ 2); Overwhelming | Full retention. … Cooperation +1. VTM +1 (Varfell). Edeyja makes contact. 4. Meeting Edeyja: Requires Overwhelming on Forgetting Check, OR Success + 1 prior successful season in T15. … TS < 30: she will not engage. TS 30–39: brief, wary contact. TS 40+: substantive contact. She never leaves T15.` Champions (`geography › Champion TS Values (BG)`): `Vaynard (VTM 3+) 30; (VTM 4+) 40; Almud Almqvist 0 — Discovery Event … Spirit Ob 1 … On success: TS gains initial awareness (GM sets value, likely 10-15 …), Certainty −1 (permanent). Politically costly: CI +1; Church learning triggers CI +2 + Heresy Investigation; Lenneth Unknown (ED-119); Restoration Weaver (TS 18) … support only (Ob −1); Any other < 30` (**H-30** GM sets TS; VTM struck — GP-03.09). BG Forgetting pool (`strategic_layer › G-06 / P-26`): `Restoration: Influence + 1D per Presence marker in T13; Varfell: Intelligence stat; other factions: Wealth ÷ 2 rounded up …; Ob 1 (floor applies). Thread-qualified presence (Restoration Weaver this season) grants −1 Ob, already at floor. A practitioner Player Character present reduces the applicable Ob by 1 in hybrid mode` — a third Forgetting pool alongside geography's Champion pool and southernmost's `Cognition + Recall. TN 8` (**CT-48**).

**F-13.02 Southernmost mechanics (personal form)** (`southernmost.md`). Zones: `Snapped | Maximum tension | Objects crumble; configurations disrupt; Locked | Frozen at catastrophe moment | Configuration instability; Certainty drain; Oscillating | Continuous cycling | Gap formation; monstrous entities`. Forgetting Check: `Pool: Cognition + Recall. TN 8. TS bonus: TS ÷ 20 (round down) additional dice.` Ob by exposure: `Boundary zone < 1 hour 1; Interior 1–4 hours 2; Deep interior 4+ hours 3; Einhir core sites 4`. Retention: `Overwhelming | Full facts + TS 40+ retains ontological understanding; Success | Facts retained; threat context partially dissolved; Partial | Emotional impressions only; Failure | Nothing operational`. Testimony penalty: `Boundary +1; Interior +2; Deep interior / Core +3`. Probability (7D, TN8): `Ob 1: 76% / 0% / 24%; 2: 60/15/24; 3: 43/33/24; 4: 27/49/24`. Awareness: `Scale: 0–7 (or 0–10 for research-intensive factions). Starting values: see params_factions_ttrpg.md` (absent — **GP-13.01**); `Ritual without Awareness 5+ → +2 Ob`. Expedition prerequisites: `Thread Tension ≥ 40; Practitioner officer TS 30+; Planning roll: relevant History, Ob 3; Resources: Ob 3; Military escort: recommended (1+ unit)`. Procedure: `1 | Approach | Escort Discipline Ob 1; practitioner Discovery Event; 2 | Exploration | Three zones; one encounter per zone; 3 | Discovery | Diagnosis: TS 50+ auto; below TS 50: Ob 3; 4+ | Repair (optional) | Extraordinary Repair Weaving Ob 3`. Hazards: `Border (Snapped) | Shifting Objects | Weave or Agi | 2 | 1 Wound/character; Inner (Oscillating) | Monstrous entity | Combat | varies | Gap open, TT +2; Core (Locked) | Config instability | Spirit (non-practitioners) | 2/round | Certainty −1`; `Core zone: Contact duration halved for practitioners.` These prerequisites (`TT ≥ 40`, planning/Resources rolls) and the 4-season structure differ from geography's 4-step procedure (CT-48 extension; no bridge between "Expedition" as a Domain-Action declaration and as a multi-season personal operation — **GP-13.02**).

**F-13.03 Ritual and Repair.** `[NAME-PENDING: ED-048] Ritual: Requirements: ED-048 Text in possession; Awareness 5+ (else +2 Ob); lead TS 60+; 2 participants TS 20+; 1 season prep. Roll: Weaving pool, Ob 5. Each participant TS 20+: +1D (max +4D bonus). Overwhelming | Permanent stabilisation. TT −10. Southernmost settleable; Success | Temporary. TT −6. Accessible 5 seasons. Re-attemptable; Partial | TT −3. Forgetting Ob −1 permanent. Lead cannot retry; new lead required; Failure | TT +8. Mode 3 entity at primary site. Lead Incapacitated.` Probabilities `18D 80%/42%; 20D 87%/53%; 22D 92%/63%`. Extraordinary Repair: `Requirements: Awareness 8+ (impossible on a 0–7 scale — **GP-13.03**); expedition reaching primary site complete; practitioner TS 50+ assigned to Research. Research roll: Recall + Einhir Scholar History, Ob 4. Repair roll (each season): Weaving pool, Ob 3. −1D Core zone contact penalty. Success/Overwhelming | TT −2 permanent | Awareness +1; Partial | TT −1 | Practitioner CD +1; Failure | TT +1 | CD +2. After 4-5 successful seasons: Southernmost fully stabilised.` Cap: `Combined TT reduction from Southernmost operations (Ritual + Extraordinary Repair): max −5 per season.` Crisis timeline: `TT 50 | Outer winding strain — visible faction event fires; player warning; TT 50 sustained 3 seasons without stabilising Weave | Cracking begins — TT +1/season from Southernmost; Cracking 3+ seasons without Ritual attempt | Outer winding fails — TT +2/season, permanent until Ritual succeeds`; `Stabilising Weave (pause cracking, not reverse): Ob 3, TS 40+, practitioner.` "Mode 3 entity", "CD", "Southernmost settleable" are undefined here (**GP-13.04**); "4-5 successful seasons" is a range not a threshold (**GP-13.05**).

**F-13.04 Coherence, fatigue, Menders** (`campaign_architecture › Part 3`). Coherence cost: `Mending (any degree) 0; Sight (passive) 0; Sight (active/forced) 1; Lock 1 per test; Dissolution 2 per test; Extraction 1–2 (by scale); Binding 2 per test`. Fatigue: `In-scene (pool depletion): … Op 1 = full pool, op 2 = −1D, op 3 = −2D, op 4 = −4D, op 5 = −7D, op 6 = −12D. Pool restores fully at the start of each new scene.`; `Cross-scene (Ob fatigue): Each scene in a season in which threadwork is used adds +2 Ob to all threadwork in subsequent scenes that season. Resets each season.` Menders: `Spirit 1–2 | Journeyman | +1 | +2; 3–4 | Skilled | +1 | +3; 5 | Expert | +2 | +4; 6 (Edeyja) | Master | +2 | +5. May Mend in destabilized zones (MS ≤ 5) without Stability Check.`; `MS recovery is cumulative across all active Menders per season.` `Edeyja is the best Mender (Spirit 6) but NOT the only one` — the Mender roster (`Warden community, some RM-adjacent practitioners (post-revelation), some Varfell-aligned threadworkers, … pre-schism Church mystics`) has no counts or activation rule (**GP-13.06**). Coherence reset 10 on generation (`generational_transition`).

**F-13.05 Thread operations at strategic scale.** Phase 4 tier 5 (`phases`); `Thread Operation (Pontifex/Weaver) | Ob 2 base | See PP-182 co-movement protocol` (`core`; PP-182 absent — **GP-13.07**). Procedure fragments (`strategic_layer › Cascade Test 3`): `1. Declare … 2. Against temporal flow check: MS below 30? … Prior Thread Debt in territory? → Thread Debt incurred. +1 Ob to NEXT operation here … 3. ### SET-13 Pipelines

**PL-13.01 Expedition (board form).** Entry: faction controls T6 with Champion TS ≥ 30 present one full season, not used for military. Steps: Declare (Praetor/Tribune Inward in T6; D-13.01 vs Ob 3; −1 Ob RM Weaver; (−1 Ob VTM ≥ 2, struck)) → Failure: retry next season / Success: depart → Forgetting Check (same pool vs Ob 2) → D-13.07 (WC +1 on S/OW; Warden Emergence fires on P/S/OW; Edeyja contact on OW) → Meeting Edeyja (D-13.24) → subsequent seasons in T15 accrue WR. Terminal: contact/WR progress; Failure returns Champion to T6.

**PL-13.02 Expedition (personal form).** Entry: prerequisites (`TT ≥ 40`, TS 30+, planning Ob 3, Resources Ob 3, escort). Steps: Season 1 Approach (Escort Discipline Ob 1; Discovery Event) → Season 2 Exploration (three zones; one encounter each per hazard table; Forgetting Checks by exposure) → Season 3 Discovery (Diagnosis TS 50+ auto / Ob 3) → Season 4+ Repair (PL-13.04). Terminal: primary site reached. (GP-13.02: relation to PL-13.01 unstated.)

**PL-13.03 Ritual.** Entry: requirements (Text; Awareness ≥ 5 or +2 Ob; lead TS ≥ 60; 2 participants TS ≥ 20; 1 season prep). Steps: Weaving pool + D-13.08 vs Ob 5 → D-13.09 → TT change (capped with Repair at −5/season). Branches: OW permanent (zones stabilised, settleable); S temporary 5 seasons, re-attemptable; P lead barred; F lead Incapacitated, Mode 3 entity. Terminal: stabilisation or lockout.

**PL-13.04 Extraordinary Repair.** Entry: Awareness 8+ (GP-13.03); expedition complete; practitioner TS ≥ 50 on Research. Steps: research roll `Recall + Einhir Scholar History, Ob 4` → each season repair roll `Weaving, Ob 3, −1D Core` → D-13.10 → after `4-5 successful seasons` fully stabilised. Terminal: stabilised.

**PL-13.05 TT crisis timeline.** Entry: TT reaches 50. Steps: warning event → 3 seasons without Stabilising Weave → cracking +1/season → 3+ cracking seasons without Ritual attempt → outer winding fails +2/season permanent until Ritual succeeds; Stabilising Weave (Ob 3, TS 40+) pauses. Terminal: Ritual success (reverses per D-13.09).

**PL-13.06 Thread operation (strategic, Pontifex tier 5).** Entry: Pontifex card declared with operation & territory. Steps: temporal-flow check (MS < 30 / prior Debt) → Debt token (+1 Ob next op) → Ob assembly D-13.17 → roll (pool per PP-616 or Influence — CT-25) → result (MS/PT/settlement effects) → Co-Movement draw (2 with Witness Node) → Attention Pool +2 on type reveal → Resonance marker. Terminal: effects applied; Debt serviced or drains at Accounting.

**PL-13.07 Seasonal MS recovery by Menders.** Entry: Accounting. Steps: for each active Mender: degree → D-13.16 contribution (Edeyja exempt from Stability Check at MS ≤ 5) → sum → MS += total; WC 3 adds +2; Restoration Weave +1 (max +2). Terminal: MS updated.

**PL-13.08 Threadwork fatigue.** Entry: a scene with a threadwork op. Steps: per op in scene apply D-13.14 pool depletion; per new scene in season apply D-13.15 +2 Ob; Coherence −cost per D-13.13 (Mending 0). Reset: pool per scene; Ob per season. Terminal: none.

**PL-13.09 Thread revelation** — SET-12 PL-12.04.

**PL-13.10 Discovery Event.** Entry: Thread-significant event with a TS-0 character present (Almud) or Private Collection use at hidden TS ≥ 14. Steps: `Spirit Ob 1` (TN 7) → Success: TS assigned (GM, H-30) / `TK +1`; `Certainty −1 (permanent)`; political costs (Almud: `CI +1`; if Church learns `CI +2 + Heresy Investigation`). Terminal: awareness state.

**PL-13.11 Warden faction activation** — SET-09 PL-09.13.

### SET-13 Throughlines applied
TL-09 — T15 uncontrollable asserted; Expedition/WR only. TL-02 — southernmost `TN 8` breaks the TN-7 convention (CT-48) though Ob floor is respected. TL-07 — settlement Thread effects `±1 per settlement stat per season`; TT cap −5; Restoration MS +2 cap. TL-12 — H-05, H-30. TL-14 — RS ≡ MS alias documented (`worldbuilding` ED-741); "Community Organizing" vs "Weaving" (CT-25). TL-08 — Private Collection migrated to resolver; Ritual/Repair/Forgetting remain dice (personal scale — in scope).

## SET-14 — Diplomacy, Treaties, Pledges & Casus Belli

### SET-14 Flattened statement

**F-14.01 Formal Crown Treaty.** Availability `Crown only` (`core › Standard Action Ob Reference`: `Formal Crown Treaty (Senator Outward) | floor(target L / 2) + 1 | Crown only. PP-512/513/514/523. See victory_v30.md §3.1.`); `Multiple Crown Treaties may exist simultaneously (one per non-Crown faction)` (`treaty_expiration › §1.3`); `Treaty requires parliamentary status` (`insurgency › §4.3`); Crown AI `Crown Treaty targets: PLAYABLE factions only, Mandate ≥ 2 … if Mandate ≥ 4 and PV ≥ 10` (`ci_political › §6.2`). Effects on binding: `Crown Treaty Success/OW | −1 [Mandate] to target; Crown +1 (OW)` (`ci_political › §4.1`); `Treaty-bound territories are protected from Crown intervention by other factions` (`treaty_expiration › §4`); protections cease at lapse. **CT-76** — three formation rules: (a) `floor(target L / 2) + 1` (`core`); (b) Senator Outward re-binding `Cost | Wealth −2; Pool | Influence + Standing modifier; Ob | Target faction Sta … Overwhelming | New Treaty bound … Target faction Standing +1; Success | New Treaty bound; Partial | Diplomatic overture acknowledged; CB-blocking effect for 1 arc. No Treaty; Failure | Standing −1 (snub)` (`treaty_expiration › §2`, `[PROVISIONAL: Senator Outward Ob = target faction Sta is part10 canon]`); (c) resolver `Treaty positioning & ratification … Positioning (contested): M = own Influence − target Influence (Success+ → initiator sets opening terms). Ratification: M = Mandate − 2 … Guarantor option → +1 M; Grand Debate Zoom-In degree → margin (Overwhelming +2 / Success +1 / Partial 0 / Failure −1) … CI institutional weight (Church) = +⌊CI/20⌋ to M` (`stats › Domain Action Resolution`). "Standing modifier" on a pool, "Guarantor", and "opening terms" are undefined (**GP-14.01**). `Diplomacy between players | Negotiated | Not a roll` (`core`).

**F-14.02 Treaty expiration** (`treaty_expiration › §1`): `At each arc boundary (4 seasons per arc), each active Crown Treaty independently rolls for lapse. … Lapse rate TREATY_LAPSE_RATE ∈ [0.90, 0.95] (canonical default: 0.90) … 1. At arc boundary, before any other arc-boundary processing, iterate active Crown Treaties. 2. For each Treaty: roll uniform [0, 1). 3. If roll < TREATY_LAPSE_RATE: Treaty lapses (clear from both factions' treaty registers). 4. Lapsed Treaty effects (territorial protections, stat modifiers, action eligibility gates) cease immediately`. Consequences: `no CB … No automatic Standing delta … Crown may re-bind … via Senator Outward`. Invariants: `independent per Treaty … memoryless`. Balance: `Crown 24.7% / Church 28.6% / Hafenmark 24.2% / Varfell 22.5%` at N=1000; `TREATY_LAPSE_RATE +0.05 → Crown −1pp`. `Strain −1` when `Treaty formed` (`phases` 4d(ii)). `da.diplomatic_alliance (treaty signed) → +1` procedural Legitimacy (`faction_behavior › §3.5.1`). The `calendar deadline (treaty expiration) mechanic` was cut as `No canon basis for starting treaties` (`conflict_architecture › What's Cut`) — no starting treaties exist.

**F-14.03 Treaty violation vs Crown-break — CT-35.** (a) `treaty_expiration › §3`: violation when `attacks (Military Conquest, Mass Battle invasion) a territory of the other treaty party; supports an external CB against the other party …; initiates Parliamentary Transfer … against the other party's territory`; consequences `Treaty immediately voided; Treaty-violation CB generated (wronged party gains CB vs violator …); Violator Standing −2; Wronged party Standing +1` `[PROVISIONAL]`. (b) `parliament.md › Crown-Break Trigger Definition (PP-525)`: `Crown breaks a Formal Crown Treaty by: (a) playing a Legionary card targeting a territory held by the Treaty partner, or (b) explicit Phase 1 dissolution declaration. Intel actions, Domain Actions in non-partner territory, and Parliamentary actions are not Crown-breaks.` (c) `parliament.md › Treaty Betrayal Cascade Timing (PP-523)`: `1. Dissolution declared at Phase 1 … 2. Crown Stability −2, Crown L −1 applied at end of Phase 4. 3. Casus Belli granted to betrayed faction at Accounting Step 1. 4. CB usable from the following season only.` (a) makes Parliamentary Transfer a violation; (b) says Parliamentary actions are not breaks. (a) costs Standing; (c) costs Stability/L. `Violation does NOT consume the arc-boundary lapse roll`. `strategic_layer › I-03 — Casus Belli Expiration vs "Permanent Until Used"` is recorded as an unresolved inconsistency with no correction (**CT-59**).

**F-14.04 Casus Belli.** Sources (`parliamentary_transfer › §3`): `Crown constitutional restoration | Auto-justified when Crown territories < 6 (starting count). Crown faction only. Refreshes per arc if condition holds; Adjacent instability | At arc boundary: if a faction has a territory at Accord ≤ 1, adjacent faction holders gain CB against that faction. CB persists 1 arc; Einhir Revival Partial | Varfell gains CB … (einhir_revival_v30.md, pending); Parliamentary Transfer Partial | Proposer gains CB vs holder for retry next arc; Military Conquest action | Various existing military actions generate CB per mass_battle_v30 and faction_canon_v30 (absent); Treaty-violation CB | …; Excommunication-related CB | … for any Church-allied parliamentary faction; Conviction Scar accumulation | Holder reaches Conviction Scar count threshold (≥3 …) → all parliamentary factions gain Punishment-mode CB`. `CB tracking is faction-pair-scoped … Multiple CB sources can stack on the same pair; consumption decrements one source per Parliamentary Transfer attempt.` Other grants: `Casus Belli granted to controlling faction on every seizure attempt (PP-510)`; `Casus Belli (standard, 3 seasons)` on Pledge breach (`parliament`); `Casus Belli at settlement scope (… one Casus Belli per settlement transferred)` (`fractional › §5`); `Cathedral | Attacker takes Church Casus Belli` (`settlement_adjacency › §2.2`); `Casus Belli +1 to the territorial faction` on unauthorised crossing (`march_layer › §5.4`); Bishop Appointment `Casus Belli NOT generated`; Insurgencies `Generate CB against other factions`. Lifetimes — **CT-59**: `3 seasons` (Pledge), `1 arc` (Adjacent instability), `refreshes per arc` (constitutional), `permanent until used` vs expiration (I-03 unresolved), numeric `+1` (march). What a CB *permits* other than Parliamentary Transfer is unstated (`use the Casus Belli (attack T14 …)` in `early_game` is superseded) (**GP-14.02**). "Church-allied" and "Einhir Revival" undefined (**GP-14.03**). `march_layer › §5.4` also: `territorial faction may treat the army as hostile from next Manoeuvre tick (engagement-on-arrival forced if the trespasser does not retreat by next-season Accounting)`; `MARCH-04 Naval Casus Belli — … open`.

**F-14.05 Pledges** (`parliament.md › Open Pledge System (PP-515)`): `Any faction may declare an Open Pledge in Phase 1: a publicly stated commitment to a specific mechanical action or abstention for the current season. … Honour: +1 Standing. Breach: Stability −1 + Casus Belli (standard, 3 seasons) to every witnessing faction. Simultaneous resolution: A Pledge to abstain from a Military action is breached if the pledging faction played a Legionary card in Phase 1, regardless of resolution order. … Forced breach exemption: Pledge honoured if breach results directly from responding to another faction's military action in the pledged territory, provided the pledging faction's card was not yet played when the threat arose. GM adjudicates. Scope: One season only. … Limit: 1 active Pledge per faction at a time.` (**H-31**). `Closed Pledge (PP-515, PP-527): Private two-faction agreement written on a face-down card. If broken: injured party may reveal at any Accounting. Witnesses at revelation = all factions present at the Accounting where revelation occurs. Consequences: Stability −1 + Casus Belli (3 seasons) to all factions at revelation Accounting + breaching faction PI −1.` ("breaching faction PI" — PI is not a faction stat, **GP-14.04**; "witnessing faction" for an Open Pledge is undefined in a no-table engine — **GP-14.05**.) `Strain −1` when `Pledge honoured` (`phases` 4d(ii)). Pledge as intelligence trade (`strategic_layer › Scenario B`: `Open Pledge: share Restoration intelligence in exchange for Crown's Institutional Pressure management commitments … Varfell gains +1D to all Military actions in Crown-adjacent territories for 2 seasons`) — bespoke effects not derivable from any rule.

**F-14.06 Diplomacy actions.** `Diplomacy vs NPC (Senator Outward) | floor(NPC Stability / 2) + 1`; `Diplomatic success vs NPC | +1 [Mandate] (per sim convention)` (`ci_political › §4.1`). **CT-36** with Senator Outward `Ob = target faction Sta` (`treaty_expiration`) and resolver contested-stat difficulty. `Diplomatic Alignment (PP-526): Once per season during a Parliamentary Session, both Church and Hafenmark count as same-side voters regardless of their individually declared votes. Declared at the start of the Parliamentary Session. Public. Applies to one motion per season. Not a free card play; requires no action spend.` `Dynastic Proclamation (Hafenmark) | floor(target Stability / 2) + 1 | Diplomat card. Hafenmark M ≥ 4, M > target controller M. +1 Ob if PT ≤ 1. −1 Ob if Diplomatic Token on target. See peninsular_strain_v1.md §5.3` (effects absent — **GP-14.06**; "Diplomatic Token" undefined — **GP-14.07**). `Tributary +1/year (receiver)`; `Blockade active −2/season` (`ci_political › §4.1`; mechanics absent). `Non-Aggression Pact 20 seasons` (diplomatic repulsion). Re-merger of splinters `via treaty (… both at Mandate ≥ 3)`. Insurgency/extra-parliamentary: `informal treaties or alliances` allowed; `formal Crown Treaties` not. `Torben … contest via Senator Outward Diplomacy (Ob = current Torben Loyalty ÷ 2)`. `Elske contact, Diplomacy Ob 2 … Overwhelming Diplomacy: Elske Loyalty +1 … AND block one declared Löwenritter order next season` (`strategic_layer › Scenario C`, example). Altonian diplomacy hooks TBD (SET-10 GP-10.01). Excommunication penance requires `one action per season must be Senator Outward (Diplomacy) toward Church`. Cross-faction Wing cession as treaty concession (SET-06). Church Rebuttal (`Parliamentary Rebuttal — Overwhelming → +1 effect`). Standing tokens (GP-08.14).

**F-14.07 Coalition rules.** Suppression coalition (SET-01 F-01.13). `Missed Coalition Penalty — PP-404 … applies when both factions' relevant stat is at or above the coalition floor (not "above"). Fog-of-war exemption per PP-404 original text.` (`parliament › PP-522`; PP-404 absent — **GP-14.08**).

**Gaps.** GP-14.01 Standing modifier / Guarantor / terms. GP-14.02 what CB permits. GP-14.03 Church-allied, Einhir Revival. GP-14.04 "faction PI". GP-14.05 witnesses. GP-14.06 Dynastic Proclamation effects. GP-14.07 Diplomatic Token. GP-14.08 PP-404. GP-14.09 treaty "protections … action eligibility gates" list. ### SET-14 Pipelines

**PL-14.01 Treaty lifecycle.** Entry: Crown Senator Outward (Wealth −2) vs partner. Steps: roll per D-14.02/D-14.03 (or positioning → ratification per D-14.05/06 — CT-76) → bound (target Mandate −1; Crown +1 on OW; Strain −1; Legitimacy +1 procedural) → protections active → each arc boundary (first process): D-14.01 lapse (90%) → lapsed (no CB, no Standing) or persists → re-bind via Senator Outward. Terminal: lapsed / voided / active. Reversible: re-bind.

**PL-14.02 Treaty violation.** Entry: D-14.07 true. Steps: void; wronged party gains Treaty-violation CB (consumable, Punishment-mode eligible); Standing −2 / +1; no lapse roll thereafter. Terminal: voided. (CT-35 with PL-14.03.)

**PL-14.03 Crown-break cascade.** Entry: Crown Legionary vs partner territory or Phase 1 dissolution. Steps: `1. Dissolution declared at Phase 1 2. Crown Stability −2, Crown L −1 … end of Phase 4 3. CB granted … Accounting Step 1 4. CB usable from the following season only`. Terminal: treaty ended. Deliberately within Cascade Depth Cap.

**PL-14.04 Casus Belli ledger.** Entry: any source predicate (D-14.10; seizure attempt; pledge breach; settlement transfer; Cathedral attack; crossing). Steps: entry created on the (holder, target) pair with expiry per D-14.11 → consumed by Parliamentary Transfer (success or fail; Partial regenerates) → expires (3 seasons / 1 arc / refresh). Terminal: consumed or expired. (GP-14.02: no other consumer defined.)

**PL-14.05 Open Pledge.** Entry: Phase 1 declaration (≤ 1 active per faction). Steps: record publicly → at resolution apply D-14.12 (card-declaration test; forced-breach exemption H-31) → honour (+1 Standing; Strain −1 at 4d) / breach (Stability −1; CB 3 seasons to witnesses). Terminal: end of season.

**PL-14.06 Closed Pledge.** Entry: two factions write face-down card. Steps: if broken → injured party may reveal at any Accounting → witnesses = factions present → Stability −1, CB 3 seasons to all present, breaching faction `PI −1`. Terminal: revealed.

**PL-14.07 Inter-faction edge crossing.** Entry: army crosses into another faction's territory. Steps: permitted (treaty/alliance/vassalage) → no cost; else CB +1 to territorial faction, IP −2 to trespasser, hostility flag (engagement forced if no retreat by next Accounting). Terminal: flag cleared on retreat or engagement. ("vassalage" undefined — GP-14.11.)

**PL-14.08 Diplomatic Alignment.** Entry: Parliamentary Session start. Steps: Church + Hafenmark declare (public) → one motion counted same-side. Terminal: motion resolved.

**PL-14.09 Treaty positioning → ratification (resolver).** Entry: initiator declares. Steps: positioning M = own Influence − target Influence → Success+ sets opening terms → ratification M = Mandate − 2 (+Guarantor; +Grand Debate degree; +CI for Church) → degree. Terminal: ratified or not. (Terms and their effect on the treaty record undefined — GP-14.01.)

**PL-14.10 Dynastic Proclamation.** Entry: Hafenmark Diplomat card; `M ≥ 4, M > target controller M`. Steps: D-14.15 Ob → roll → effects (absent). Terminal: unknown (GP-14.06).

### SET-14 Throughlines applied
TL-01 — `GD-1 binding (treaty expiration produces faction-relationship delta, not victory trigger)` asserted; "treaties counting" toward Peninsular Sovereignty (`ci_political › §2.2`) is the only place treaties touch victory and its meaning is unstated (GP-07.07). TL-08 — treaty positioning/ratification migrated; Senator Outward re-binding and Formal Crown Treaty Ob remain dice-form (CT-03, CT-76). TL-16 (CB consumable, pair-scoped, time-limited) — asserted by `parliamentary_transfer`; violated by numeric `+1` CB (`march_layer`), "permanent until used" (`strategic_layer › I-03`), and Pledge CB "to every witnessing faction" (holder set undefined). TL-17 — arc boundary "before any other arc-boundary processing" is the only inter-process ordering statement in the corpus. TL-12 — H-31. TL-14 — "Standing" (CT-57), "IP −2 to the trespassing faction" (CT-26).

## TL. Throughline register

A throughline is a rule about mechanics that recurs across documents. Each entry lists the commitment, the documents that assert or rely on it, and every violation found. Violations cite the contradiction or gap index where one exists.

**TL-01 — GD-1: the sole victory condition is Peninsular Sovereignty; no mechanic is a victory trigger.**
- Asserted / relied on: `core (1).md` header comment (`SOLE victory condition for all factions is Peninsular Sovereignty (control 11+ of 15 territories)`); `ci_political_v30 › §2.2, §7.1` (`Victory = Peninsular Sovereignty for all factions. CI is a tool, not a win condition`); `treaty_expiration › GD constraints`; `parliamentary_transfer › GD constraints`; `insurgency_pipeline › §7, §7.1`; `faction_behavior › §3.1` (`faction-specific tracks in victory_v30 §3 are approaches, not win triggers`); `campaign_architecture › §1.3` (`CI=100 does not mean Church wins`); `campaign_architecture › §6.3` (Wardens: `still Peninsular Sovereignty`).
- Violations: `phases › Phase 5` step 12 (per-faction conditions, co-victory pairings, shared loss); `phases › Year-End` 6 and `strategic_layer › PP-035, Cascade Test 2` (Hollow Victory); `strategic_layer › I-06 / P-17` (Restoration `Mending Stability ≥ 50` victory); `geography › Varfell Path B — Southernmost Dominion` and `› Varfell Intelligence Path` (`Intelligence Hegemony victory path requires TCV ≥ 10 + VTM ≥ 3 …`); `institutions › Löwenritter — Reconstitution` (`Regency primary victory (PI ≥ 4 required)`); `ci_political › §6.2` (`HF victory needs PI ≥ 5`); `stats_1_7_scale › Starting Stats` (RM `Victory via Cultural Uprising of T9 Himmelenger`); `campaign_architecture › §1.3` (`all 15 territories at Accord ≥ 2` — a different measure from 11/15); `settlement_layer › §7.1` (`victory gate (≤ 6)` on Political Stability); `strategic_layer › Part Eight` (`Church Holy State Victory`). → CT-28, CT-18, CT-71, GP-07.07.

**TL-02 — Ob minimum is 1; no modifier reduces Ob below 1.**
- Asserted: `core › Dice System`; `strategic_layer › CORRECTION 2`; every Ob table (`All Obs: floor 1`); `stats › PP-402` (`min 1`); `southernmost` implicitly.
- Violations: none found. Related anomaly: `southernmost › Forgetting Check` uses `TN 8` against the corpus-wide `TN 7` (CT-48).

**TL-03 — Three-scale resolution: Settlement → Province → Peninsula each season; control flows upward, pressure downward.**
- Asserted: `phases › Three-Scale Resolution Model`; `conflict_architecture › The Three Scales`; `settlement_layer › §1.3` (Accord from Order), `§8.1`; `valoria_political_hierarchy › §2.4` (aggregation).
- Violations / unreconciled: `phases › Phase 4 Resolution Priority Order` orders by card tier (Intel → Military → Domain → Social → Thread → Unique → Project), not by scale; four phase schemes never mapped (GP-02.02, GP-02.03); `governance_play_redesign` [PROPOSAL] resolves world→player at season open.

**TL-04 — Accord is per territory, 0–3; TCV/PV counts only at Accord ≥ 2; military conquest sets Accord 1, non-military acquisition 2+.**
- Asserted: `tracks › Accord`; `core › Accord`; `geography › Starting Accord`; `phases › 4c`; `parliamentary_transfer › §1.2` (`Accord = 1`); `campaign_architecture › §1.3`; `settlement_layer › §8.1`.
- Violations: `insurgency_pipeline › §5.1` (`Accord ≥ 4 averaged` — impossible, CT-43); `parliamentary_transfer › §2` Appeasement (`+2 instead of +1`, CT-58); `settlement_layer › §4.3` (`Accord growth +0.5/season`, GP-06.10); `settlement_layer › §4.7` (`Settlement Accord −0.5`, CT-16); `campaign_architecture › §1.3` (seizure, a political act, yields Accord 1 "military-equivalent", CT-65); derived-Accord model (CT-16) leaves "Accord +1" effects without a target.

**TL-05 — Mandate is a derived aggregate of per-settlement Legitimacy and Popular Support (0–7 each); it is not authored or directly mutated.**
- Asserted: `settlement_layer › §1.8` (Jordan ruling 2026-05-30); `faction_behavior › §2, §3.4, §3.5, §4`; `stats_1_7_scale › Stats (1–7 scale)` header; `tracks.md` and `core` PP-686 notes.
- Violations: `tracks`/`core` note formula `round(0.5 × L + 0.5 × PS)` (CT-07); `core › Faction Starting Stats` authored L/PS columns and `Legitimacy_init = …` (seed only — acceptable) but `ministry.md` (`L 3 … PS not tracked`), `insurgency › §3.2, §4.2` (authored Mandate/Legitimacy), `settlement_layer › §6.2 ED-790` (`L 2; PS 3` faction-level), `faction_succession_split › §2.4` (`Mandate | Winner: 60%`), `baralta_crown_claim` (Mandate as pool and threshold), every `Mandate −1` / `L −1` effect on a faction (`ci_political › §4.1` table; Strain checks; Consecration `−2`; Seizure Failure; Censure/Outlawry), `strategic_layer › P-15/P-20` (`Mandate drops to 0`; `cannot exceed 7`), `worldbuilding › Prudence Crisis` (`Church Mandate −1 in each territory`). → CT-07, CT-08.

**TL-06 — Cascade Depth Cap: at most 3 immediate mechanical effects per card-play resolution step; excess queues to next Accounting; state-based environmental modifiers exempt.**

- Violations / gaps: whether clock changes count is left open (`strategic_layer › Cascade Test 2` "Confirm … Add this to PATCH P-29" — P-29 as printed is unrelated) (GP-02.09); queue ordering unstated (GP-02.12); `strategic_layer › Cascade Test 4` acknowledges `5+ cascading effects` from one seizure roll.

- Asserted: `stats › Stats, PP-242, ED-039`; `ci_political › §4.1, §2.4`; `ci_seizure`; `faction_politics › §2.2b`; `player_agency › §5.4`; `settlement_layer › §4.4, §1.8`; `southernmost › Combined TT Reduction Cap`; `strategic_layer › P-29`; `stats › PP-281`.
- Violations: `campaign_architecture › §1.1` Cathedral `+2/season` PT (CT-53); `baralta › §2–3` `CI +10`, `CI −5` with no cap note (CT-75); `stats › SAD` `CI −3` (within cap but combined with structural −1 and seizure could exceed); `faction_behavior › §3.5` continuous λ deltas onto integer L/PS (GP-08.08); `stats › Military Stat Change on Unit Destruction` immediate `−1` vs BG queueing (`strategic_layer › PP-041`).

- Violations (dice-form faction checks that were not migrated): `parliament.md` (L suppression, coalition +2D, Excommunication cap); `ministry.md` (`L (3D) vs Ob 1`, Corrupt Ministry); `institutions.md` (Reconstitution `L vs Ob 3`, Nomination `L vs Ob 2`, Church Levies); `ci_seizure.md` (seizure pool, Battle Ob, Suppress); `fractional_province_ownership › §2.4, §2.6` (Consolidation, Fragmentation `5d10 vs Ob 3`); `parliamentary_transfer › §4` (`Pool-vs-Ob roll … Continuous Engine`); `baralta › §2` (`pool vs Ob 3 (TN 7)`); `faction_succession › §3` (example rolls); `tracks › Accord` (Popular Uprising `Military vs Ob 2`); `clocks › Vanguard` (`Military vs Ob 3`); `core › Standard Action Ob Reference` (all rows); `stats › Hafenmark — Sovereign Authority Doctrine` (pending); `treaty_expiration › §2` (Senator Outward dice degrees); `strategic_layer` throughout. → CT-03; GP-01.02 (no mapping for composite pools / bonus dice into margin).

**TL-14 — Vocabulary aliases are stable and one-to-one (RS ≡ MS; TC ≡ CI; CV ≡ PT; Turmoil ≡ Strain; TCV ≡ PV; territory ≡ province tier; Compromise → Appease; Coup Counter → Autonomy stages; Cultural Reformation → Reclamation).**

- Violations: `PI` = Parliament Integrity (`parliament`, `core`, `ministry`) and Public Instability (`stats › PP-255, ED-174, PP-281`) (CT-27); `IP` = Invasion Pressure (`core`, `clocks`, `geography`, `settlement_layer`) and Institutional Pressure (`stats`, `strategic_layer`, `baralta`, `worldbuilding`, `early_game`) (CT-26); `Church Influence` = CI clock and Church's Influence stat (`settlement_layer › §1.7` `Ob = Church Influence ÷ 2`) (CT-56); `Standing` = PC rank and faction-pair reputation tokens (CT-57); `L` / `Mandate` / `Legitimacy` used interchangeably for the same triggers (CT-55, CT-08); `Coup Counter` and `Autonomy` both live (CT-21); `Community Organizing` / `Community Weaving` (CT-25); `Piety Track` as a contest type (`faction_politics › §1.0a, §3.6.2`); `Piety Track (CT)` glossary line (`faction_politics` header) vs `PT`; `Southernmost` / `Askeheim` (ED-645 open); "Revolution" for RM (`stats › PP-237`); `Territory`/`Province` deprecation contested (CT-18).

**TL-15 — Ratchets: certain states are irreversible or non-decaying (Split; Insurgency stages 3–4 non-demoting; WR lock once returned to 0; Renown/Shadow Renown no decay; Cathedral irreplaceable; Dishonored flag; TD 5 permanent; Secession "permanent until reconquest"; repulsion freezes IP).**
- Asserted: `conflict_architecture › Graduated Löwenritter Autonomy`; `insurgency › §6.4`; `geography › Path B`; `player_agency › §5.4`; `faction_politics › §2.2b, §1.0`; `campaign_architecture › §1.1, §5.2`; `tracks › TD`; `fractional › §2.6`.
- Violations: `stats › Coup Trigger` (`Counter never decrements`) vs reversible stages (CT-21); `institutions › Parish / Cathedral` (`Cathedral degrades to Parish on control transfer`) vs `Irreplaceable` (CT-68); `faction_succession › §2.5` allows re-merge of a split faction (a designed exception, not a violation).

**TL-16 — Casus Belli is a discrete, faction-pair-scoped, consumable, time-limited token.**
- Asserted: `parliamentary_transfer › §3` (pair-scoped ledger; consumed on use; sources; 1-arc persistence); `parliament › PP-515` (`standard, 3 seasons`); `ci_seizure › PP-510`; `fractional › §5` (per settlement).
- Violations: `march_layer › §5.4` (`Casus Belli +1` — numeric); `strategic_layer › I-03` (`Permanent Until Used` unresolved); lifetimes disagree (CT-59); no consumer other than Parliamentary Transfer is specified (GP-14.02).

**TL-17 — Time: the season is the atomic tick; 4 seasons = 1 year = 1 arc; Winter (every 4th season) is Year-End; arc boundary = end of season 4, 8, 12, ….**
- Asserted: `phases › Phase 5` 13, `› Year-End`; `treaty_expiration › §1.1`; `faction_politics` ("year-arc"); `insurgency › §2`; `parliamentary_transfer › §1.1`.
- Violations / unmapped units: `settlement_layer › §1.4.3` (`per decade`); `stats › SAD` (`once per campaign arc`); `worldbuilding › Jarnstal Independence` (`once per game`); `march_layer › §2.4` (`season Q4 and Q1`); `settlement_layer › §7.1` (`+1 per 5 years`); `faction_politics › §8.1` (`Campaign Elapsed Seasons ≥ 24`); arc-boundary vs Year-End step ordering unstated (GP-02.08); campaign length unfixed (GP-12.08).

**TL-18 — Information is fogged: enemy faction stats are hidden (four qualitative bands) unless revealed by Intel; Intel itself is always hidden; map state outside vision persists as last-known.**
- Asserted: `strategic_layer › §9.2`; `stats › Stats` (Intel roles); `march_layer › §3.1`; `ci_seizure › PP-507` (seizure visibility by Intel).
- Violations / tensions: `player_agency › §5.1` (`Standing 2 — faction intelligence access (rival stats, territory conditions)`) grants stat visibility by rank, not Intel; `ci_seizure › PP-507` requires a per-territory Intel (`Intel ≥ 3 in the target territory`) that no document defines (GP-04.09); `parliament › PP-515` Open Pledges are "recorded openly" (fine) but Closed Pledges rely on physical face-down cards.

## H. Specification-hole index (human referee or non-evaluable decision)

## Method notes for stages B and C

1. Every `P-`, `D-`, `PL-` entry cites a `filename › heading`; where the corpus states a number it is reproduced with its original glyphs. Any value that appears in this document but not in the corpus is marked `[INFERRED]`.
2. Contradictions are not resolved. Where a later-dated document *claims* to supersede an earlier one, both are still listed, because in nine cases the superseded rule remains load-bearing in a third document (CT-09, CT-12, CT-21, CT-24, CT-25, CT-40, CT-64, CT-68, CT-71).
3. `[PROPOSAL]` entries (all from `governance_play_redesign_v1.md`) are segregated by tag so they can be excluded wholesale.
4. Referenced-but-absent documents that the corpus treats as binding — `peninsular_strain_v30`, `faction_layer_v30`, `mass_battle_v30`, `victory_v30`, `military_layer_v30`, `social_contest_v30`, `conviction_track_v30`, `calamity_radiation_v30`, `threadwork_v30`, `npc_behavior_v30`, `scale_transitions_v30`, `valoria_geography_v30.yaml`, `params/factions.md`, `PP-182`, `PP-404`, `PP-487`, `PP-430`, `SUC-01..03`, `LIN-01..04`, `OFC-01..04`, `MIN-01..06`, `GD-2`, `faction_layer §1.5 / §2 / §3 / §5` — are named in the relevant gap entries. Their absence is the single largest source of non-executability; the second largest is the resolver/dice bridge (GP-01.02).
